===== FILE: src/app/[handle]/opengraph-image.tsx =====

import { cache } from "react";
import { avatarDataUri, landingOg, OG_SIZE, OG_TYPE, profileOg } from "@/lib/og";
import { toProfile } from "@/lib/profile-mapper";
import { createPublicClient } from "@/lib/supabase/public";
import { DEMO_PROFILE } from "@/lib/themes/demo";
import { FALLBACK_THEME, resolveTheme } from "@/lib/themes/registry";

export const alt = "Page de liens Ndeletik";
export const size = OG_SIZE;
export const contentType = OG_TYPE;
export const revalidate = 300;

const load = cache(async (handle: string) => {
  const decoded = decodeURIComponent(handle);
  if (!decoded.startsWith("@")) return null;
  if (decoded === "@demo") return { profile: DEMO_PROFILE, plan: "business" as const };
  const { data } = await createPublicClient().rpc("get_public_profile", { p_slug: decoded.slice(1) });
  return data ? toProfile(data) : null;
});

export default async function Image({ params }: { params: Promise<{ handle: string }> }) {
  const { handle } = await params;
  const res = await load(handle);
  if (!res) return landingOg();

  const { profile, plan } = res;
  const { theme } = resolveTheme(profile.themeId, plan);
  return profileOg({
    name: profile.displayName,
    slug: profile.slug,
    headline: profile.headline,
    avatar: await avatarDataUri(profile.avatarUrl),
    tokens: (theme ?? FALLBACK_THEME).tokens,
  });
}

===== FILE: src/app/[handle]/page.tsx =====

import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { cache } from "react";
import { ProfileRenderer } from "@/components/profile/ProfileRenderer";
import { ViewBeacon } from "@/components/profile/ViewBeacon";
import { DEMO_PROFILE } from "@/lib/themes/demo";
import { toProfile } from "@/lib/profile-mapper";
import { createPublicClient } from "@/lib/supabase/public";

// Page publique : /@slug (les routes statiques /preview, /go, /api… ont priorité sur [handle]).
export const revalidate = 60;

type Params = { handle: string };

/** Le segment dynamique peut arriver encodé (« %40demo ») : on le normalise avant toute comparaison. */
const decodeHandle = (raw: string) => {
  try {
    return decodeURIComponent(raw);
  } catch {
    return raw;
  }
};
type Props = { params: Promise<Params> | Params };

const load = cache(async (handle: string) => {
  const decoded = decodeURIComponent(handle);
  if (!decoded.startsWith("@")) return null;
  const { data } = await createPublicClient().rpc("get_public_profile", { p_slug: decoded.slice(1) });
  return data ? toProfile(data) : null;
});

export async function generateMetadata({ params }: Props): Promise<Metadata> {
  const handle = decodeHandle((await params).handle);
  if (handle === "@demo") {
    const description = "Découvre les blocs Ndeletik : offres, contact, séparateurs, newsletter, sur un thème Business.";
    return {
      title: "Démo",
      description,
      openGraph: { title: "Démo — Ndeletik", description, type: "website", siteName: "Ndeletik", locale: "fr_FR" },
      twitter: { card: "summary_large_image", title: "Démo — Ndeletik", description },
    };
  }
  const res = await load(handle);
  if (!res) return { title: "Page introuvable" };
  const description = res.profile.bio ?? res.profile.headline;
  const title = res.profile.displayName;
  return {
    title,
    description,
    // Le bloc openGraph du layout est remplacé en entier : on le redéclare avec le nom du profil.
    openGraph: { title: `${title} — Ndeletik`, description, type: "profile", siteName: "Ndeletik", locale: "fr_FR" },
    twitter: { card: "summary_large_image", title: `${title} — Ndeletik`, description },
  };
}

export default async function PublicProfilePage({ params }: Props) {
  const handle = decodeHandle((await params).handle);

  // Vitrine statique des nouveaux designs : aucun profil « demo » en base (slug réservé).
  if (handle === "@demo") {
    return <ProfileRenderer profile={DEMO_PROFILE} plan="business" preview />;
  }

  const res = await load(handle);
  if (!res) notFound();
  return (
    <>
      <ViewBeacon slug={res.profile.slug} />
      <ProfileRenderer profile={res.profile} plan={res.plan} />
    </>
  );
}
===== FILE: src/app/[handle]/twitter-image.tsx =====

import { cache } from "react";
import { avatarDataUri, landingOg, OG_SIZE, OG_TYPE, profileOg } from "@/lib/og";
import { toProfile } from "@/lib/profile-mapper";
import { createPublicClient } from "@/lib/supabase/public";
import { DEMO_PROFILE } from "@/lib/themes/demo";
import { FALLBACK_THEME, resolveTheme } from "@/lib/themes/registry";

export const alt = "Page de liens Ndeletik";
export const size = OG_SIZE;
export const contentType = OG_TYPE;
export const revalidate = 300;

const load = cache(async (handle: string) => {
  const decoded = decodeURIComponent(handle);
  if (!decoded.startsWith("@")) return null;
  if (decoded === "@demo") return { profile: DEMO_PROFILE, plan: "business" as const };
  const { data } = await createPublicClient().rpc("get_public_profile", { p_slug: decoded.slice(1) });
  return data ? toProfile(data) : null;
});

export default async function Image({ params }: { params: Promise<{ handle: string }> }) {
  const { handle } = await params;
  const res = await load(handle);
  if (!res) return landingOg();

  const { profile, plan } = res;
  const { theme } = resolveTheme(profile.themeId, plan);
  return profileOg({
    name: profile.displayName,
    slug: profile.slug,
    headline: profile.headline,
    avatar: await avatarDataUri(profile.avatarUrl),
    tokens: (theme ?? FALLBACK_THEME).tokens,
  });
}

===== FILE: src/app/admin/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import { useMemo } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import dynamic from "next/dynamic";
import { ChevronRight, Eye, Globe2, Link2, MousePointerClick, Users } from "lucide-react";
import { Card, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/Card";
import { buttonClass } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/ui/PageHeader";
import { ProfileAvatar } from "@/components/dashboard/ProfileAvatar";
import { useAdmin } from "@/components/admin/AdminProvider";
import { PlanDot } from "@/components/admin/bits";
import { MapBoundary, PlanDonut, Sparkline } from "@/components/admin/charts";
import { computeStats } from "@/components/admin/stats";
import { PLAN_COLORS, PLAN_ORDER, fmt, fmtDate } from "@/lib/admin-types";

const WorldMap = dynamic(() => import("@/components/admin/WorldMap").then((m) => m.WorldMap), {
  ssr: false,
  loading: () => <div className="h-64 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-soft" />,
});

export default function AdminOverviewPage() {
  const { rows } = useAdmin();
  const s = useMemo(() => computeStats(rows), [rows]);
  const latest = useMemo(() => [...rows].sort((a, b) => +new Date(b.created_at) - +new Date(a.created_at)).slice(0, 6), [rows]);
  const maxCountry = Math.max(1, ...s.topCountries.map((c) => c.total));

  const kpis = [
    { icon: Users, label: "Créateurs inscrits", value: s.totals.users, sub: `+${s.buckets[7]} cette semaine`, spark: true },
    { icon: Link2, label: "Liens publiés", value: s.totals.links, sub: "tous créateurs confondus" },
    { icon: MousePointerClick, label: "Clics cumulés", value: s.totals.clicks, sub: "tous liens confondus" },
    { icon: Eye, label: "Vues cumulées", value: s.totals.views, sub: "pages publiques" },
  ];

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        title="Vue d'ensemble"
        description="L'activité de Ndeletik en un coup d'œil."
        actions={
          <Link href="/admin/users" className={buttonClass({ variant: "secondary" })}>
            <Users size={16} aria-hidden /> Voir les utilisateurs
          </Link>
        }
      />

      <div className="space-y-6">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 md:grid-cols-4">
          {kpis.map((k) => (
            <Card key={k.label} className="p-4">
              <span className="mb-2 flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-fractal-ocre/10 text-fractal-ocre">
                <k.icon size={16} aria-hidden />
              </span>
              <div className="font-display text-2xl font-extrabold">{fmt(k.value)}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-muted">{k.label}</div>
              {k.spark ? (
                <>
                  <Sparkline data={s.buckets} />
                  <div className="text-[11px] font-medium text-fractal-terra">{k.sub}</div>
                </>
              ) : (
                <div className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-muted/80">{k.sub}</div>
              )}
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[1.6fr_1fr]">
          <Card>
            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
              <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 font-display text-sm font-bold">
                <Globe2 size={16} className="text-fractal-ocre" aria-hidden /> Abonnés par pays
              </h2>
              <span className="text-xs text-muted">{s.markers.length} zone(s) active(s)</span>
            </div>
            <MapBoundary>
              <WorldMap markers={s.markers} />
            </MapBoundary>
            {s.unlocated > 0 && (
              <p className="mt-2 text-[11px] text-muted">{s.unlocated} profil(s) sans pays reconnu ne sont pas placés sur la carte.</p>
            )}
          </Card>

          <div className="space-y-4">
            <Card>
              <CardTitle>Répartition des abonnements</CardTitle>
              <PlanDonut counts={s.planCounts} />
            </Card>
            <Card>
              <CardTitle>Top pays</CardTitle>
              {s.topCountries.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="text-xs text-muted">Aucune donnée de pays pour le moment.</p>
              ) : (
                <ul className="space-y-3">
                  {s.topCountries.map((c) => {
                    const dominant = PLAN_ORDER.reduce((a, b) => ((c.plans[a] ?? 0) >= (c.plans[b] ?? 0) ? a : b), "free");
                    return (
                      <li key={c.country}>
                        <div className="mb-1 flex justify-between text-sm">
                          <span className="truncate pr-3">{c.country}</span>
                          <span className="text-muted">{c.total}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="h-2 rounded-full bg-soft">
                          <div
                            className="h-2 rounded-full transition-all duration-500"
                            style={{ width: `${(c.total / maxCountry) * 100}%`, background: PLAN_COLORS[dominant] }}
                          />
                        </div>
                      </li>
                    );
                  })}
                </ul>
              )}
            </Card>
          </div>
        </div>

        <Card padded={false}>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 pb-2 pt-5">
            <h2 className="font-display text-sm font-bold">Dernières inscriptions</h2>
            <Link href="/admin/users" className="text-xs font-semibold text-fractal-terra hover:underline">
              Tout voir
            </Link>
          </div>
          <ul className="divide-y divide-line">
            {latest.map((r) => (
              <li key={r.id}>
                <Link href={`/admin/users/${r.id}`} className="flex items-center gap-3 px-5 py-3 transition-colors hover:bg-soft/60">
                  <ProfileAvatar profile={r} size={36} />
                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <p className="truncate text-sm font-semibold">@{r.slug}</p>
                    <p className="truncate text-xs text-muted">
                      {r.display_name} · {fmtDate(r.created_at)}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <span className="hidden sm:block">
                    <PlanDot plan={r.plan} />
                  </span>
                  <ChevronRight size={16} className="text-muted" aria-hidden />
                </Link>
              </li>
            ))}
            {latest.length === 0 && <li className="px-5 py-8 text-center text-sm text-muted">Aucune inscription pour le moment.</li>}
          </ul>
        </Card>

        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-line bg-soft/50 p-6 text-center">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold">Revenus Mobile Money</p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted">
            Le tableau des commissions (plans Pro/Business + transactions) apparaîtra ici dès l'intégration Sebpay.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/app/admin/users/[id]/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { ArrowLeft, Copy, Crown, ExternalLink, Eye, Link2, MousePointerClick, Trash2, Users } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, buttonClass } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { Card, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/Card";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/ui/EmptyState";
import { Field, inputClass } from "@/components/ui/Field";
import { Modal } from "@/components/ui/Modal";
import { ProfileAvatar } from "@/components/dashboard/ProfileAvatar";
import { useToast } from "@/components/dashboard/Toast";
import { useAdmin } from "@/components/admin/AdminProvider";
import { PlanDot, RoleBadge } from "@/components/admin/bits";
import { fmt, fmtDate } from "@/lib/admin-types";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabaseClient";

type LinkRow = { id: string; title: string; url: string; clicks: number; enabled: boolean };

export default function AdminUserPage() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const router = useRouter();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const { rows, meId, toggleRole, deleteUser } = useAdmin();
  const row = rows.find((r) => r.id === id);

  const [links, setLinks] = useState<LinkRow[] | null>(null);
  const [roleModal, setRoleModal] = useState(false);
  const [delModal, setDelModal] = useState(false);
  const [typed, setTyped] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!id) return;
    let alive = true;
    setLinks(null);
    supabase
      .from("links")
      .select("id, title, url, clicks, enabled")
      .eq("profile_id", id)
      .order("position", { ascending: true })
      .then(({ data }) => {
        if (alive) setLinks((data ?? []) as LinkRow[]);
      });
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [id]);

  if (!row) {
    return (
      <EmptyState
        icon={<Users size={22} aria-hidden />}
        title="Utilisateur introuvable"
        description="Ce profil n'existe plus ou a été supprimé."
        action={
          <Link href="/admin/users" className={buttonClass({ variant: "secondary" })}>
            <ArrowLeft size={16} aria-hidden /> Retour aux utilisateurs
          </Link>
        }
      />
    );
  }

  const isMe = row.user_id === meId;
  const isAdmin = row.role === "admin";
  const siblings = rows.filter((r) => r.user_id === row.user_id && r.id !== row.id);
  const lastProfile = siblings.length === 0;

  const copyId = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(row.user_id);
      toast("Identifiant copié");
    } catch {
      toast("Copie impossible", false);
    }
  };

  const confirmRole = async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    const ok = await toggleRole(row);
    setBusy(false);
    if (ok) setRoleModal(false);
  };

  const confirmDelete = async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    const ok = await deleteUser(row);
    setBusy(false);
    if (ok) router.replace("/admin/users");
  };

  return (
    <>
      <Link href="/admin/users" className="mb-4 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-muted hover:text-fractal-terra">
        <ArrowLeft size={15} aria-hidden /> Utilisateurs
      </Link>

      <Card className="mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4">
        <ProfileAvatar profile={row} size={64} />
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <h1 className="truncate font-display text-2xl font-extrabold tracking-tight">@{row.slug}</h1>
          <p className="truncate text-sm text-muted">{row.display_name}</p>
          <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
            <PlanDot plan={row.plan} />
            <RoleBadge role={row.role} />
            {isMe && <span className="text-xs font-medium text-fractal-terra">C'est toi</span>}
          </div>
        </div>
        <a href={`/@${row.slug}`} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className={buttonClass({ variant: "secondary" })}>
          <ExternalLink size={16} aria-hidden /> Voir sa page
        </a>
      </Card>

      <div className="mb-6 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
        <Stat icon={<Link2 size={18} aria-hidden />} label="Liens" value={fmt(row.links_count)} />
        <Stat icon={<MousePointerClick size={18} aria-hidden />} label="Clics" value={fmt(row.clicks_total)} />
        <Stat icon={<Eye size={18} aria-hidden />} label="Vues de la page" value={fmt(row.views)} />
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_340px]">
        <div className="min-w-0 space-y-6">
          <Card padded={false}>
            <div className="px-5 pb-2 pt-5">
              <CardTitle hint="Les liens publiés et masqués de ce profil.">Liens du profil</CardTitle>
            </div>
            {links === null ? (
              <div className="space-y-2 px-5 pb-5">
                <div className="h-10 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-line/50" />
                <div className="h-10 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-line/50" />
              </div>
            ) : links.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="px-5 pb-6 text-sm text-muted">Aucun lien pour le moment.</p>
            ) : (
              <ul className="divide-y divide-line">
                {links.map((l) => (
                  <li key={l.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 px-5 py-3">
                    <div className={`min-w-0 flex-1 ${l.enabled ? "" : "opacity-55"}`}>
                      <p className="truncate text-sm font-semibold">{l.title}</p>
                      <p className="truncate text-xs text-muted">
                        {l.enabled ? "" : "Masqué · "}
                        {l.url.replace(/^https?:\/\//, "")}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <span className="shrink-0 rounded-full bg-soft px-2.5 py-1 text-xs text-muted">
                      {fmt(l.clicks)} clic{l.clicks > 1 ? "s" : ""}
                    </span>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            )}
          </Card>

          {siblings.length > 0 && (
            <Card padded={false}>
              <div className="px-5 pb-2 pt-5">
                <CardTitle hint="Autres profils rattachés au même compte.">Autres profils du compte</CardTitle>
              </div>
              <ul className="divide-y divide-line">
                {siblings.map((s) => (
                  <li key={s.id}>
                    <Link href={`/admin/users/${s.id}`} className="flex items-center gap-3 px-5 py-3 hover:bg-soft/60">
                      <ProfileAvatar profile={s} size={32} />
                      <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                        <span className="block truncate text-sm font-semibold">@{s.slug}</span>
                        <span className="block truncate text-xs text-muted">{s.display_name}</span>
                      </span>
                    </Link>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </Card>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-6">
          <Card>
            <CardTitle>Détails</CardTitle>
            <dl className="space-y-3 text-sm">
              <Detail label="Pays" value={row.country ?? "—"} />
              <Detail label="Thème" value={row.theme_id} />
              <Detail label="Inscrit le" value={fmtDate(row.created_at)} />
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <dt className="text-muted">Identifiant</dt>
                <dd className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5">
                  <code className="truncate text-xs">{row.user_id.slice(0, 8)}…</code>
                  <button type="button" onClick={copyId} aria-label="Copier l'identifiant du compte" className="rounded-full p-1.5 text-muted hover:bg-soft hover:text-fractal-terra">
                    <Copy size={13} aria-hidden />
                  </button>
                </dd>
              </div>
            </dl>
          </Card>

          <Card>
            <CardTitle hint={isAdmin ? "Peut accéder à cette administration." : "Accès standard à son espace."}>Rôle</CardTitle>
            <Button variant="secondary" full disabled={isMe} onClick={() => setRoleModal(true)}>
              <Crown size={16} aria-hidden /> {isAdmin ? "Retirer le rôle administrateur" : "Promouvoir administrateur"}
            </Button>
            {isMe && <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-muted">Tu ne peux pas modifier ton propre rôle.</p>}
          </Card>

          <Card className="border-red-200">
            <CardTitle hint="Action irréversible.">Zone sensible</CardTitle>
            <Button
              variant="danger"
              full
              disabled={isMe}
              className="border border-red-200"
              onClick={() => {
                setTyped("");
                setDelModal(true);
              }}
            >
              <Trash2 size={16} aria-hidden /> Supprimer ce profil
            </Button>
            {isMe && <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-muted">Tu ne peux pas supprimer ton propre compte ici.</p>}
          </Card>
        </div>
      </div>

      <Modal
        open={roleModal}
        onClose={() => setRoleModal(false)}
        title={isAdmin ? "Retirer le rôle administrateur ?" : "Promouvoir administrateur ?"}
        description={
          isAdmin
            ? `@${row.slug} n'aura plus accès à l'administration.`
            : `@${row.slug} pourra voir tous les utilisateurs, modifier les rôles et supprimer des comptes.`
        }
        size="sm"
      >
        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setRoleModal(false)}>
            Annuler
          </Button>
          <Button onClick={confirmRole} disabled={busy} data-autofocus>
            {busy ? "En cours…" : "Confirmer"}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </Modal>

      <Modal
        open={delModal}
        onClose={() => setDelModal(false)}
        title="Supprimer ce profil ?"
        description={
          lastProfile
            ? `@${row.slug}, ses liens et son compte seront supprimés définitivement.`
            : `@${row.slug} et ses liens seront supprimés. Le compte reste actif : il a ${siblings.length} autre${siblings.length > 1 ? "s" : ""} profil${siblings.length > 1 ? "s" : ""}.`
        }
      >
        <div className="grid gap-4">
          <Field label={`Pour confirmer, tape « ${row.slug} »`}>
            <input value={typed} onChange={(e) => setTyped(e.target.value)} className={inputClass} autoCapitalize="none" autoCorrect="off" data-autofocus />
          </Field>
          <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setDelModal(false)}>
              Annuler
            </Button>
            <Button className="!bg-red-600 hover:!bg-red-700" disabled={typed.trim() !== row.slug || busy} onClick={confirmDelete}>
              <Trash2 size={16} aria-hidden /> {busy ? "Suppression…" : "Supprimer définitivement"}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Modal>
    </>
  );
}

function Stat({ icon, label, value }: { icon: React.ReactNode; label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <Card className="flex items-center gap-4">
      <span className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-fractal-ocre/10 text-fractal-ocre">{icon}</span>
      <div>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted">{label}</p>
        <p className="font-display text-2xl font-extrabold">{value}</p>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

function Detail({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
      <dt className="text-muted">{label}</dt>
      <dd className="truncate font-medium">{value}</dd>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/app/admin/users/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { ChevronRight, Download, FilterX, Search, Users } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/Card";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/ui/EmptyState";
import { inputClass } from "@/components/ui/Field";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/ui/PageHeader";
import { ProfileAvatar } from "@/components/dashboard/ProfileAvatar";
import { useAdmin } from "@/components/admin/AdminProvider";
import { PlanDot, RoleBadge } from "@/components/admin/bits";
import { PLAN_LABELS, PLAN_ORDER, SORTS, fmt, fmtDate, type SortKey } from "@/lib/admin-types";

const PAGE = 50;

export default function AdminUsersPage() {
  const { rows, exportCsv } = useAdmin();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [country, setCountry] = useState("all");
  const [plan, setPlan] = useState("all");
  const [role, setRole] = useState("all");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<SortKey>("recent");
  const [limit, setLimit] = useState(PAGE);

  const countryOptions = useMemo(() => {
    const m = new Map<string, number>();
    rows.forEach((r) => {
      const c = r.country?.trim();
      if (c) m.set(c, (m.get(c) ?? 0) + 1);
    });
    return [...m.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[0].localeCompare(b[0], "fr"));
  }, [rows]);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const nq = q.trim().toLowerCase();
    const list = rows.filter((r) => {
      if (nq && !(r.slug.toLowerCase().includes(nq) || r.display_name.toLowerCase().includes(nq))) return false;
      if (country !== "all" && (r.country?.trim() ?? "—") !== country) return false;
      if (plan !== "all" && r.plan !== plan) return false;
      if (role !== "all" && r.role !== role) return false;
      return true;
    });
    const by: Record<SortKey, (a: (typeof rows)[number], b: (typeof rows)[number]) => number> = {
      recent: (a, b) => +new Date(b.created_at) - +new Date(a.created_at),
      old: (a, b) => +new Date(a.created_at) - +new Date(b.created_at),
      clicks: (a, b) => b.clicks_total - a.clicks_total,
      views: (a, b) => b.views - a.views,
      links: (a, b) => b.links_count - a.links_count,
      az: (a, b) => a.slug.localeCompare(b.slug, "fr"),
    };
    return [...list].sort(by[sort]);
  }, [rows, q, country, plan, role, sort]);

  const hasFilters = q !== "" || country !== "all" || plan !== "all" || role !== "all";
  const reset = () => {
    setQ("");
    setCountry("all");
    setPlan("all");
    setRole("all");
    setLimit(PAGE);
  };
  const shown = filtered.slice(0, limit);
  const accounts = new Set(rows.map((r) => r.user_id)).size;

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        title="Utilisateurs"
        description={`${fmt(rows.length)} profil${rows.length > 1 ? "s" : ""} · ${fmt(accounts)} compte${accounts > 1 ? "s" : ""}`}
        actions={
          <Button onClick={() => exportCsv(filtered)} disabled={filtered.length === 0}>
            <Download size={16} aria-hidden /> Export CSV
          </Button>
        }
      />

      <Card className="mb-4">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 lg:grid-cols-6">
          <label className="relative col-span-2">
            <span className="sr-only">Rechercher</span>
            <Search size={15} aria-hidden className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted" />
            <input
              value={q}
              onChange={(e) => {
                setQ(e.target.value);
                setLimit(PAGE);
              }}
              placeholder="Pseudo ou nom…"
              className={`${inputClass} pl-9`}
            />
          </label>
          <select value={country} onChange={(e) => setCountry(e.target.value)} className={inputClass} aria-label="Filtrer par pays">
            <option value="all">Tous les pays</option>
            {countryOptions.map(([c, n]) => (
              <option key={c} value={c}>
                {c} ({n})
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <select value={plan} onChange={(e) => setPlan(e.target.value)} className={inputClass} aria-label="Filtrer par plan">
            <option value="all">Tous les plans</option>
            {PLAN_ORDER.map((p) => (
              <option key={p} value={p}>
                {PLAN_LABELS[p]}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <select value={role} onChange={(e) => setRole(e.target.value)} className={inputClass} aria-label="Filtrer par rôle">
            <option value="all">Tous les rôles</option>
            <option value="admin">Admins</option>
            <option value="user">Utilisateurs</option>
          </select>
          <select value={sort} onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value as SortKey)} className={inputClass} aria-label="Trier">
            {SORTS.map((s) => (
              <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>
                {s.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-muted">
          <span>
            {fmt(filtered.length)} affiché{filtered.length > 1 ? "s" : ""} sur {fmt(rows.length)}
          </span>
          {hasFilters && (
            <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={reset}>
              <FilterX size={13} aria-hidden /> Réinitialiser
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      </Card>

      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<Users size={22} aria-hidden />}
          title="Aucun utilisateur ne correspond"
          description="Modifie ta recherche ou tes filtres."
          action={hasFilters ? <Button variant="secondary" onClick={reset}>Réinitialiser les filtres</Button> : undefined}
        />
      ) : (
        <>
          {/* Mobile : cartes */}
          <ul className="space-y-2.5 md:hidden">
            {shown.map((r) => (
              <li key={r.id}>
                <Link
                  href={`/admin/users/${r.id}`}
                  className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-3.5 shadow-soft transition-colors hover:border-fractal-ocre/60"
                >
                  <ProfileAvatar profile={r} size={44} />
                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <p className="truncate text-sm font-semibold">@{r.slug}</p>
                    <p className="truncate text-xs text-muted">
                      {r.display_name}
                      {r.country ? ` · ${r.country}` : ""}
                    </p>
                    <div className="mt-1.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1">
                      <PlanDot plan={r.plan} />
                      {r.role === "admin" && <RoleBadge role={r.role} />}
                      <span className="text-[11px] text-muted">
                        {r.links_count} lien{r.links_count > 1 ? "s" : ""} · {fmt(r.clicks_total)} clic{r.clicks_total > 1 ? "s" : ""}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <ChevronRight size={18} className="shrink-0 text-muted" aria-hidden />
                </Link>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>

          {/* Ordinateur : tableau */}
          <Card padded={false} className="hidden overflow-hidden md:block">
            <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-line text-xs text-muted">
                  <th className="px-5 py-3 font-medium">Utilisateur</th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-3 font-medium">Pays</th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-3 text-center font-medium">Liens</th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-3 text-center font-medium">Clics</th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-3 font-medium">Plan</th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-3 font-medium">Rôle</th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-3 font-medium">Inscrit le</th>
                  <th className="w-10 px-3 py-3" aria-label="Ouvrir" />
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {shown.map((r) => (
                  <tr
                    key={r.id}
                    onClick={() => router.push(`/admin/users/${r.id}`)}
                    className="cursor-pointer border-b border-line/60 transition-colors last:border-0 hover:bg-soft/60"
                  >
                    <td className="px-5 py-3">
                      <Link href={`/admin/users/${r.id}`} onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()} className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <ProfileAvatar profile={r} size={34} />
                        <span className="min-w-0">
                          <span className="block truncate font-medium">@{r.slug}</span>
                          <span className="block truncate text-xs text-muted">{r.display_name}</span>
                        </span>
                      </Link>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-3 text-muted">{r.country ?? "—"}</td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-3 text-center">{r.links_count}</td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-3 text-center">{fmt(r.clicks_total)}</td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-3">
                      <PlanDot plan={r.plan} />
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-3">
                      <RoleBadge role={r.role} />
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-3 text-xs text-muted">{fmtDate(r.created_at)}</td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-3 text-muted">
                      <ChevronRight size={16} aria-hidden />
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </Card>

          {filtered.length > shown.length && (
            <div className="mt-4 flex justify-center">
              <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setLimit((l) => l + PAGE)}>
                Afficher {Math.min(PAGE, filtered.length - shown.length)} de plus
              </Button>
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/app/api/click/route.ts =====

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabaseClient";

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  try {
    const { linkId } = await request.json();

    if (!linkId || typeof linkId !== "string") {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "linkId invalide" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const { error } = await supabase.rpc("increment_clicks", {
      link_id: linkId,
    });

    if (error) throw error;

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (err: any) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: err.message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabaseClient';

export async function GET() {
  try {
    // Requête ultra-légère pour "ping" la base de données
    const { error } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('id').limit(1);
    
    if (error) throw error;

    return NextResponse.json({ 
      success: true, 
      message: 'Supabase keep-alive ping successful.',
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString() 
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    return NextResponse.json({ 
      success: false, 
      error: error.message 
    }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
===== FILE: src/app/api/newsletter/route.ts =====

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { createAdminClient } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";

const EMAIL = /^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/;
const SLUG = /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_.-]{2,29}$/;

// Formulaire du bloc newsletter (POST form-data : slug, email).
// Avant la prod : ajouter une limitation de débit / anti-spam (ex. Turnstile).
export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const form = await req.formData();
  const slug = String(form.get("slug") ?? "").toLowerCase();
  const email = String(form.get("email") ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!SLUG.test(slug) || !EMAIL.test(email) || email.length > 254) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid" }, { status: 400 });
  }
  const admin = createAdminClient();
  const { data: profile } = await admin.from("profiles").select("id").eq("slug", slug).maybeSingle();
  if (!profile) return NextResponse.json({ error: "not_found" }, { status: 404 });
  const { error } = await admin.from("subscribers").insert({ profile_id: profile.id, email });
  if (error && error.code !== "23505") return NextResponse.json({ error: "failed" }, { status: 500 });
  return NextResponse.redirect(new URL(`/@${slug}?subscribed=1`, req.url), 303);
}
===== FILE: src/app/api/profile/theme/route.ts =====

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { canUse, canUseTheme, requiredPlanForTheme, sanitizeCustomTokens } from "@/lib/entitlements";
import type { Plan } from "@/lib/plans";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";

// POST { themeId: string, customTokens?: { accent: "#FF6A1A", ... } }
// 1re barrière (messages clairs) ici ; barrière définitive = triggers SQL.
const DB_ERRORS: Record<string, number> = {
  theme_locked: 403,
  theme_unknown: 400,
  custom_colors_locked: 403,
  custom_token_invalid: 400,
  badge_locked: 403,
};

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) return NextResponse.json({ error: "unauthenticated" }, { status: 401 });

  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  const themeId = typeof body?.themeId === "string" ? body.themeId : "";
  if (!themeId) return NextResponse.json({ error: "themeId_required" }, { status: 400 });

  const { data: planData } = await supabase.rpc("my_plan");
  const plan = (["free", "pro", "business"].includes(planData) ? planData : "free") as Plan;

  if (!canUseTheme(plan, themeId)) {
    const required = requiredPlanForTheme(themeId);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: required ? "theme_locked" : "theme_unknown", requiredPlan: required },
      { status: required ? 403 : 400 }
    );
  }

  const update: Record<string, unknown> = { theme_id: themeId };
  if (body.customTokens !== undefined) {
    const tokens = sanitizeCustomTokens(body.customTokens);
    if (Object.keys(tokens).length && !canUse(plan, "customColors")) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "custom_colors_locked", requiredPlan: "pro" }, { status: 403 });
    }
    update.custom_tokens = tokens;
  }

  const { error } = await supabase.from("profiles").update(update).eq("user_id", user.id);
  if (error) {
    const hit = Object.keys(DB_ERRORS).find((k) => error.message.includes(k));
    if (hit) return NextResponse.json({ error: hit }, { status: DB_ERRORS[hit] });
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "update_failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, plan, themeId });
}
===== FILE: src/app/api/slug-check/route.ts =====

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { isReserved } from "@/lib/reserved";
import { createPublicClient } from "@/lib/supabase/public";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const SLUG = /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_.-]{2,29}$/;

/** GET /api/slug-check?slug=xxx → { available, reason? } (respecte la RLS via la RPC publique). */
export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const slug = (new URL(req.url).searchParams.get("slug") ?? "").toLowerCase();
  if (!SLUG.test(slug)) return NextResponse.json({ available: false, reason: "invalid" });
  if (isReserved(slug)) return NextResponse.json({ available: false, reason: "reserved" });
  const { data } = await createPublicClient().rpc("get_public_profile", { p_slug: slug });
  return NextResponse.json({ available: !data, reason: data ? "taken" : undefined });
}
===== FILE: src/app/api/view/route.ts =====

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { createPublicClient } from "@/lib/supabase/public";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  const slug = typeof body?.slug === "string" ? body.slug.toLowerCase().slice(0, 40) : "";
  if (!slug) return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid" }, { status: 400 });
  const { error } = await createPublicClient().rpc("increment_views", { p_slug: slug });
  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: "failed" }, { status: 500 });
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true });
}

===== FILE: src/app/auth/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import { Suspense, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { ArrowLeft, Eye, EyeOff, KeyRound, Loader2, Mail, Smartphone } from "lucide-react";
import { FaGoogle } from "react-icons/fa6";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabaseClient";
import { Logo } from "@/components/landing/ui";
import { isReserved } from "@/lib/reserved";

type Method = "password" | "magic" | "sms";

const FRENCH_ERRORS: [RegExp, string][] = [
  [/invalid login credentials/i, "Email ou mot de passe incorrect."],
  [/email not confirmed/i, "Confirme d'abord ton adresse email (voir ta boîte mail)."],
  [/user already registered/i, "Un compte existe déjà avec cet email."],
  [/phone number .* invalid|invalid phone/i, "Numéro invalide. Format international : +242…"],
  [/provider is not enabled|sms provider/i, "Connexion par SMS non activée pour le moment."],
  [/token has expired/i, "Ce code a expiré. Demande un nouveau."],
  [/invalid otp/i, "Code invalide. Vérifie les 6 chiffres."],
  [/rate limit/i, "Trop de tentatives. Attends une minute."],
  [/password should be/i, "Mot de passe : 6 caractères minimum."],
];
const fr = (msg: string) => FRENCH_ERRORS.find(([re]) => re.test(msg))?.[1] ?? msg;
const digits = (v: string) => v.replace(/[^\d+]/g, "");

export default function AuthPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={null}>
      <AuthInner />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

function AuthInner() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const params = useSearchParams();
  const [method, setMethod] = useState<Method>("password");
  const [isLogin, setIsLogin] = useState(true);
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [showPwd, setShowPwd] = useState(false);
  const [username, setUsername] = useState(params.get("slug") ?? "");
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState("");
  const [otpSent, setOtpSent] = useState(false);
  const [otpCode, setOtpCode] = useState("");
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState("");
  const [resetMode, setResetMode] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [info, setInfo] = useState("");
  const plan = params.get("plan");

  /* Profil manquant (connexion Google / OAuth) : création automatique. */
  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      if (!session) return;
      const { data: rows } = await supabase.from("profiles").select("id").eq("user_id", session.user.id);
      if ((rows ?? []).length === 0) {
        const base =
          (session.user.email ?? "user").split("@")[0].toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "").slice(0, 20) || "user";
        let slug = base;
        let i = 0;
        for (;;) {
          const taken = isReserved(slug);
          const { data: existing } = await supabase.from("profiles").select("id").eq("slug", slug).maybeSingle();
          if (!taken && !existing) break;
          slug = `${base}${++i}`;
        }
        await supabase.from("profiles").insert({
          user_id: session.user.id,
          slug,
          display_name: session.user.user_metadata?.full_name ?? slug,
          bio: "Nouveau sur Ndeletik 🌍",
          avatar_url:
            session.user.user_metadata?.avatar_url ?? `https://api.dicebear.com/7.x/avataaars/svg?seed=${slug}`,
        });
      }
      if (params.get("reset") === "1") setResetMode(true);
    })();
  }, [params]);

  const generateSlug = (mail: string) => {
    const base = mail.split("@")[0].toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "");
    return `${base}${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 6)}`;
  };

  const submitPassword = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");
    setInfo("");
    try {
      if (isLogin) {
        const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });
        if (error) throw error;
      } else {
        const slug = username.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, "") || generateSlug(email);
        if (isReserved(slug)) throw new Error("Ce pseudo est réservé.");
        const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signUp({ email, password });
        if (error) throw error;
        if (data.user) {
          const { error: pe } = await supabase.from("profiles").insert({
            user_id: data.user.id,
            slug,
            display_name: slug,
            bio: "Nouveau sur Ndeletik 🌍",
            avatar_url: `https://api.dicebear.com/7.x/avataaars/svg?seed=${slug}`,
          });
          if (pe) {
            if (pe.code === "23505") throw new Error("Ce pseudo est déjà pris, choisis-en un autre.");
            if (pe.message.includes("slug_reserved")) throw new Error("Ce pseudo est réservé.");
            throw pe;
          }
        }
      }
      router.push("/dashboard");
      router.refresh();
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(fr(err.message));
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const submitMagic = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");
    setInfo("");
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithOtp({
      email,
      options: { emailRedirectTo: `${window.location.origin}/dashboard` },
    });
    setLoading(false);
    if (error) setError(fr(error.message));
    else setInfo("Lien de connexion envoyé ! Ouvre tes emails.");
  };

  const submitSms = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");
    setInfo("");
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithOtp({ phone: digits(phone) });
    setLoading(false);
    if (error) setError(fr(error.message));
    else {
      setOtpSent(true);
      setInfo("Code envoyé par SMS.");
    }
  };

  const verifySms = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.verifyOtp({ phone: digits(phone), token: otpCode, type: "sms" });
    setLoading(false);
    if (error) setError(fr(error.message));
    else {
      router.push("/dashboard");
      router.refresh();
    }
  };

  const google = async () => {
    setError("");
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithOAuth({
      provider: "google",
      options: { redirectTo: `${window.location.origin}/auth` },
    });
    if (error) setError(fr(error.message));
  };

  const forgot = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");
    setInfo("");
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.resetPasswordForEmail(email, {
      redirectTo: `${window.location.origin}/auth?reset=1`,
    });
    setLoading(false);
    if (error) setError(fr(error.message));
    else setInfo("Email de réinitialisation envoyé. Suis le lien.");
  };

  const submitReset = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({ password: newPassword });
    setLoading(false);
    if (error) setError(fr(error.message));
    else {
      setInfo("Mot de passe mis à jour.");
      router.push("/dashboard");
      router.refresh();
    }
  };

  const inputCls =
    "w-full px-4 py-3 bg-bg border border-line rounded-xl text-ink placeholder:text-muted/70 focus:outline-none focus:border-fractal-ocre transition-colors";

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-bg relative overflow-hidden">
      <Link
        href="/"
        className="absolute top-6 left-6 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted hover:text-fractal-terra transition-colors"
      >
        <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Retour à l&apos;accueil
      </Link>
      <div className="absolute -top-24 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-[500px] h-[500px] bg-fractal-or/20 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />

      <motion.div
        initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
        transition={{ duration: 0.5 }}
        className="w-full max-w-md bg-surface border border-line rounded-3xl p-8 shadow-soft relative z-10"
      >
        <div className="text-center mb-6">
          <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-fractal-or via-fractal-ocre to-fractal-terra mb-4 shadow-soft">
            <Logo className="h-6 w-6 brightness-0 invert" />
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-display font-extrabold text-ink mb-2">
            {resetMode ? "Nouveau mot de passe" : isLogin && method === "password" ? "Bon retour" : "Rejoins Ndeletik"}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted">
            {resetMode
              ? "Choisis un nouveau mot de passe."
              : "Ta page de liens unique, en 2 minutes."}
          </p>
          {plan && !resetMode && (
            <span className="mt-2 inline-block rounded-full bg-fractal-ocre/10 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-fractal-terra">
              Plan {plan} sélectionné
            </span>
          )}
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div className="mb-4 p-3 bg-fractal-terra/10 border border-fractal-terra/30 rounded-xl text-fractal-terra text-sm">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}
        {info && (
          <div className="mb-4 p-3 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-xl text-emerald-700 text-sm">
            {info}
          </div>
        )}

        {resetMode ? (
          <form onSubmit={submitReset} className="space-y-4">
            <input
              type="password"
              required
              minLength={6}
              value={newPassword}
              onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
              className={inputCls}
              placeholder="Nouveau mot de passe"
              autoComplete="new-password"
            />
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="w-full py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-fractal-or via-fractal-ocre to-fractal-terra text-white font-semibold rounded-xl disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {loading ? <Loader2 size={18} className="mx-auto animate-spin" /> : "Mettre à jour"}
            </button>
          </form>
        ) : (
          <>
            {/* Sélecteur de méthode */}
            <div className="mb-5 grid grid-cols-3 gap-1 rounded-full bg-soft p-1 text-xs font-medium" role="tablist">
              {(
                [
                  ["password", "Mot de passe", KeyRound],
                  ["magic", "Lien email", Mail],
                  ["sms", "Code SMS", Smartphone],
                ] as [Method, string, any][]
              ).map(([m, label, Icon]) => (
                <button
                  key={m}
                  role="tab"
                  aria-selected={method === m}
                  onClick={() => {
                    setMethod(m);
                    setError("");
                    setInfo("");
                    setOtpSent(false);
                  }}
                  className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-full py-2 transition ${
                    method === m ? "bg-surface text-ink shadow-soft" : "text-muted hover:text-ink"
                  }`}
                >
                  <Icon size={13} /> {label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            {method === "password" && (
              <form onSubmit={submitPassword} className="space-y-4">
                {!isLogin && (
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-ink mb-1">Nom d&apos;utilisateur (optionnel)</label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={username}
                      onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
                      className={inputCls}
                      placeholder="Laisser vide = généré automatiquement"
                      autoComplete="username"
                    />
                  </div>
                )}
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-ink mb-1">Email</label>
                  <input
                    type="email"
                    required
                    value={email}
                    onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                    className={inputCls}
                    placeholder="ton@email.com"
                    autoComplete="email"
                    inputMode="email"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-ink mb-1">Mot de passe</label>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <input
                      type={showPwd ? "text" : "password"}
                      required
                      minLength={6}
                      value={password}
                      onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                      className={`${inputCls} pr-11`}
                      placeholder="••••••••"
                      autoComplete={isLogin ? "current-password" : "new-password"}
                    />
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setShowPwd((v) => !v)}
                      aria-label={showPwd ? "Masquer le mot de passe" : "Afficher le mot de passe"}
                      className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted hover:text-ink"
                    >
                      {showPwd ? <EyeOff size={16} /> : <Eye size={16} />}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
                {isLogin && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={forgot}
                    className="text-xs text-fractal-terra hover:text-fractal-ocre transition-colors"
                  >
                    Mot de passe oublié ?
                  </button>
                )}
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="w-full py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-fractal-or via-fractal-ocre to-fractal-terra text-white font-semibold rounded-xl disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {loading ? <Loader2 size={18} className="mx-auto animate-spin" /> : isLogin ? "Se connecter" : "Créer mon compte"}
                </button>
                <div className="text-center">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setIsLogin((v) => !v)}
                    className="text-sm text-fractal-terra hover:text-fractal-ocre transition-colors"
                  >
                    {isLogin ? "Pas encore de compte ? S'inscrire" : "Déjà un compte ? Se connecter"}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </form>
            )}

            {method === "magic" && (
              <form onSubmit={submitMagic} className="space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-ink mb-1">Email</label>
                  <input
                    type="email"
                    required
                    value={email}
                    onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                    className={inputCls}
                    placeholder="ton@email.com"
                    autoComplete="email"
                    inputMode="email"
                  />
                </div>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="w-full py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-fractal-or via-fractal-ocre to-fractal-terra text-white font-semibold rounded-xl disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {loading ? <Loader2 size={18} className="mx-auto animate-spin" /> : "Recevoir le lien de connexion"}
                </button>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted text-center">Sans mot de passe : un clic dans ton email et tu es connecté.</p>
              </form>
            )}

            {method === "sms" && (
              <form onSubmit={otpSent ? verifySms : submitSms} className="space-y-4">
                {!otpSent ? (
                  <>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-ink mb-1">Numéro de téléphone</label>
                      <input
                        type="tel"
                        required
                        value={phone}
                        onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
                        className={inputCls}
                        placeholder="+242 06 000 00 00"
                        autoComplete="tel"
                        inputMode="tel"
                      />
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted mt-1">Format international. Un code à 6 chiffres sera envoyé.</p>
                    </div>
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      disabled={loading}
                      className="w-full py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-fractal-or via-fractal-ocre to-fractal-terra text-white font-semibold rounded-xl disabled:opacity-50"
                    >
                      {loading ? <Loader2 size={18} className="mx-auto animate-spin" /> : "Recevoir le code"}
                    </button>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-ink mb-1">Code à 6 chiffres</label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        required
                        inputMode="numeric"
                        maxLength={6}
                        value={otpCode}
                        onChange={(e) => setOtpCode(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, ""))}
                        className={`${inputCls} text-center text-2xl tracking-[0.5em]`}
                        placeholder="000000"
                        autoComplete="one-time-code"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      disabled={loading || otpCode.length !== 6}
                      className="w-full py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-fractal-or via-fractal-ocre to-fractal-terra text-white font-semibold rounded-xl disabled:opacity-50"
                    >
                      {loading ? <Loader2 size={18} className="mx-auto animate-spin" /> : "Vérifier et entrer"}
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setOtpSent(false)}
                      className="w-full text-xs text-muted hover:text-ink transition-colors"
                    >
                      Changer de numéro
                    </button>
                  </>
                )}
              </form>
            )}

            <div className="my-5 flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-muted">
              <span className="h-px flex-1 bg-line" /> ou <span className="h-px flex-1 bg-line" />
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={google}
              className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-3 rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface text-sm font-semibold text-ink hover:border-fractal-ocre transition-colors"
            >
              <FaGoogle size={16} /> Continuer avec Google
            </button>
          </>
        )}
      </motion.div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/app/contact/page.tsx =====

import { Mail, MessageCircle, ArrowLeft, Clock, HelpCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { Wordmark } from "@/components/landing/ui";

export const metadata = {
  title: "Contact",
  description: "Contacte l'équipe Ndeletik par email ou WhatsApp. Réponse sous 48h ouvrées.",
};

/* ⚠️ Remplace par tes vrais canaux avant le push */
const EMAIL = "hello@ndeletik.com";
const WHATSAPP = "242060000000";

export default function ContactPage() {
  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-bg text-ink">
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 border-b border-line bg-surface/80 backdrop-blur-xl">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-3xl items-center justify-between px-4 py-3">
          <Link href="/">
            <Wordmark />
          </Link>
          <Link
            href="/"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-muted hover:text-fractal-terra transition-colors"

            <ArrowLeft size={14} /> Accueil
          </Link>
        </div>
      </header>

      <article className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 py-10 sm:py-14">
        <h1 className="font-display text-3xl font-extrabold sm:text-4xl">Contact</h1>
        <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted">
          Une question, un problème, un partenariat ? Deux canaux directs, sans formulaire inutile.
        </p>

        <div className="mt-8 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <a
            href={`mailto:${EMAIL}?subject=[Ndeletik]%20Contact`}
            className="group rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-5 shadow-soft transition-colors hover:border-fractal-ocre"
          >
            <span className="mb-3 flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-soft text-fractal-ocre">
              <Mail size={18} />
            </span>
            <h2 className="text-sm font-bold">Email</h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted break-all">{EMAIL}</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-[11px] text-fractal-terra opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100">
              Cliquer pour écrire →
            </p>
          </a>

          <a
            href={`https://wa.me/${WHATSAPP}?text=Bonjour%20Ndeletik%20!`}
            target="_blank"
            rel="noopener noreferrer"
            className="group rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-5 shadow-soft transition-colors hover:border-fractal-ocre"
          >
            <span className="mb-3 flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-soft text-fractal-ocre">
              <MessageCircle size={18} />
            </span>
            <h2 className="text-sm font-bold">WhatsApp</h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted">Support rapide, du lundi au samedi.</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-[11px] text-fractal-terra opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100">
              Ouvrir la conversation →
            </p>
          </a>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-line bg-soft p-5 text-sm sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
          <Clock size={16} className="shrink-0 text-fractal-ocre" />
          <p className="text-muted">
            Délai de réponse habituel : <span className="font-semibold text-ink">moins de 48 h ouvrées</span>. Pour
            les urgences de paiement, précise ta commande dans le premier message.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-line p-5 text-sm">
          <HelpCircle size={16} className="shrink-0 text-fractal-ocre" />
          <p className="text-muted">
            La plupart des réponses sont déjà dans la{" "}
            <Link href="/#faq" className="font-semibold text-fractal-terra hover:text-fractal-ocre">
              FAQ
            </Link>
            .
          </p>
        </div>
      </article>
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Reorder } from "framer-motion";
import { Eye, Link2, Plus, Sparkles } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, buttonClass } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/Card";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/ui/EmptyState";
import { Modal } from "@/components/ui/Modal";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/ui/PageHeader";
import { AddBlockModal, type BlockPreset } from "@/components/dashboard/AddBlockModal";
import { useDashboard } from "@/components/dashboard/DashboardProvider";
import { LinkAnalyticsModal } from "@/components/dashboard/LinkAnalyticsModal";
import { LinkItem } from "@/components/dashboard/LinkItem";
import { LinkTemplates } from "@/components/dashboard/LinkTemplates";
import { LivePreview } from "@/components/dashboard/LivePreview";
import { ShortLinkManager } from "@/components/dashboard/ShortLinkManager";
import type { DbLink } from "@/lib/dashboard-types";

export default function LinksPage() {
  const d = useDashboard();
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState<{ open: boolean; preset?: BlockPreset }>({ open: false });
  const [previewOpen, setPreviewOpen] = useState(false);
  const [analyticsLink, setAnalyticsLink] = useState<DbLink | null>(null);

  const limited = d.maxLinks !== null;
  const atLimit = limited && d.activeLinks >= (d.maxLinks as number);

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        title="Mes liens"
        description="Ajoute, réorganise par glisser-déposer et publie les liens de ta page."
        actions={
          <>
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setPreviewOpen(true)} className="lg:hidden">
              <Eye size={16} aria-hidden /> Aperçu
            </Button>
            <Button onClick={() => setAdding({ open: true })}>
              <Plus size={16} aria-hidden /> Ajouter un lien
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      />

      <div className="grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_340px]">
        <div className="min-w-0 space-y-8">
          {limited && (
            <Card className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-6 gap-y-3">
              <div className="min-w-[10rem] flex-1">
                <div className="mb-1.5 flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
                  <span className="font-semibold text-ink">Liens actifs</span>
                  <span className={atLimit ? "font-semibold text-fractal-terra" : "text-muted"}>
                    {d.activeLinks} / {d.maxLinks}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-line/60" role="progressbar" aria-valuenow={d.activeLinks} aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={d.maxLinks as number} aria-label="Liens actifs sur la limite du plan">
                  <div
                    className="h-full rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-fractal-or to-fractal-terra transition-all"
                    style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, (d.activeLinks / (d.maxLinks as number)) * 100)}%` }}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              <Link href="/#tarifs" className={buttonClass({ variant: atLimit ? "primary" : "secondary", size: "sm" })}>
                <Sparkles size={14} aria-hidden /> {atLimit ? "Débloquer plus de liens" : "Passer au Pro"}
              </Link>
            </Card>
          )}

          <LinkTemplates onPick={(t) => setAdding({ open: true, preset: { title: t.title, url: t.url, icon: t.icon } })} />

          <section aria-labelledby="links-list">
            <h2 id="links-list" className="mb-3 font-display text-sm font-bold text-ink">
              Mes liens ({d.links.length})
            </h2>
            {d.links.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState
                icon={<Link2 size={22} aria-hidden />}
                title="Aucun lien pour le moment"
                description="Choisis un modèle ci-dessus ou ajoute ton premier lien. Il apparaîtra tout de suite dans l'aperçu."
                action={
                  <Button onClick={() => setAdding({ open: true })}>
                    <Plus size={16} aria-hidden /> Ajouter mon premier lien
                  </Button>
                }
              />
            ) : (
              <Reorder.Group axis="y" values={d.links} onReorder={d.setLinks} className="m-0 space-y-2.5 p-0">
                {d.links.map((l) => (
                  <LinkItem
                    key={l.id}
                    link={l}
                    onUpdate={d.updateLink}
                    onDelete={d.deleteLink}
                    onDragEnd={d.persistOrder}
                    onShowAnalytics={setAnalyticsLink}
                  />
                ))}
              </Reorder.Group>
            )}
          </section>

          {d.userId && <ShortLinkManager userId={d.userId} plan={d.plan} />}
        </div>

        <aside className="hidden lg:block">
          <div className="sticky top-24">
            <LivePreview />
          </div>
        </aside>
      </div>

      <AddBlockModal
        open={adding.open}
        initial={adding.preset}
        onClose={() => setAdding({ open: false })}
        onAdd={d.addLink}
      />

      <Modal open={previewOpen} onClose={() => setPreviewOpen(false)} title="Aperçu de ma page" size="sm">
        <LivePreview framed={false} />
      </Modal>

      {d.profileId && (
        <LinkAnalyticsModal
          open={!!analyticsLink}
          onClose={() => setAnalyticsLink(null)}
          link={analyticsLink}
          profile={{ id: d.profileId }}
        />
      )}
    </>
  );
}

import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { SectionsEditor } from "@/components/dashboard/BlocksEditor";
import { toProfile } from "@/lib/profile-mapper";
import { getCurrentProfileRow } from "@/lib/current-profile";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
export const metadata: Metadata = { title: "Sections de ma page" };

export default async function SectionsPage() {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) redirect("/auth");

  const row = await getCurrentProfileRow(supabase, user.id);

  if (!row) {
    return (
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-xl px-6 py-24 text-center text-muted">
        Crée d&apos;abord ta page pour gérer tes sections.
      </main>
    );
  }

  const { profile } = toProfile({ profile: row, plan: "free", links: [] });

  return (
    <SectionsEditor
      profileId={row.id}
      initialBlocks={profile.blocks ?? []}
      initialSocials={profile.socials}
    />
  );
}
