===== FILE: src/app/dashboard/stats/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import dynamic from "next/dynamic";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { BarChart3, Download, Globe, MousePointerClick, Trophy } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { Card, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/Card";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/ui/EmptyState";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/ui/PageHeader";
import { AnalyticsChart } from "@/components/dashboard/AnalyticsChart";
import { useDashboard } from "@/components/dashboard/DashboardProvider";
import { LinkAnalyticsModal } from "@/components/dashboard/LinkAnalyticsModal";
import { useToast } from "@/components/dashboard/Toast";
import type { DbLink } from "@/lib/dashboard-types";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabaseClient";

const WorldMap = dynamic(() => import("@/components/admin/WorldMap").then((m) => m.WorldMap), {
  ssr: false,
  loading: () => <div className="h-64 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-soft" />,
});

type TrafficSource = { name: string; count: number; percentage: number };
type CountryMarker = { lat: number; lon: number; plan: string; count: number; country: string };

const COUNTRY_COORDS: Record<string, [number, number]> = {
  FR: [46.6, 2.2], US: [37.0, -95.7], GB: [55.3, -3.4], DE: [51.1, 10.4],
  ES: [40.4, -3.7], IT: [41.8, 12.5], BE: [50.5, 4.4], CH: [46.8, 8.2],
  CA: [56.1, -106.3], CG: [-0.2, 15.8], CD: [-2.9, 23.7], CM: [7.3, 12.3],
  CI: [7.5, -5.5], SN: [14.4, -14.4], ML: [17.5, -4.0], BF: [12.2, -1.6],
  MA: [31.7, -7.0], TN: [33.8, 9.7], DZ: [28.0, 1.6], GA: [-0.8, 11.6],
  NG: [9.1, 8.7], ZA: [-30.6, 22.9], BR: [-14.2, -51.9], PT: [39.4, -8.2],
};

function extractSource(url: string): string {
  if (!url) return "Direct";
  try {
    const domain = new URL(url).hostname.replace(/^www\./, "");
    if (domain.includes("instagram")) return "Instagram";
    if (domain.includes("tiktok")) return "TikTok";
    if (domain.includes("facebook") || domain.includes("fb.")) return "Facebook";
    if (domain.includes("twitter") || domain.includes("x.com")) return "X (Twitter)";
    if (domain.includes("youtube")) return "YouTube";
    if (domain.includes("linkedin")) return "LinkedIn";
    if (domain.includes("google")) return "Google";
    return domain;
  } catch {
    return "Direct";
  }
}

export default function StatsPage() {
  const d = useDashboard();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [sources, setSources] = useState<TrafficSource[]>([]);
  const [markers, setMarkers] = useState<CountryMarker[]>([]);
  const [analyticsLink, setAnalyticsLink] = useState<DbLink | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!d.profileId) return;
    let cancelled = false;
    (async () => {
      const { data: clicks } = await supabase.from("link_clicks").select("referrer, country").eq("profile_id", d.profileId);
      if (cancelled) return;

      const referrers = (clicks ?? []).map((c: any) => c.referrer).filter(Boolean) as string[];
      const sourceMap = new Map<string, number>();
      referrers.forEach((r) => sourceMap.set(extractSource(r), (sourceMap.get(extractSource(r)) ?? 0) + 1));
      const total = referrers.length || 1;
      setSources(
        Array.from(sourceMap.entries())
          .map(([name, count]) => ({ name, count, percentage: (count / total) * 100 }))
          .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count)
          .slice(0, 5)
      );

      const countryMap = new Map<string, number>();
      (clicks ?? []).forEach((c: any) => {
        if (c.country) countryMap.set(c.country.toUpperCase(), (countryMap.get(c.country.toUpperCase()) ?? 0) + 1);
      });
      setMarkers(
        Array.from(countryMap.entries())
          .filter(([code]) => COUNTRY_COORDS[code])
          .map(([code, count]) => ({ lat: COUNTRY_COORDS[code][0], lon: COUNTRY_COORDS[code][1], plan: "free", count, country: code }))
      );
    })();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [d.profileId]);

  const totalClicks = useMemo(() => d.links.reduce((s, l) => s + l.clicks, 0), [d.links]);
  const maxClicks = Math.max(1, ...d.links.map((l) => l.clicks));
  const best = useMemo(() => [...d.links].sort((a, b) => b.clicks - a.clicks)[0], [d.links]);

  const exportCsv = async () => {
    if (!d.profileId) return;
    const since = new Date(Date.now() - 90 * 86400000).toISOString();
    const [{ data: clicks }, { data: lnks }] = await Promise.all([
      supabase
        .from("link_clicks")
        .select("created_at, referrer, country, link_id")
        .eq("profile_id", d.profileId)
        .gte("created_at", since)
        .order("created_at", { ascending: false })
        .limit(5000),
      supabase.from("links").select("id, title").eq("profile_id", d.profileId),
    ]);
    const titles = new Map<string, string>((lnks ?? []).map((l: any) => [l.id, l.title]));
    const rows = (clicks ?? []).map((c: any) => [
      new Date(c.created_at).toLocaleString("fr-FR"),
      titles.get(c.link_id) ?? "(lien supprimé)",
      extractSource(c.referrer ?? ""),
      c.country ?? "",
    ]);
    const csv = [["date", "lien", "source", "pays"], ...rows]
      .map((r) => r.map((v) => `"${String(v).replace(/"/g, '""')}"`).join(","))
      .join("\n");
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob(["\uFEFF" + csv], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8" }));
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `ndeletik-clics-90j-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.csv`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    toast(`Export CSV : ${rows.length} clic(s) sur 90 jours`);
  };

  if (!d.profileId) return null;

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        title="Statistiques"
        description="Qui clique sur tes liens, et d'où viennent tes visiteurs."
        actions={
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={exportCsv}>
            <Download size={16} aria-hidden /> Export CSV
          </Button>
        }
      />

      {d.links.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          icon={<BarChart3 size={22} aria-hidden />}
          title="Pas encore de statistiques"
          description="Ajoute des liens à ta page et partage-la : les clics apparaîtront ici."
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-6">
          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
            <Kpi icon={<MousePointerClick size={18} aria-hidden />} label="Clics au total" value={String(totalClicks)} />
            <Kpi icon={<BarChart3 size={18} aria-hidden />} label="Liens actifs" value={`${d.activeLinks} / ${d.links.length}`} />
            <Kpi icon={<Trophy size={18} aria-hidden />} label="Lien le plus cliqué" value={best && best.clicks > 0 ? best.title : "—"} small />
          </div>

          <AnalyticsChart profile={{ id: d.profileId }} />

          <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
            {sources.length > 0 && (
              <Card>
                <CardTitle hint="Les 5 premières provenances.">Sources de trafic</CardTitle>
                <ul className="space-y-3">
                  {sources.map((s) => (
                    <li key={s.name}>
                      <div className="mb-1 flex justify-between text-sm">
                        <span className="font-medium">{s.name}</span>
                        <span className="text-muted">
                          {s.count} ({s.percentage.toFixed(1)}%)
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="h-2 rounded-full bg-line/50">
                        <motion.div
                          initial={{ width: 0 }}
                          animate={{ width: `${s.percentage}%` }}
                          transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: "easeOut" }}
                          className="h-2 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-fractal-ocre to-fractal-terra"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              </Card>
            )}

            <Card className={sources.length === 0 ? "lg:col-span-2" : undefined}>
              <CardTitle hint="Clique sur un lien pour voir son détail.">Détail par lien</CardTitle>
              <ul className="space-y-3">
                {[...d.links]
                  .sort((a, b) => b.clicks - a.clicks)
                  .map((l) => (
                    <li key={l.id}>
                      <button type="button" onClick={() => setAnalyticsLink(l)} className="group w-full text-left">
                        <div className="mb-1 flex justify-between text-sm">
                          <span className="truncate pr-3 font-medium transition-colors group-hover:text-fractal-terra">{l.title}</span>
                          <span className="shrink-0 text-muted">{l.clicks} clics</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="h-2 rounded-full bg-line/50">
                          <motion.div
                            initial={{ width: 0 }}
                            animate={{ width: `${(l.clicks / maxClicks) * 100}%` }}
                            transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: "easeOut" }}
                            className="h-2 rounded-full bg-fractal-ocre"
                          />
                        </div>
                      </button>
                    </li>
                  ))}
              </ul>
            </Card>
          </div>

          {markers.length > 0 && (
            <Card>
              <CardTitle>
                <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Globe size={16} className="text-fractal-ocre" aria-hidden /> Pays de tes visiteurs
                </span>
              </CardTitle>
              <WorldMap markers={markers} />
            </Card>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      <LinkAnalyticsModal open={!!analyticsLink} onClose={() => setAnalyticsLink(null)} link={analyticsLink} profile={{ id: d.profileId }} />
    </>
  );
}

function Kpi({ icon, label, value, small }: { icon: React.ReactNode; label: string; value: string; small?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <Card className="flex items-center gap-4">
      <span className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-fractal-ocre/10 text-fractal-ocre">{icon}</span>
      <div className="min-w-0">
        <p className="text-xs text-muted">{label}</p>
        <p className={small ? "truncate font-display text-base font-extrabold text-ink" : "font-display text-2xl font-extrabold text-ink"}>{value}</p>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/app/dashboard/themes/page.tsx =====

import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { ThemeSelector } from "@/components/editor/ThemeSelector";
import { toProfile } from "@/lib/profile-mapper";
import { resolveTheme } from "@/lib/themes/registry";
import { getCurrentProfileRow } from "@/lib/current-profile";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export default async function ThemesPage() {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) redirect("/auth");

  const [row, { data: planData }] = await Promise.all([
    getCurrentProfileRow(supabase, user.id),
    supabase.rpc("my_plan"),
  ]);

  if (!row) {
    return (
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-xl px-6 py-24 text-center text-muted">
        Crée d&apos;abord ta page pour choisir un thème.
      </main>
    );
  }

  const { data: links } = await supabase
    .from("links")
    .select("*")
    .eq("profile_id", row.id)
    .order("position");

  const { profile, plan } = toProfile({ profile: row, plan: planData ?? "free", links: links ?? [] });

  // Abonnement expiré : le thème choisi est en pause, un thème gratuit s'affiche à la place.
  const { theme: shown, locked } = resolveTheme(profile.themeId, plan);

  return (
    <ThemeSelector
      profile={profile}
      plan={plan}
      currentThemeId={shown.id}
      currentCustom={profile.customTokens ?? {}}
      pausedThemeId={locked ? profile.themeId : undefined}
    />
  );
}
===== FILE: src/app/demo/page.tsx =====

import Link from "next/link";
import { Sparkles, ArrowLeft } from "lucide-react";
import { Wordmark } from "@/components/landing/ui";
import { DemoLinks } from "@/components/demo/DemoLinks";

export const metadata = {
  title: "Page exemple",
  description: "Découvre à quoi ressemble une page Ndeletik complète : liens, design et boutons officiels.",
};

export default function DemoPage() {
  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-bg relative overflow-hidden">
      {/* Décor fractal clair */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none" aria-hidden="true">
        <div className="absolute -top-32 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-[500px] h-[500px] bg-fractal-or/20 rounded-full blur-3xl" />
        <div className="absolute top-10 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-[600px] h-[600px] border border-fractal-ocre/10 rounded-full" />
        <div className="absolute top-24 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-[450px] h-[450px] border border-fractal-ocre/15 rounded-full" />
      </div>

      <header className="relative z-20 flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-4">
        <Link href="/">
          <Wordmark />
        </Link>
        <Link
          href="/"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-muted hover:text-fractal-terra transition-colors"
        >
          <ArrowLeft size={14} /> Accueil
        </Link>
      </header>

      {/* Bandeau démo */}
      <div className="relative z-20 mx-auto max-w-lg px-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-line bg-surface px-4 py-2 text-xs text-muted shadow-soft">
          <Sparkles size={12} className="text-fractal-ocre" />
          Page de démonstration — données fictives
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="relative z-10 max-w-lg mx-auto px-4 pt-8 pb-24">
        {/* Profil fictif */}
        <header className="text-center">
          <div className="relative w-28 h-28 mx-auto mb-5">
            <div className="absolute -inset-1.5 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-fractal-or via-fractal-ocre to-fractal-terra opacity-90 blur-[2px]" />
            <div
              aria-hidden="true"
              className="relative flex h-28 w-28 items-center justify-center rounded-full border-4 border-bg bg-gradient-to-br from-fractal-or to-fractal-terra font-display text-4xl font-extrabold text-white"
            >
              A
            </div>
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-display font-extrabold text-ink">@awa.demo</h1>
          <p className="mt-3 text-muted leading-relaxed">
            Créatrice de contenu · Coaching & beauté 🌍
            <br />
            Voici à quoi ressemble ta page Ndeletik.
          </p>
        </header>

        <DemoLinks />

        {/* CTA final */}
        <footer className="mt-16 text-center">
          <p className="text-sm text-muted mb-4">Cette page pourrait être la tienne en 5 minutes.</p>
          <Link
            href="/auth?mode=signup"
            className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-ink px-7 py-3.5 text-sm font-medium text-bg shadow-lg transition hover:opacity-90"
          >
            Créer ma page gratuite
          </Link>
        </footer>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/app/go/[id]/route.ts =====

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { safeUrl } from "@/lib/safe-url";
import { createAdminClient } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";
import { createPublicClient } from "@/lib/supabase/public";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const UUID = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;

/**
 * /go/[id] :
 *  - UUID  → lien de profil : compte le clic (atomique) puis redirige.
 *  - sinon → raccourci personnalisé : résout le slug puis redirige.
 * L'URL cible vient TOUJOURS de la base, jamais du client.
 */
export async function GET(req: NextRequest, ctx: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> | { id: string } }) {
  const { id } = await ctx.params;

  // 1) Suivi de clic d'un lien de profil
  if (UUID.test(id)) {
    const admin = createAdminClient();
    const { data: link } = await admin
      .from("links")
      .select("id, url, enabled")
      .eq("id", id)
      .maybeSingle();
    if (!link || !link.enabled || !safeUrl(link.url)) {
      return new NextResponse("Lien introuvable", { status: 404 });
    }
    try {
      await createPublicClient().rpc("track_link_click", {
        p_link: link.id,
        p_referrer: req.headers.get("referer")?.slice(0, 300) ?? null,
        p_country: req.headers.get("x-vercel-ip-country")?.slice(0, 2) ?? null,
      });
    } catch {
      /* le comptage ne bloque jamais la redirection */
    }
    const res = NextResponse.redirect(link.url, 302);
    res.headers.set("Cache-Control", "no-store");
    return res;
  }

  // 2) Raccourci personnalisé (/go/mon-alias)
  const slug = id.trim().slice(0, 100);
  if (!slug) return NextResponse.redirect(new URL("/", req.url));
  const { data: url } = await createPublicClient().rpc("resolve_short_link", { p_slug: slug });
  if (!url || !safeUrl(url)) return NextResponse.redirect(new URL("/", req.url));
  const res = NextResponse.redirect(url, 302);
  res.headers.set("Cache-Control", "no-store");
  return res;
}

===== FILE: src/app/onboarding/page.tsx =====

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";
import { ArrowRight, ArrowLeft, Loader2, SkipForward } from "lucide-react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabaseClient";
import { FREE_THEMES } from "@/lib/themes/free";
import { LinkTemplates } from "@/components/dashboard/LinkTemplates";

const STEPS = ["Profil", "Premier lien", "Thème", "Prêt !"] as const;
type Step = (typeof STEPS)[number];

export default function OnboardingPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>("Profil");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  // Étape 1 : Profil
  const [displayName, setDisplayName] = useState("");
  const [headline, setHeadline] = useState("");
  const [bio, setBio] = useState("");

  // Étape 2 : Premier lien
  const [linkTitle, setLinkTitle] = useState("");
  const [linkUrl, setLinkUrl] = useState("");

  // Étape 3 : Thème
  const [selectedThemeId, setSelectedThemeId] = useState(FREE_THEMES[0].id);

  const next = () => {
    const idx = STEPS.indexOf(step);
    if (idx < STEPS.length - 1) setStep(STEPS[idx + 1]);
  };

  const prev = () => {
    const idx = STEPS.indexOf(step);
    if (idx > 0) setStep(STEPS[idx - 1]);
  };

  const complete = async (skip = false) => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      if (!user) throw new Error("Session expirée");

      const { data: profile } = await supabase
        .from("profiles")
        .select("id")
        .eq("user_id", user.id)
        .single();

      if (!profile) throw new Error("Profil introuvable");

      // Mise à jour du profil
      if (!skip) {
        const { error: profileError } = await supabase
          .from("profiles")
          .update({
            display_name: displayName || "Mon profil",
            headline: headline || null,
            bio: bio || null,
            theme_id: selectedThemeId,
            onboarding_completed: true,
          })
          .eq("id", profile.id);

        if (profileError) throw profileError;

        // Création du premier lien
        if (linkTitle && linkUrl) {
          const { error: linkError } = await supabase.from("links").insert({
            profile_id: profile.id,
            title: linkTitle,
            url: linkUrl,
            position: 0,
          });
          if (linkError) throw linkError;
        }
      } else {
        // Skip : juste marquer comme complété
        const { error: skipError } = await supabase
          .from("profiles")
          .update({ onboarding_completed: true })
          .eq("id", profile.id);
        if (skipError) throw skipError;
      }

      router.push("/dashboard");
      router.refresh();
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || "Erreur inattendue");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const inputCls =
    "w-full rounded-xl border border-line bg-bg px-4 py-3 text-sm text-ink placeholder:text-muted/70 focus:outline-none focus:border-fractal-ocre transition-colors";

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-bg p-4 sm:p-8">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl">
        {/* Header avec progression */}
        <div className="mb-8 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {STEPS.map((s, i) => (
              <div key={s} className="flex items-center">
                <div
                  className={`h-2 w-2 rounded-full ${
                    STEPS.indexOf(step) >= i ? "bg-fractal-ocre" : "bg-line"
                  }`}
                />
                {i < STEPS.length - 1 && (
                  <div
                    className={`h-0.5 w-8 ${
                      STEPS.indexOf(step) > i ? "bg-fractal-ocre" : "bg-line"
                    }`}
                  />
                )}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => complete(true)}
            disabled={loading}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-muted hover:text-fractal-terra transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
          >
            <SkipForward size={14} /> Passer
          </button>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div className="mb-4 rounded-xl border border-fractal-terra/30 bg-fractal-terra/10 p-3 text-sm text-fractal-terra">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
          {step === "Profil" && (
            <motion.div
              key="profil"
              initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 20 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, x: -20 }}
              className="rounded-3xl border border-line bg-surface p-6 shadow-soft"
            >
              <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold mb-2">👤 Ton profil</h1>
              <p className="text-sm text-muted mb-6">Comment veux-tu apparaître sur ta page ?</p>

              <div className="space-y-4">
                <label className="block">
                  <span className="text-xs font-medium text-ink mb-1.5 block">Nom affiché *</span>
                  <input
                    value={displayName}
                    onChange={(e) => setDisplayName(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Ex : Alvine Coach"
                    className={inputCls}
                    required
                  />
                </label>
                <label className="block">
                  <span className="text-xs font-medium text-ink mb-1.5 block">Titre (sous le nom)</span>
                  <input
                    value={headline}
                    onChange={(e) => setHeadline(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Ex : Coach business · Pointe-Noire"
                    className={inputCls}
                  />
                </label>
                <label className="block">
                  <span className="text-xs font-medium text-ink mb-1.5 block">Bio (optionnel)</span>
                  <textarea
                    value={bio}
                    onChange={(e) => setBio(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Présente-toi en quelques mots…"
                    rows={3}
                    className={inputCls}
                  />
                </label>
              </div>

              <button
                onClick={next}
                disabled={!displayName.trim() || loading}
                className="mt-6 w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-fractal-ocre hover:bg-fractal-terra px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
              >
                Continuer <ArrowRight size={16} />
              </button>
            </motion.div>
          )}

          {step === "Premier lien" && (
            <motion.div
              key="lien"
              initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 20 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, x: -20 }}
              className="rounded-3xl border border-line bg-surface p-6 shadow-soft"
            >
              <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold mb-2">🔗 Ton premier lien</h1>
              <p className="text-sm text-muted mb-6">Où veux-tu que tes visiteurs aillent en premier ?</p>

              <LinkTemplates
                onAdd={async (title, url) => {
                  setLinkTitle(title);
                  setLinkUrl(url);
                  return true;
                }}
              />

              <div className="mt-6 space-y-4">
                <label className="block">
                  <span className="text-xs font-medium text-ink mb-1.5 block">Titre</span>
                  <input
                    value={linkTitle}
                    onChange={(e) => setLinkTitle(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Ex : Mon WhatsApp"
                    className={inputCls}
                  />
                </label>
                <label className="block">
                  <span className="text-xs font-medium text-ink mb-1.5 block">URL</span>
                  <input
                    value={linkUrl}
                    onChange={(e) => setLinkUrl(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="https://wa.me/…"
                    className={inputCls}
                  />
                </label>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-6 flex gap-3">
                <button
                  onClick={prev}
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-line px-5 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-muted hover:bg-soft transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Précédent
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={next}
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-fractal-ocre hover:bg-fractal-terra px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  Continuer <ArrowRight size={16} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          )}

          {step === "Thème" && (
            <motion.div
              key="theme"
              initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 20 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, x: -20 }}
              className="rounded-3xl border border-line bg-surface p-6 shadow-soft"
            >
              <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold mb-2">🎨 Choisis un thème</h1>
              <p className="text-sm text-muted mb-6">Tu pourras le changer plus tard dans l'éditeur de thèmes.</p>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-4">
                {FREE_THEMES.map((t) => (
                  <button
                    key={t.id}
                    onClick={() => setSelectedThemeId(t.id)}
                    className={`rounded-2xl border-2 p-3 text-center transition ${
                      selectedThemeId === t.id
                        ? "border-fractal-ocre bg-fractal-ocre/10"
                        : "border-line hover:border-fractal-ocre/50"
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div
                      className="mx-auto mb-2 h-16 w-16 rounded-xl"
                      style={{ background: t.tokens.bg }}
                    />
                    <p className="text-xs font-semibold">{t.name}</p>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>

              <div className="mt-6 flex gap-3">
                <button
                  onClick={prev}
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-line px-5 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-muted hover:bg-soft transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Précédent
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={next}
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-fractal-ocre hover:bg-fractal-terra px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  Continuer <ArrowRight size={16} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          )}

          {step === "Prêt !" && (
            <motion.div
              key="pret"
              initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 20 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, x: -20 }}
              className="rounded-3xl border border-line bg-surface p-6 shadow-soft text-center"
            >
              <h1 className="font-display text-3xl font-bold mb-2">🚀 Ta page est prête !</h1>
              <p className="text-sm text-muted mb-6">Tu peux maintenant personnaliser davantage ou commencer à partager.</p>

              <div className="mx-auto mb-6 max-w-xs rounded-2xl border border-line bg-soft p-4">
                <p className="text-xs text-muted mb-1">Ton lien public</p>
                <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-fractal-terra truncate">ndeletik.com/@alvine</p>
              </div>

              <div className="flex gap-3">
                <button
                  onClick={prev}
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-line px-5 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-muted hover:bg-soft transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Précédent
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => complete(false)}
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-fractal-ocre hover:bg-fractal-terra px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  {loading ? (
                    <>
                      <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" /> Chargement…
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      Aller au dashboard <ArrowRight size={16} />
                    </>
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          )}
        </AnimatePresence>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// src/app/preview/page.tsx
import { ProfileRenderer } from "@/components/profile/ProfileRenderer";
import { ALL_THEMES } from "@/lib/themes/registry";
import { SAMPLE_PROFILE } from "@/lib/themes/sample";
import type { Plan } from "@/lib/plans";
type SP = Record<string, string | undefined>;
export default async function PreviewPage({ searchParams }: { searchParams: Promise<SP> | SP }) {
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const plan = (["free", "pro", "business"].includes(sp.plan ?? "") ? sp.plan : "business") as Plan;
  const themeId = sp.theme ?? ALL_THEMES[0]?.id ?? "fallback";
  return <ProfileRenderer profile={{ ...SAMPLE_PROFILE, themeId }} plan={plan} preview />;
}

}

===== FILE: src/components/admin/AdminProvider.tsx =====

"use client";

import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabaseClient";
import { fmtDate, type AdminRow } from "@/lib/admin-types";
import { ToastProvider, useToast } from "@/components/dashboard/Toast";

type Status = "loading" | "denied" | "ready";

type Ctx = {
  status: Status;
  meId: string | null;
  rows: AdminRow[];
  /** Admin → utilisateur ou l'inverse (concerne tous les profils du compte). */
  toggleRole: (r: AdminRow) => Promise<boolean>;
  /** Supprime le profil ; supprime aussi le compte s'il n'en reste aucun autre. */
  deleteUser: (r: AdminRow) => Promise<boolean>;
  exportCsv: (list: AdminRow[]) => void;
};

const AdminCtx = createContext<Ctx | null>(null);

export function useAdmin(): Ctx {
  const ctx = useContext(AdminCtx);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error("useAdmin doit être utilisé dans <AdminProvider>");
  return ctx;
}

export function AdminProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <ToastProvider>
      <Inner>{children}</Inner>
    </ToastProvider>
  );
}

function Inner({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<Status>("loading");
  const [meId, setMeId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<AdminRow[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    (async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      if (!session) return router.replace("/auth");

      const [{ data: profs }, { data: accs }, { data: lnks }] = await Promise.all([
        supabase.from("profiles").select("id, user_id, slug, display_name, avatar_url, country, views, theme_id, created_at"),
        supabase.from("accounts").select("user_id, plan, role"),
        supabase.from("links").select("profile_id, clicks"),
      ]);
      if (!alive) return;

      const me = (accs ?? []).find((a: any) => a.user_id === session.user.id);
      setMeId(session.user.id);
      if (!me || me.role !== "admin") return setStatus("denied");

      const accByUser = new Map<string, any>((accs ?? []).map((a: any) => [a.user_id, a]));
      const linkAgg = new Map<string, { count: number; clicks: number }>();
      (lnks ?? []).forEach((l: any) => {
        const a = linkAgg.get(l.profile_id) ?? { count: 0, clicks: 0 };
        a.count += 1;
        a.clicks += l.clicks ?? 0;
        linkAgg.set(l.profile_id, a);
      });

      setRows(
        ((profs ?? []) as any[]).map((p) => ({
          id: p.id,
          user_id: p.user_id,
          slug: p.slug,
          display_name: p.display_name,
          avatar_url: p.avatar_url ?? null,
          country: p.country ?? null,
          views: p.views ?? 0,
          theme_id: p.theme_id,
          created_at: p.created_at,
          plan: accByUser.get(p.user_id)?.plan ?? "free",
          role: accByUser.get(p.user_id)?.role ?? "user",
          links_count: linkAgg.get(p.id)?.count ?? 0,
          clicks_total: linkAgg.get(p.id)?.clicks ?? 0,
        }))
      );
      setStatus("ready");
    })();
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [router]);

  const toggleRole = useCallback(
    async (r: AdminRow) => {
      if (r.user_id === meId) {
        toast("Tu ne peux pas modifier ton propre rôle.", false);
        return false;
      }
      const next = r.role === "admin" ? "user" : "admin";
      const { error } = await supabase.from("accounts").update({ role: next }).eq("user_id", r.user_id);
      if (error) {
        toast("Modification du rôle échouée", false);
        return false;
      }
      setRows((prev) => prev.map((x) => (x.user_id === r.user_id ? { ...x, role: next } : x)));
      toast(next === "admin" ? `@${r.slug} est maintenant administrateur` : `@${r.slug} n'est plus administrateur`);
      return true;
    },
    [meId, toast]
  );

  const deleteUser = useCallback(
    async (r: AdminRow) => {
      if (r.user_id === meId) {
        toast("Tu ne peux pas supprimer ton propre compte depuis l'admin.", false);
        return false;
      }
      const { error: e1 } = await supabase.from("profiles").delete().eq("id", r.id);
      if (e1) {
        toast("Suppression échouée", false);
        return false;
      }
      // Le compte n'est supprimé que s'il ne reste plus aucun profil pour cet utilisateur.
      const remaining = rows.filter((x) => x.user_id === r.user_id && x.id !== r.id).length;
      if (remaining === 0) {
        const { error: e2 } = await supabase.from("accounts").delete().eq("user_id", r.user_id);
        if (e2) toast("Profil supprimé, mais le compte n'a pas pu l'être", false);
      }
      setRows((prev) => prev.filter((x) => x.id !== r.id));
      toast(`@${r.slug} supprimé`);
      return true;
    },
    [meId, rows, toast]
  );

  const exportCsv = useCallback(
    (list: AdminRow[]) => {
      const header = ["pseudo", "nom_affiche", "pays", "plan", "role", "theme", "liens", "clics", "vues", "inscrit_le"];
      const lines = list.map((r) =>
        [r.slug, r.display_name, r.country ?? "", r.plan, r.role, r.theme_id, r.links_count, r.clicks_total, r.views, fmtDate(r.created_at)]
          .map((v) => `"${String(v).replace(/"/g, '""')}"`)
          .join(",")
      );
      const blob = new Blob(["\uFEFF" + [header.join(","), ...lines].join("\n")], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8" });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement("a");
      a.href = url;
      a.download = `ndeletik-utilisateurs-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.csv`;
      a.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      toast(`Export CSV : ${list.length} ligne(s)`);
    },
    [toast]
  );

  const value = useMemo(
    () => ({ status, meId, rows, toggleRole, deleteUser, exportCsv }),
    [status, meId, rows, toggleRole, deleteUser, exportCsv]
  );

  return <AdminCtx.Provider value={value}>{children}</AdminCtx.Provider>;
}

===== FILE: src/components/admin/AdminShell.tsx =====

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { Shield } from "lucide-react";
import { clsx } from "clsx";
import { Wordmark } from "@/components/landing/ui";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/Badge";
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/Card";
import { buttonClass } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { isActive } from "@/components/dashboard/nav";
import { useAdmin } from "./AdminProvider";
import { ADMIN_NAV, BACK_ITEM } from "./nav";

/** Même cadre que le dashboard, avec un repère « Admin » : menu latéral (ordinateur), onglets (mobile). */
export function AdminShell({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { status, rows } = useAdmin();
  const pathname = usePathname();

  if (status === "loading") return <Skeleton />;
  if (status === "denied") return <Denied />;

  const mobileItems = [...ADMIN_NAV, BACK_ITEM];

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-bg text-ink">
      <aside className="fixed inset-y-0 left-0 z-40 hidden w-64 flex-col border-r border-line bg-surface lg:flex">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-5 pb-4 pt-5">
          <Link href="/admin" aria-label="Ndeletik, administration">
            <Wordmark />
          </Link>
          <Badge tone="brand">
            <Shield size={10} aria-hidden /> Admin
          </Badge>
        </div>

        <nav aria-label="Navigation de l'administration" className="mt-2 flex-1 space-y-1 px-3">
          {ADMIN_NAV.map((item) => {
            const active = isActive(item, pathname);
            return (
              <Link
                key={item.href}
                href={item.href}
                aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
                className={clsx(
                  "relative flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm transition-colors",
                  active ? "bg-fractal-ocre/10 font-semibold text-fractal-terra" : "font-medium text-muted hover:bg-soft hover:text-ink"
                )}
              >
                {active && (
                  <span className="absolute inset-y-2 left-0 w-1 rounded-r-full bg-gradient-to-b from-fractal-or via-fractal-ocre to-fractal-terra" />
                )}
                <item.icon size={18} aria-hidden />
                {item.label}
                {item.href === "/admin/users" && <span className="ml-auto text-xs text-muted">{rows.length}</span>}
              </Link>
            );
          })}
        </nav>

        <div className="border-t border-line p-3">
          <Link
            href={BACK_ITEM.href}
            className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-muted transition-colors hover:bg-soft hover:text-ink"
          >
            <BACK_ITEM.icon size={18} aria-hidden /> {BACK_ITEM.label}
          </Link>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <div className="lg:pl-64">
        <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 border-b border-line bg-bg/85 backdrop-blur">
          <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-6xl items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 sm:px-6">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 lg:hidden">
              <Wordmark />
              <Badge tone="brand">Admin</Badge>
            </div>
            <p className="ml-auto text-sm text-muted">
              {new Date().toLocaleDateString("fr-FR", { weekday: "long", day: "numeric", month: "long", year: "numeric" })}
            </p>
          </div>
        </header>
        <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-6xl px-4 pb-28 pt-6 sm:px-6 sm:pt-8 lg:pb-12">{children}</main>
      </div>

      <nav
        aria-label="Navigation de l'administration"
        className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-40 border-t border-line bg-surface/95 backdrop-blur lg:hidden"
        style={{ paddingBottom: "env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px)" }}
      >
        <ul className="mx-auto grid max-w-lg grid-cols-3">
          {mobileItems.map((item) => {
            const active = isActive(item, pathname);
            return (
              <li key={item.href}>
                <Link
                  href={item.href}
                  aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
                  className={clsx(
                    "relative flex h-16 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors",
                    active ? "text-fractal-terra" : "text-muted"
                  )}
                >
                  {active && (
                    <span className="absolute inset-x-6 top-0 h-0.5 rounded-b-full bg-gradient-to-r from-fractal-or via-fractal-ocre to-fractal-terra" />
                  )}
                  <item.icon size={20} aria-hidden />
                  {item.short}
                </Link>
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      </nav>
    </div>
  );
}

function Denied() {
  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-bg p-6">
      <Card className="max-w-md text-center">
        <span className="mx-auto mb-4 flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-fractal-ocre/10 text-fractal-ocre">
          <Shield size={22} aria-hidden />
        </span>
        <h1 className="font-display text-xl font-extrabold">Accès réservé aux administrateurs</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted">Ton compte n'a pas les droits pour afficher cette page.</p>
        <Link href="/dashboard" className={buttonClass({ size: "lg", className: "mt-5" })}>
          Retour à mon dashboard
        </Link>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

function Skeleton() {
  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-bg" role="status" aria-label="Chargement de l'administration">
      <div className="fixed inset-y-0 left-0 hidden w-64 border-r border-line bg-surface p-5 lg:block">
        <div className="h-7 w-32 animate-pulse rounded bg-line/70" />
        <div className="mt-8 space-y-2">
          <div className="h-10 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-line/50" />
          <div className="h-10 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-line/50" />
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="lg:pl-64">
        <div className="h-16 border-b border-line" />
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl space-y-4 px-4 pt-8 sm:px-6">
          <div className="h-9 w-56 animate-pulse rounded bg-line/70" />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 md:grid-cols-4">
            {[0, 1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
              <div key={i} className="h-28 animate-pulse rounded-2xl bg-line/60" />
            ))}
          </div>
          <div className="h-80 animate-pulse rounded-2xl bg-line/60" />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/components/admin/WorldMap.tsx =====

"use client";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { geoEquirectangular, geoPath } from "d3-geo";
import { feature } from "topojson-client";

export const PLAN_COLORS: Record<string, string> = {
  free: "#F9A825",
  pro: "#FF6A1A",
  business: "#C1440E",
};

export const PLAN_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  free: "Gratuit",
  pro: "Pro",
  business: "Business",
};

export type MapMarker = {
  lat: number;
  lon: number;
  plan: string;
  count: number;
  country: string;
};

const WIDTH = 800;
const HEIGHT = 400;

type HoverState = {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  marker: MapMarker;
};

export function WorldMap({ markers }: { markers: MapMarker[] }) {
  const [geographies, setGeographies] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  const [hover, setHover] = useState<HoverState | null>(null);
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/world-atlas@2/countries-110m.json")
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then((topo: any) => {
        const fc: any = feature(topo, topo.objects.countries);
        setGeographies(fc.features);
      })
      .catch(() => setFailed(true));
  }, []);

  const projection = useMemo(
    () =>
      geoEquirectangular().fitExtent(
        [
          [0, 0],
          [WIDTH, HEIGHT],
        ],
        { type: "Sphere" } as any
      ),
    []
  );
  const path = useMemo(() => geoPath(projection), [projection]);

  const handleMarkerEnter = (e: React.MouseEvent<SVGCircleElement>, marker: MapMarker) => {
    const rect = containerRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (!rect) return;
    setHover({
      x: e.clientX - rect.left,
      y: e.clientY - rect.top,
      marker,
    });
  };

  const handleMarkerMove = (e: React.MouseEvent<SVGCircleElement>, marker: MapMarker) => {
    const rect = containerRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (!rect) return;
    setHover({
      x: e.clientX - rect.left,
      y: e.clientY - rect.top,
      marker,
    });
  };

  const handleMarkerLeave = () => setHover(null);

  if (failed) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-64 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-soft text-sm text-muted">
        Carte indisponible (connexion requise pour charger le fond de carte).
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div ref={containerRef} className="relative">
      <svg
        viewBox={`0 0 ${WIDTH} ${HEIGHT}`}
        className="w-full h-auto"
        role="img"
        aria-label="Carte mondiale des abonnés"
      >
        <path
          d={path({ type: "Sphere" } as any) ?? ""}
          fill="#FBF7F1"
          stroke="var(--color-line)"
          strokeWidth={0.6}
        />
        {geographies.length === 0 ? (
          <rect
            x="0"
            y="0"
            width={WIDTH}
            height={HEIGHT}
            fill="var(--color-soft)"
            className="animate-pulse"
          />
        ) : (
          geographies.map((g: any, i: number) => (
            <path key={g.id ?? i} d={path(g) ?? ""} fill="#EDE4D8" stroke="#FFFFFF" strokeWidth={0.5} />
          ))
        )}
        {markers.map((m, i) => {
          const xy = projection([m.lon, m.lat]);
          if (!xy) return null;
          const [x, y] = xy;
          const r = 3 + Math.min(7, Math.sqrt(m.count) * 1.4);
          const color = PLAN_COLORS[m.plan] ?? PLAN_COLORS.free;
          const isActive = hover?.marker === m;
          return (
            <g key={i}>
              <circle
                cx={x}
                cy={y}
                r={r * (isActive ? 2.5 : 2)}
                fill={color}
                opacity={isActive ? 0.3 : 0.18}
                className="transition-all duration-200"
              />
              <circle
                cx={x}
                cy={y}
                r={r * (isActive ? 1.3 : 1)}
                fill={color}
                opacity={0.9}
                stroke="#FFFFFF"
                strokeWidth={1.2}
                className="cursor-pointer transition-all duration-200"
                onMouseEnter={(e) => handleMarkerEnter(e, m)}
                onMouseMove={(e) => handleMarkerMove(e, m)}
                onMouseLeave={handleMarkerLeave}
              />
            </g>
          );
        })}
      </svg>

      {/* Tooltip custom stylé YEKOLA */}
      {hover && (
        <div
          className="pointer-events-none absolute z-10 rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface shadow-soft px-3 py-2.5 min-w-[180px]"
          style={{
            left: `${hover.x + 12}px`,
            top: `${hover.y + 12}px`,
            transform: hover.x > 600 ? "translateX(-110%)" : "none",
          }}
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
            <span
              className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full"
              style={{ background: PLAN_COLORS[hover.marker.plan] }}
            />
            <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-ink">{hover.marker.country}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
            <span className="text-muted">Plan :</span>
            <span
              className="rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 font-medium"
              style={{
                background: `${PLAN_COLORS[hover.marker.plan]}20`,
                color: PLAN_COLORS[hover.marker.plan],
              }}
            >
              {PLAN_LABELS[hover.marker.plan]}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs mt-1">
            <span className="text-muted">Abonnés :</span>
            <span className="font-bold text-ink">{hover.marker.count}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Légende */}
      <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 text-xs text-muted">
        {(Object.keys(PLAN_COLORS) as string[]).map((p) => (
          <span key={p} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full" style={{ background: PLAN_COLORS[p] }} />
            {PLAN_LABELS[p]}
          </span>
        ))}
        <span className="ml-auto text-[11px]">Survole un point pour le détail</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
===== FILE: src/components/admin/bits.tsx =====

import { Crown } from "lucide-react";
import { clsx } from "clsx";
import { PLAN_COLORS, PLAN_LABELS } from "@/lib/admin-types";

export function PlanDot({ plan }: { plan: string }) {
  return (
    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium">
      <span className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full" style={{ background: PLAN_COLORS[plan] ?? PLAN_COLORS.free }} />
      {PLAN_LABELS[plan] ?? plan}
    </span>
  );
}

export function RoleBadge({ role }: { role: string }) {
  const admin = role === "admin";
  return (
    <span
      className={clsx(
        "inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold",
        admin ? "bg-fractal-ocre/10 text-fractal-terra" : "bg-soft text-muted"
      )}
    >
      {admin && <Crown size={11} aria-hidden />}
      {admin ? "Admin" : "Utilisateur"}
    </span>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/components/admin/charts.tsx =====

"use client";

import { Component, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { PLAN_COLORS, PLAN_LABELS, PLAN_ORDER } from "@/lib/admin-types";

export class MapBoundary extends Component<{ children: ReactNode }, { failed: boolean }> {
  state = { failed: false };
  static getDerivedStateFromError() {
    return { failed: true };
  }
  render() {
    if (this.state.failed) {
      return (
        <div className="flex h-64 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-soft text-sm text-muted">
          Carte indisponible (npm i d3-geo topojson-client).
        </div>
      );
    }
    return this.props.children;
  }
}

export function Sparkline({ data }: { data: number[] }) {
  const max = Math.max(1, ...data);
  const pts = data
    .map((v, i) => `${(i / Math.max(1, data.length - 1)) * 100},${26 - (v / max) * 20 - 3}`)
    .join(" ");
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 100 30" className="h-8 w-full" preserveAspectRatio="none" aria-hidden>
      <polyline points={pts} fill="none" stroke="#FF6A1A" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" />
    </svg>
  );
}

export function PlanDonut({ counts }: { counts: Record<string, number> }) {
  const total = Math.max(1, PLAN_ORDER.reduce((s, p) => s + (counts[p] ?? 0), 0));
  const R = 40;
  const C = 2 * Math.PI * R;
  let acc = 0;
  const segs = PLAN_ORDER.map((p) => {
    const frac = (counts[p] ?? 0) / total;
    const s = { p, frac, offset: acc };
    acc += frac;
    return s;
  });
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-5">
      <div className="relative h-32 w-32 shrink-0">
        <svg viewBox="0 0 100 100" className="h-full w-full -rotate-90" role="img" aria-label="Répartition des comptes par plan">
          <circle cx="50" cy="50" r={R} fill="none" stroke="var(--color-soft)" strokeWidth="13" />
          {segs.map(
            (s) =>
              s.frac > 0 && (
                <circle
                  key={s.p}
                  cx="50"
                  cy="50"
                  r={R}
                  fill="none"
                  stroke={PLAN_COLORS[s.p]}
                  strokeWidth="13"
                  strokeDasharray={`${C * s.frac} ${C}`}
                  strokeDashoffset={-C * s.offset}
                />
              )
          )}
        </svg>
        <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
          <span className="font-display text-xl font-extrabold">{total}</span>
          <span className="text-[10px] text-muted">comptes</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <ul className="flex-1 space-y-2 text-sm">
        {segs.map((s) => (
          <li key={s.p} className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full" style={{ background: PLAN_COLORS[s.p] }} />
            <span className="text-muted">{PLAN_LABELS[s.p]}</span>
            <span className="ml-auto font-semibold">{counts[s.p] ?? 0}</span>
            <span className="w-10 text-right text-xs text-muted">{Math.round(((counts[s.p] ?? 0) / total) * 100)}%</span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/components/admin/stats.ts =====

import { getCoords } from "@/lib/countryCoords";
import { PLAN_ORDER, type AdminRow } from "@/lib/admin-types";

export type MapMarker = { lat: number; lon: number; plan: string; count: number; country: string };

/** Agrégats de la vue d'ensemble (calculés côté navigateur, comme avant). */
export function computeStats(rows: AdminRow[]) {
  const totals = rows.reduce(
    (acc, r) => ({
      users: acc.users + 1,
      links: acc.links + r.links_count,
      clicks: acc.clicks + r.clicks_total,
      views: acc.views + r.views,
    }),
    { users: 0, links: 0, clicks: 0, views: 0 }
  );

  const planCounts: Record<string, number> = { free: 0, pro: 0, business: 0 };
  rows.forEach((r) => {
    planCounts[r.plan] = (planCounts[r.plan] ?? 0) + 1;
  });

  const now = Date.now();
  const buckets: number[] = Array(8).fill(0);
  rows.forEach((r) => {
    const w = Math.floor((now - new Date(r.created_at).getTime()) / 604800000);
    if (w >= 0 && w < 8) buckets[7 - w]++;
  });

  const countryAgg = new Map<string, { country: string; total: number; plans: Record<string, number>; lat: number; lon: number }>();
  let unlocated = 0;
  rows.forEach((r) => {
    const coords = getCoords(r.country);
    if (!coords) {
      unlocated++;
      return;
    }
    const key = r.country!.trim().toLowerCase();
    const e = countryAgg.get(key) ?? { country: r.country!.trim(), total: 0, plans: {}, lat: coords[0], lon: coords[1] };
    e.total++;
    e.plans[r.plan] = (e.plans[r.plan] ?? 0) + 1;
    countryAgg.set(key, e);
  });

  const markers: MapMarker[] = [];
  countryAgg.forEach((c) => {
    PLAN_ORDER.forEach((p, idx) => {
      if ((c.plans[p] ?? 0) > 0) {
        markers.push({ lat: c.lat + (idx - 1) * 1.6, lon: c.lon + (idx - 1) * 1.6, plan: p, count: c.plans[p], country: c.country });
      }
    });
  });

  const topCountries = [...countryAgg.values()].sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total).slice(0, 5);
  return { totals, planCounts, buckets, markers, unlocated, topCountries };
}

===== FILE: src/components/dashboard/AddBlockModal.tsx =====

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { Field, inputClass } from "@/components/ui/Field";
import { Modal } from "@/components/ui/Modal";
import { detectIcon } from "@/lib/detect-icon";
import { normalizeUrl } from "./blockMeta";
import { validUrl } from "./LinkItem";
import { useToast } from "./Toast";

const PRESETS = [
  { label: "Site web", title: "Mon site web", prefix: "" },
  { label: "WhatsApp", title: "Mon WhatsApp", prefix: "wa.me/" },
  { label: "Instagram", title: "Mon Instagram", prefix: "instagram.com/" },
  { label: "TikTok", title: "Mon TikTok", prefix: "tiktok.com/@" },
  { label: "YouTube", title: "Ma chaîne YouTube", prefix: "youtube.com/@" },
  { label: "E-mail", title: "Me contacter", prefix: "" },
];

export type BlockPreset = { title: string; url: string; icon?: string };

type Props = {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onAdd: (title: string, url: string, icon?: string) => Promise<boolean>;
  initial?: BlockPreset;
};

export function AddBlockModal({ open, onClose, onAdd, initial }: Props) {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [url, setUrl] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    setTitle(initial?.title ?? "");
    setUrl(initial?.url ?? "");
  }, [open, initial]);

  const submit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!title.trim() || !url.trim() || busy) return;
    if (!validUrl(url)) return toast("Adresse invalide (ex : https://… ou wa.me/…)", false);
    setBusy(true);
    const icon = initial?.icon && initial.icon !== "link" ? initial.icon : detectIcon(normalizeUrl(url));
    const ok = await onAdd(title.trim(), url.trim(), icon);
    setBusy(false);
    if (ok) onClose();
  };

  return (
    <Modal open={open} onClose={onClose} title="Ajouter un lien" description="Il apparaîtra tout de suite sur ta page.">
      <form onSubmit={submit} className="grid gap-4">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2" aria-label="Modèles">
          {PRESETS.map((p) => (
            <button
              type="button"
              key={p.label}
              onClick={() => {
                setTitle(p.title);
                setUrl(p.prefix);
              }}
              className="rounded-full border border-line px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-muted transition-colors hover:border-fractal-ocre hover:text-fractal-terra"
            >
              {p.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        <Field label="Titre du bouton">
          <input
            data-autofocus={initial?.url ? undefined : ""}
            value={title}
            onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Ex : Mon WhatsApp"
            className={inputClass}
          />
        </Field>
        <Field label="Adresse du lien" hint="Lien, numéro WhatsApp (wa.me/…) ou e-mail.">
          <input
            data-autofocus={initial?.url ? "" : undefined}
            value={url}
            onChange={(e) => setUrl(e.target.value)}
            inputMode="url"
            placeholder="https://…"
            className={inputClass}
          />
        </Field>

        <Button type="submit" size="lg" full disabled={busy || !title.trim() || !url.trim()}>
          {busy ? "Ajout…" : "Ajouter le lien"}
        </Button>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/components/dashboard/AnalyticsChart.tsx =====

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabaseClient";

type ClickData = { date: string; count: number };

type Props = {
  profile: { id: string };
  linkId?: string; // Si fourni, filtre sur ce lien
};

export function AnalyticsChart({ profile, linkId }: Props) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<ClickData[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      const thirtyDaysAgo = new Date();
      thirtyDaysAgo.setDate(thirtyDaysAgo.getDate() - 30);

      let query = supabase
        .from("link_clicks")
        .select("created_at")
        .eq("profile_id", profile.id)
        .gte("created_at", thirtyDaysAgo.toISOString())
        .order("created_at", { ascending: true });

      if (linkId) {
        query = query.eq("link_id", linkId);
      }

      const { data: clicks } = await query;

      // Grouper par jour
      const dayMap = new Map<string, number>();
      (clicks ?? []).forEach((c: any) => {
        const day = new Date(c.created_at).toISOString().split("T")[0];
        dayMap.set(day, (dayMap.get(day) ?? 0) + 1);
      });

      // Remplir les jours vides
      const result: ClickData[] = [];
      for (let i = 29; i >= 0; i--) {
        const date = new Date();
        date.setDate(date.getDate() - i);
        const key = date.toISOString().split("T")[0];
        result.push({ date: key, count: dayMap.get(key) ?? 0 });
      }

      setData(result);
      setLoading(false);
    })();
  }, [profile.id, linkId]);

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="h-48 bg-soft rounded-xl animate-pulse" />;
  }

  const max = Math.max(1, ...data.map((d) => d.count));
  const total = data.reduce((s, d) => s + d.count, 0);

  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-5 shadow-soft">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold">Clics (30 derniers jours)</h3>
        <span className="text-2xl font-display font-bold text-fractal-ocre">{total}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="flex items-end gap-1 h-32">
        {data.map((d, i) => (
          <motion.div
            key={d.date}
            initial={{ height: 0 }}
            animate={{ height: `${(d.count / max) * 100}%` }}
            transition={{ delay: i * 0.02, duration: 0.3 }}
            className="flex-1 bg-gradient-to-t from-fractal-ocre to-fractal-terra rounded-t hover:opacity-80 transition-opacity cursor-pointer group relative"
            title={`${d.date}: ${d.count} clics`}
          >
            <div className="absolute -top-8 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bg-ink text-bg text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity whitespace-nowrap pointer-events-none">
              {d.count}
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-muted mt-2">
        <span>{new Date(data[0]?.date).toLocaleDateString("fr-FR", { day: "numeric", month: "short" })}</span>
        <span>{new Date(data[data.length - 1]?.date).toLocaleDateString("fr-FR", { day: "numeric", month: "short" })}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
===== FILE: src/components/dashboard/BlocksEditor.tsx =====

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { ArrowDown, ArrowUp, Plus, Save, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabaseClient";
import { useToast } from "./Toast";
import { ConfirmDialog } from "@/components/ui/ConfirmDialog";
import { LINK_ICONS } from "@/components/profile/icons";
import type { Block, Social, SocialType } from "@/lib/themes/types";

const SOCIAL_TYPES: SocialType[] = [
  "instagram", "tiktok", "youtube", "facebook", "whatsapp",
  "x", "linkedin", "telegram", "pinterest", "email",
];

const ICON_KEYS = Object.keys(LINK_ICONS);

const BLOCK_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  about: "À propos",
  stats: "Chiffres clés",
  faq: "FAQ",
  newsletter: "Newsletter",
  offer: "Offre / service",
  contact: "Contact / adresse",
  hint: "Appel à l'action",
  separator: "Séparateur",
};

const inputCls =
  "w-full rounded-xl border border-line bg-bg px-3 py-2 text-sm text-ink focus:outline-none focus:border-fractal-ocre transition-colors";

function newBlock(type: string): Block {
  switch (type) {
    case "about":      return { type: "about", title: "À propos", text: "" };
    case "stats":      return { type: "stats", items: [{ value: "100+", label: "Clients" }] };
    case "faq":        return { type: "faq", items: [{ q: "Question ?", a: "Réponse." }] };
    case "newsletter": return { type: "newsletter", title: "Rejoins ma newsletter", subtitle: "" };
    case "offer":      return { type: "offer", title: "Mon offre", desc: "", tag: "", cta: "Réserver", icon: "gift" };
    case "contact":    return { type: "contact", title: "Où me trouver", text: "", icon: "pin" };
    case "hint":       return { type: "hint", label: "Clique sur mes liens", emoji: "👆" };
    default:           return { type: "separator", label: "Conecte-se" };
  }
}

type Props = {
  profileId: string;
  initialBlocks: Block[];
  initialSocials: Social[];
};

export function SectionsEditor({ profileId, initialBlocks, initialSocials }: Props) {
  const { toast, node: toastNode } = useToast();
  const [blocks, setBlocks] = useState<Block[]>(initialBlocks);
  const [socials, setSocials] = useState<Social[]>(initialSocials);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [deleteIndex, setDeleteIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const patch = (i: number, p: Partial<Block>) =>
    setBlocks((prev) => prev.map((b, j) => (j === i ? ({ ...b, ...p } as Block) : b)));

  const move = (i: number, dir: -1 | 1) =>
    setBlocks((prev) => {
      const next = [...prev];
      const j = i + dir;
      if (j < 0 || j >= next.length) return prev;
      [next[i], next[j]] = [next[j], next[i]];
      return next;
    });

  const save = async () => {
    setSaving(true);
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from("profiles")
      .update({
        blocks,
        socials: socials.filter((s) => s.url.trim() !== ""),
      })
      .eq("id", profileId);
    setSaving(false);
    if (error) return toast(`Enregistrement échoué : ${error.message}`, false);
    toast("Sections enregistrées");
  };

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 py-8 space-y-8">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold">Sections de ma page</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted">Réseaux sociaux, offres, contact, FAQ… (10 blocs max)</p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={save}
          disabled={saving || blocks.length > 10}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-fractal-ocre hover:bg-fractal-terra px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
        >
          <Save size={16} /> {saving ? "Enregistrement…" : "Enregistrer"}
        </button>
      </div>

      {blocks.length > 10 && (
        <p className="rounded-xl border border-fractal-terra/30 bg-fractal-terra/10 p-3 text-sm text-fractal-terra">
          Maximum 10 blocs : supprime-en un pour pouvoir enregistrer.
        </p>
      )}

      {/* ---------- Réseaux sociaux ---------- */}
      <section className="rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-5 shadow-soft">
        <h2 className="mb-3 text-sm font-semibold">Réseaux sociaux ({socials.length}/12)</h2>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {socials.map((s, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="flex gap-2">
              <select
                value={s.type}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setSocials((prev) => prev.map((x, j) => (j === i ? { ...x, type: e.target.value as SocialType } : x)))
                }
                className={`${inputCls} w-36`}
                aria-label="Type de réseau"
              >
                {SOCIAL_TYPES.map((t) => (
                  <option key={t} value={t}>{t}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
              <input
                value={s.url}
                onChange={(e) => setSocials((prev) => prev.map((x, j) => (j === i ? { ...x, url: e.target.value } : x)))}
                placeholder="https://instagram.com/…"
                className={inputCls}
              />
              <button
                onClick={() => setSocials((prev) => prev.filter((_, j) => j !== i))}
                aria-label="Retirer"
                className="p-2 rounded-lg text-muted hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-600 transition-colors"
              >
                <Trash2 size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        {socials.length < 12 && (
          <button
            onClick={() => setSocials((prev) => [...prev, { type: "instagram", url: "" }])}
            className="mt-3 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-line px-4 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted hover:border-fractal-ocre hover:text-fractal-terra transition-colors"
          >
            <Plus size={14} /> Ajouter un réseau
          </button>
        )}
      </section>

      {/* ---------- Blocs ---------- */}
      <section className="space-y-4">
        <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold">Blocs de contenu ({blocks.length}/10)</h2>

        {blocks.map((b, i) => (
          <div key={i} className="rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-4 shadow-soft space-y-3">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="rounded-full bg-soft px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-fractal-terra">
                {BLOCK_LABELS[b.type]}
              </span>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                <button onClick={() => move(i, -1)} aria-label="Monter" className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-muted hover:bg-soft transition-colors"><ArrowUp size={14} /></button>
                <button onClick={() => move(i, 1)} aria-label="Descendre" className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-muted hover:bg-soft transition-colors"><ArrowDown size={14} /></button>
                <button onClick={() => setDeleteIndex(i)} aria-label="Supprimer" className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-muted hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-600 transition-colors"><Trash2 size={14} /></button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {b.type === "about" && (
              <>
                <input value={b.title} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { title: e.target.value })} placeholder="Titre" className={inputCls} />
                <textarea rows={3} value={b.text} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { text: e.target.value })} placeholder="Ton histoire…" className={inputCls} />
              </>
            )}

            {b.type === "stats" && (
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {b.items.map((s, k) => (
                  <div key={k} className="flex gap-2">
                    <input value={s.value} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { items: b.items.map((x, j) => (j === k ? { ...x, value: e.target.value } : x)) })} placeholder="500+" className={`${inputCls} w-24`} />
                    <input value={s.label} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { items: b.items.map((x, j) => (j === k ? { ...x, label: e.target.value } : x)) })} placeholder="Clients" className={inputCls} />
                  </div>
                ))}
                {b.items.length < 4 && (
                  <button onClick={() => patch(i, { items: [...b.items, { value: "", label: "" }] })} className="text-xs text-fractal-terra hover:text-fractal-ocre">+ Ajouter un chiffre</button>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {b.type === "faq" && (
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {b.items.map((f, k) => (
                  <div key={k} className="space-y-1 rounded-xl bg-soft p-2">
                    <input value={f.q} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { items: b.items.map((x, j) => (j === k ? { ...x, q: e.target.value } : x)) })} placeholder="Question" className={inputCls} />
                    <input value={f.a} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { items: b.items.map((x, j) => (j === k ? { ...x, a: e.target.value } : x)) })} placeholder="Réponse" className={inputCls} />
                  </div>
                ))}
                {b.items.length < 10 && (
                  <button onClick={() => patch(i, { items: [...b.items, { q: "", a: "" }] })} className="text-xs text-fractal-terra hover:text-fractal-ocre">+ Ajouter une question</button>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {b.type === "newsletter" && (
              <>
                <input value={b.title} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { title: e.target.value })} placeholder="Titre" className={inputCls} />
                <input value={b.subtitle ?? ""} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { subtitle: e.target.value })} placeholder="Sous-titre" className={inputCls} />
              </>
            )}

            {b.type === "offer" && (
              <>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <input value={b.tag ?? ""} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { tag: e.target.value })} placeholder="Badge (ex : Aulas Presenciais)" className={`${inputCls} w-1/2`} />
                  <select value={b.icon ?? ""} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { icon: e.target.value || undefined })} className={`${inputCls} w-1/2`} aria-label="Icône">
                    <option value="">— Icône —</option>
                    {ICON_KEYS.map((k) => <option key={k} value={k}>{k}</option>)}
                  </select>
                </div>
                <input value={b.title} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { title: e.target.value })} placeholder="Titre de l'offre" className={inputCls} />
                <input value={b.desc ?? ""} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { desc: e.target.value })} placeholder="Description courte" className={inputCls} />
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <input value={b.dateLabel ?? ""} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { dateLabel: e.target.value })} placeholder="Widget date : libellé" className={`${inputCls} w-1/3`} />
                  <input value={b.dateValue ?? ""} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { dateValue: e.target.value })} placeholder="Valeur" className={`${inputCls} w-1/3`} />
                  <input value={b.emoji ?? ""} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { emoji: e.target.value })} placeholder="Ou emoji" className={`${inputCls} w-1/3`} />

              </>
            )}

            {b.type === "contact" && (
              <>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <select value={b.icon ?? ""} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { icon: e.target.value || undefined })} className={`${inputCls} w-1/2`} aria-label="Icône">
                    <option value="">— Icône —</option>
                    {ICON_KEYS.map((k) => <option key={k} value={k}>{k}</option>)}
                  </select>
                  <input value={b.emoji ?? ""} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { emoji: e.target.value })} placeholder="Ou emoji (ex : 💈)" className={`${inputCls} w-1/2`} />
