                <input value={b.text ?? ""} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { text: e.target.value })} placeholder="Adresse / texte" className={inputCls} />
                <input value={b.url ?? ""} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { url: e.target.value })} placeholder="Lien (optionnel, ex : itinéraire)" className={inputCls} />
              </>
            )}

            {b.type === "hint" && (
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <input value={b.emoji ?? ""} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { emoji: e.target.value })} placeholder="Emoji (ex : 👆)" className={`${inputCls} w-24`} />
                <input value={b.label} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { label: e.target.value })} placeholder="Texte (ex : Clique sur mes icônes)" className={inputCls} />
              </div>
            )}

            {b.type === "separator" && (
              <input value={b.label ?? ""} onChange={(e) => patch(i, { label: e.target.value })} placeholder="Libellé (ex : Conecte-se)" className={inputCls} />
            )}
          </div>
        ))}

        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {Object.keys(BLOCK_LABELS).map((t) => (
            <button
              key={t}
              onClick={() => setBlocks((prev) => (prev.length >= 10 ? prev : [...prev, newBlock(t)]))}
              disabled={blocks.length >= 10}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-line px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-muted hover:border-fractal-ocre hover:text-fractal-terra transition-colors disabled:opacity-40"
            >
              <Plus size={12} /> {BLOCK_LABELS[t]}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>

      <ConfirmDialog
        open={deleteIndex !== null}
        title="Supprimer ce bloc ?"
        message="Le bloc sera retiré de ta page publique."
        confirmLabel="Supprimer"
        danger
        onConfirm={() => {
          if (deleteIndex !== null) setBlocks((prev) => prev.filter((_, j) => j !== deleteIndex));
          setDeleteIndex(null);
        }}
        onCancel={() => setDeleteIndex(null)}
      />
      {toastNode}
    </div>
  );
}
===== FILE: src/components/dashboard/CropModal.tsx =====

"use client";
import { useState, useCallback } from "react";
import Cropper from "react-easy-crop";
import { X, ZoomIn, ZoomOut } from "lucide-react";
import { getCroppedImg } from "@/lib/cropImage";

type Props = {
  imageSrc: string;
  aspect: number;
  onConfirm: (blob: Blob) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
};

export function CropModal({ imageSrc, aspect, onConfirm, onClose }: Props) {
  const [crop, setCrop] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
  const [croppedAreaPixels, setCroppedAreaPixels] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const onCropComplete = useCallback((_: any, croppedPixels: any) => {
    setCroppedAreaPixels(croppedPixels);
  }, []);

  const handleConfirm = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const blob = await getCroppedImg(imageSrc, croppedAreaPixels);
      onConfirm(blob);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center bg-ink/80 p-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
      <div className="w-full max-w-lg bg-surface rounded-2xl shadow-soft overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[90vh] border border-line">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-4 border-b border-line">
          <h3 className="text-base font-display font-bold text-ink">Ajuster l'image</h3>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1 rounded-md hover:bg-soft text-muted">
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="relative bg-ink h-[300px] sm:h-[350px]">
          <Cropper
            image={imageSrc}
            crop={crop}
            zoom={zoom}
            aspect={aspect}
            onCropChange={setCrop}
            onZoomChange={setZoom}
            onCropComplete={onCropComplete}
          />
        </div>
        <div className="px-5 py-4 space-y-4 bg-soft">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <ZoomOut size={16} className="text-muted" />
            <input
              type="range"
              min={1}
              max={3}
              step={0.1}
              value={zoom}
              onChange={(e) => setZoom(Number(e.target.value))}
              className="flex-1 h-1.5 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer"
            />
            <ZoomIn size={16} className="text-muted" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
            <button onClick={onClose} className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium text-muted hover:bg-surface transition-colors">
              Annuler
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={handleConfirm}
              disabled={loading}
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-fractal-ocre hover:bg-fractal-terra hover:opacity-90 text-white text-sm font-semibold disabled:opacity-50 shadow-soft transition-all"
            >
              {loading ? "Traitement..." : "Confirmer"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
===== FILE: src/components/dashboard/DashboardProvider.tsx =====

"use client";

import {
  createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type ReactNode,
} from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabaseClient";
import { toProfile } from "@/lib/profile-mapper";
import { isReserved } from "@/lib/reserved";
import { ENTITLEMENTS } from "@/lib/entitlements";
import { canCreateProfile, profileLimitMessage, PROFILE_LIMITS } from "@/lib/multi-profiles";
import { PROFILE_COOKIE, type DbLink, type DbProfile } from "@/lib/dashboard-types";
import type { Plan } from "@/lib/plans";
import type { Profile as PublicProfile } from "@/lib/themes/types";
import { normalizeUrl } from "./blockMeta";
import { ToastProvider, useToast } from "./Toast";

/** Champs modifiables du profil (clés camelCase côté interface, converties en colonnes SQL à l'enregistrement). */
export type ProfilePatch = Partial<{
  slug: string;
  displayName: string;
  headline: string | undefined;
  bio: string | undefined;
  avatarUrl: string | undefined;
  coverUrl: string | undefined;
}>;

const COLUMNS: Record<keyof ProfilePatch, string> = {
  slug: "slug",
  displayName: "display_name",
  headline: "headline",
  bio: "bio",
  avatarUrl: "avatar_url",
  coverUrl: "cover_url",
};

type Ctx = {
  ready: boolean;
  userId: string | null;
  plan: Plan;
  profiles: DbProfile[];
  profileId: string | null;
  row: DbProfile | null;
  /** Profil au format du rendu public, recalculé à chaque modification des liens (aperçu réellement « en direct »). */
  profile: PublicProfile | null;
  links: DbLink[];
  setLinks: (links: DbLink[]) => void;
  publicUrl: string;
  activeLinks: number;
  maxLinks: number | null;
  profileLimit: number;
  canCreateProfile: boolean;
  selectProfile: (id: string) => void;
  createNewProfile: () => Promise<void>;
  addLink: (title: string, url: string, icon?: string) => Promise<boolean>;
  updateLink: (id: string, updates: Partial<DbLink>) => Promise<void>;
  deleteLink: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
  persistOrder: () => Promise<void>;
  saveProfile: (patch: ProfilePatch) => Promise<boolean>;
  copyUrl: () => Promise<void>;
  shareUrl: () => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
};

const DashboardCtx = createContext<Ctx | null>(null);

export function useDashboard(): Ctx {
  const ctx = useContext(DashboardCtx);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error("useDashboard doit être utilisé dans <DashboardProvider>");
  return ctx;
}

const byCreated = (a: DbProfile, b: DbProfile) => (a.created_at ?? "").localeCompare(b.created_at ?? "");

function readCookie(): string | null {
  const m = document.cookie.match(new RegExp(`(?:^|; )${PROFILE_COOKIE}=([^;]*)`));
  return m ? decodeURIComponent(m[1]) : null;
}

export function DashboardProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <ToastProvider>
      <Inner>{children}</Inner>
    </ToastProvider>
  );
}

function Inner({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { toast } = useToast();

  const [userId, setUserId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [plan, setPlan] = useState<Plan>("free");
  const [profiles, setProfiles] = useState<DbProfile[]>([]);
  const [profileId, setProfileId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [row, setRow] = useState<DbProfile | null>(null);
  const [links, setLinks] = useState<DbLink[]>([]);
  const [booted, setBooted] = useState(false);

  const committed = useRef<DbLink[]>([]);
  const latest = useRef<DbLink[]>([]);
  latest.current = links;
  const lastDeleted = useRef<DbLink | null>(null);

  /* 1) Session, profils de l'utilisateur et plan */
  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      if (!session) return router.replace("/auth");

      const [{ data: owned }, { data: myPlan }] = await Promise.all([
        supabase.from("profiles").select("*").eq("user_id", session.user.id),
        supabase.rpc("my_plan"),
      ]);
      const list = ((owned ?? []) as DbProfile[]).slice().sort(byCreated);
      if (list.length === 0) return router.replace("/onboarding");

      const remembered = readCookie();
      setUserId(session.user.id);
      setPlan(["free", "pro", "business"].includes(myPlan) ? (myPlan as Plan) : "free");
      setProfiles(list);
      setProfileId(list.find((p) => p.id === remembered)?.id ?? list[0].id);
      setBooted(true);
    })();
  }, [router]);

  /* 2) Données du profil sélectionné */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!profileId) return;
    let cancelled = false;
    (async () => {
      const [{ data: p }, { data: l }] = await Promise.all([
        supabase.from("profiles").select("*").eq("id", profileId).single(),
        supabase.from("links").select("*").eq("profile_id", profileId).order("position", { ascending: true }),
      ]);
      if (cancelled || !p) return;
      const rows = (l ?? []) as DbLink[];
      setRow(p as DbProfile);
      setLinks(rows);
      committed.current = rows;
    })();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [profileId]);

  const profile = useMemo(
    () => (row ? toProfile({ profile: row, plan, links }).profile : null),
    [row, plan, links]
  );

  const ready = booted && !!row && row.id === profileId;
  const publicUrl = useMemo(
    () => (typeof window !== "undefined" && row ? `${window.location.origin}/@${row.slug}` : ""),
    [row]
  );

  const selectProfile = useCallback((id: string) => {
    document.cookie = `${PROFILE_COOKIE}=${encodeURIComponent(id)}; path=/; max-age=31536000; samesite=lax`;
    setProfileId(id);
  }, []);

  const createNewProfile = async () => {
    if (!canCreateProfile(plan, profiles.length)) return toast(profileLimitMessage(plan), false);
    if (!userId) return;
    const n = profiles.length + 1;
    const slug = `profil-${n}`;
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from("profiles")
      .insert({
        user_id: userId,
        slug,
        display_name: `Profil ${n}`,
        bio: "Nouveau profil",
        avatar_url: `https://api.dicebear.com/7.x/avataaars/svg?seed=${slug}`,
      })
      .select("*")
      .single();
    if (error || !data) {
      if (error?.code === "23505") toast("Ce pseudo est déjà pris", false);
      else toast(`Erreur : ${error?.message ?? "création impossible"}`, false);
      return;
    }
    setProfiles((prev) => [...prev, data as DbProfile]);
    selectProfile((data as DbProfile).id);
    toast("Nouveau profil créé");
  };

  /* ---------- Liens ---------- */
  const rollback = useCallback(
    (msg: string) => {
      setLinks(committed.current);
      toast(msg, false);
    },
    [toast]
  );

  const addLink = async (title: string, url: string, icon?: string) => {
    if (!profileId) return false;
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from("links")
      .insert({ profile_id: profileId, title, url: normalizeUrl(url), icon: icon || "link", position: latest.current.length })
      .select()
      .single();
    if (error || !data) {
      if (error?.message.includes("link_limit_reached")) {
        toast(`Limite de ${ENTITLEMENTS.free.maxLinks} liens actifs atteinte (plan Gratuit). Passe au Pro pour en ajouter plus.`, false);
      } else toast("Impossible d'ajouter le lien", false);
      return false;
    }
    const next = [...latest.current, data as DbLink];
    setLinks(next);
    committed.current = next;
    toast("Lien ajouté");
    return true;
  };

  const updateLink = async (id: string, updates: Partial<DbLink>) => {
    setLinks((prev) => prev.map((l) => (l.id === id ? { ...l, ...updates } : l)));
    const { error } = await supabase.from("links").update(updates).eq("id", id);
    if (error) {
      if (error.message.includes("link_limit_reached")) {
        rollback(`Limite de ${ENTITLEMENTS.free.maxLinks} liens actifs atteinte. Passe au Pro pour en publier plus.`);
      } else rollback("Modification échouée");
      return;
    }
    committed.current = committed.current.map((l) => (l.id === id ? { ...l, ...updates } : l));
    toast("Enregistré");
  };

  const restoreLink = async () => {
    const victim = lastDeleted.current;
    if (!victim) return;
    const { error } = await supabase.from("links").insert(victim);
    if (error) return toast("Restauration échouée", false);
    const sort = (a: DbLink, b: DbLink) => a.position - b.position;
    setLinks((prev) => [...prev, victim].sort(sort));
    committed.current = [...committed.current, victim].sort(sort);
    lastDeleted.current = null;
    toast("Lien restauré");
  };

  const deleteLink = async (id: string) => {
    const victim = latest.current.find((l) => l.id === id) ?? null;
    setLinks((prev) => prev.filter((l) => l.id !== id));
    const { error } = await supabase.from("links").delete().eq("id", id);
    if (error) return rollback("Suppression échouée");
    committed.current = committed.current.filter((l) => l.id !== id);
    lastDeleted.current = victim;
    toast("Lien supprimé", true, { label: "Annuler", onClick: restoreLink });
  };

  const persistOrder = async () => {
    if (!profileId) return;
    const ids = latest.current.map((l) => l.id);
    if (ids.join() === committed.current.map((l) => l.id).join()) return;
    const { error } = await supabase.rpc("update_links_order", { p_profile: profileId, p_ids: ids });
    if (error) return rollback(`Réorganisation échouée : ${error.message}`);
    committed.current = [...latest.current];
  };

  /* ---------- Profil ---------- */
  const saveProfile = async (patch: ProfilePatch) => {
    if (!row) return false;
    if (patch.slug && patch.slug !== row.slug && isReserved(patch.slug)) {
      toast("Ce pseudo est réservé", false);
      return false;
    }
    const update: Record<string, string | null> = {};
    (Object.keys(patch) as (keyof ProfilePatch)[]).forEach((k) => {
      const v = patch[k];
      if ((k === "slug" || k === "displayName") && !v) return; // jamais vides
      update[COLUMNS[k]] = v ? v : null;
    });
    if (Object.keys(update).length === 0) return true;

    const prev = row;
    setRow({ ...row, ...update });
    const { error } = await supabase.from("profiles").update(update).eq("id", row.id);
    if (error) {
      setRow(prev);
      if (error.code === "23505") toast("Ce pseudo est déjà pris", false);
      else if (error.message.includes("slug_reserved")) toast("Ce pseudo est réservé", false);
      else toast(`Profil non enregistré : ${error.message}`, false);
      return false;
    }
    setProfiles((ps) => ps.map((p) => (p.id === row.id ? { ...p, ...update } : p)));
    toast("Profil enregistré");
    return true;
  };

  const copyUrl = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(publicUrl);
      toast("Lien copié");
    } catch {
      toast("Copie impossible", false);
    }
  };

  const shareUrl = async () => {
    if (navigator.share) {
      try {
        await navigator.share({ title: row?.display_name, url: publicUrl });
      } catch {
        /* partage annulé */
      }
    } else await copyUrl();
  };

  const logout = async () => {
    await supabase.auth.signOut();
    router.replace("/auth");
  };

  const value: Ctx = {
    ready,
    userId,
    plan,
    profiles,
    profileId,
    row,
    profile,
    links,
    setLinks,
    publicUrl,
    activeLinks: links.filter((l) => l.enabled).length,
    maxLinks: ENTITLEMENTS[plan].maxLinks,
    profileLimit: PROFILE_LIMITS[plan],
    canCreateProfile: canCreateProfile(plan, profiles.length),
    selectProfile,
    createNewProfile,
    addLink,
    updateLink,
    deleteLink,
    persistOrder,
    saveProfile,
    copyUrl,
    shareUrl,
    logout,
  };

  return <DashboardCtx.Provider value={value}>{children}</DashboardCtx.Provider>;
}

===== FILE: src/components/dashboard/DashboardShell.tsx =====

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { type ReactNode } from "react";
import { ChevronsUpDown, Copy, ExternalLink, LogOut, Plus, Share2 } from "lucide-react";
import { clsx } from "clsx";
import { Wordmark } from "@/components/landing/ui";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/Badge";
import { buttonClass } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { PLAN_LABEL } from "@/lib/dashboard-types";
import { useDashboard } from "./DashboardProvider";
import { OfflineBanner } from "./OfflineBanner";
import { ProfileAvatar } from "./ProfileAvatar";
import { NAV, isActive } from "./nav";

/**
 * Cadre commun de tout l'espace connecté : menu latéral (ordinateur), barre d'onglets (mobile)
 * et barre supérieure avec le lien public toujours à portée de main.
 */
export function DashboardShell({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const d = useDashboard();
  const pathname = usePathname();

  if (!d.ready || !d.row) return <ShellSkeleton />;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-bg text-ink">
      <OfflineBanner />

      {/* Menu latéral : ordinateur */}
      <aside className="fixed inset-y-0 left-0 z-40 hidden w-64 flex-col border-r border-line bg-surface lg:flex">
        <div className="px-5 pb-4 pt-5">
          <Link href="/dashboard" aria-label="Ndeletik, tableau de bord">
            <Wordmark />
          </Link>
        </div>

        <div className="px-3">
          <ProfileSwitcher />
        </div>

        <nav aria-label="Navigation du tableau de bord" className="mt-5 flex-1 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto px-3">
          {NAV.map((item) => {
            const active = isActive(item, pathname);
            return (
              <Link
                key={item.href}
                href={item.href}
                aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
                className={clsx(
                  "relative flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm transition-colors",
                  active ? "bg-fractal-ocre/10 font-semibold text-fractal-terra" : "font-medium text-muted hover:bg-soft hover:text-ink"
                )}
              >
                {active && (
                  <span className="absolute inset-y-2 left-0 w-1 rounded-r-full bg-gradient-to-b from-fractal-or via-fractal-ocre to-fractal-terra" />
                )}
                <item.icon size={18} aria-hidden />
                {item.label}
              </Link>
            );
          })}
        </nav>

        <div className="space-y-2 border-t border-line p-3">
          <PlanCard />
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={d.logout}
            className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-muted transition-colors hover:bg-soft hover:text-ink"
          >
            <LogOut size={18} aria-hidden /> Déconnexion
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <div className="lg:pl-64">
        {/* Barre supérieure : profil (mobile) + lien public */}
        <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 border-b border-line bg-bg/85 backdrop-blur">
          <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-6xl items-center gap-3 px-4 sm:px-6">
            <div className="lg:hidden">
              <MobileProfile />
            </div>
            <PublicLinkBar />
          </div>
        </header>

        <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-6xl px-4 pb-28 pt-6 sm:px-6 sm:pt-8 lg:pb-12">{children}</main>
      </div>

      {/* Onglets du bas : mobile */}
      <nav
        aria-label="Navigation du tableau de bord"
        className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-40 border-t border-line bg-surface/95 backdrop-blur lg:hidden"
        style={{ paddingBottom: "env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px)" }}
      >
        <ul className="mx-auto grid max-w-lg grid-cols-5">
          {NAV.map((item) => {
            const active = isActive(item, pathname);
            return (
              <li key={item.href}>
                <Link
                  href={item.href}
                  aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
                  className={clsx(
                    "relative flex h-16 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors",
                    active ? "text-fractal-terra" : "text-muted"
                  )}
                >
                  {active && (
                    <span className="absolute inset-x-6 top-0 h-0.5 rounded-b-full bg-gradient-to-r from-fractal-or via-fractal-ocre to-fractal-terra" />
                  )}
                  <item.icon size={20} aria-hidden />
                  {item.short}
                </Link>
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      </nav>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- Lien public (copier / partager / voir) ---------- */
function PublicLinkBar() {
  const { publicUrl, copyUrl, shareUrl, row } = useDashboard();
  if (!row) return null;
  const short = publicUrl.replace(/^https?:\/\//, "");

  return (
    <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-2 lg:justify-end">
      <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-line bg-surface py-1 pl-4 pr-1 shadow-soft">
        <span className="truncate text-sm text-muted">{short}</span>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={copyUrl}
          aria-label="Copier le lien de ma page"
          title="Copier le lien"
          className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-soft text-muted transition-colors hover:text-fractal-terra"
        >
          <Copy size={14} aria-hidden />
        </button>
      </div>
      <button type="button" onClick={shareUrl} className={buttonClass({ size: "sm", className: "hidden sm:inline-flex" })}>
        <Share2 size={14} aria-hidden /> Partager
      </button>
      <a
        href={`/@${row.slug}`}
        target="_blank"
        rel="noopener noreferrer"
        aria-label="Voir ma page publique"
        title="Voir ma page"
        className={buttonClass({ variant: "secondary", size: "sm", className: "!px-2.5" })}
      >
        <ExternalLink size={14} aria-hidden />
      </a>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- Sélecteur de profil ---------- */
function ProfileSwitcher() {
  const { profiles, profileId, row, selectProfile, createNewProfile, profileLimit, canCreateProfile } = useDashboard();
  if (!row) return null;
  const multi = profiles.length > 1;

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="relative rounded-2xl border border-line bg-bg p-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <ProfileAvatar profile={row} size={40} />
          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <p className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-ink">{row.display_name}</p>
            <p className="truncate text-xs text-muted">@{row.slug}</p>
          </div>
          {multi && <ChevronsUpDown size={16} className="shrink-0 text-muted" aria-hidden />}
        </div>
        {multi && (
          <select
            aria-label="Changer de profil"
            value={profileId ?? ""}
            onChange={(e) => selectProfile(e.target.value)}
            className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full cursor-pointer opacity-0"
          >
            {profiles.map((p) => (
              <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>
                {p.display_name} (@{p.slug})
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        )}
      </div>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={createNewProfile}
        className={clsx(
          "mt-2 flex w-full items-center justify-between rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-soft",
          canCreateProfile ? "text-fractal-terra" : "text-muted"
        )}
      >
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <Plus size={14} aria-hidden /> Nouveau profil
        </span>
        <span>
          {profiles.length}/{profileLimit}
        </span>
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

          className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full cursor-pointer opacity-0"

          {profiles.map((p) => (
            <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>
              {p.display_name} (@{p.slug})
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/components/dashboard/LinkAnalyticsModal.tsx =====

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { X, Globe, TrendingUp } from "lucide-react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabaseClient";
import type { DbLink } from "@/lib/dashboard-types";
import { AnalyticsChart } from "./AnalyticsChart";

type TrafficSource = { name: string; count: number; percentage: number };
type CountryData = { country: string; count: number };

type Props = {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  link: DbLink | null;
  profile: { id: string };
};

export function LinkAnalyticsModal({ open, onClose, link, profile }: Props) {
  const [sources, setSources] = useState<TrafficSource[]>([]);
  const [countries, setCountries] = useState<CountryData[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open || !link) return;

    (async () => {
      const { data: clicks } = await supabase
        .from("link_clicks")
        .select("referrer, country")
        .eq("link_id", link.id);

      // Sources de trafic
      const referrers = (clicks ?? []).map((c: any) => c.referrer).filter(Boolean);
      const sourceMap = new Map<string, number>();
      referrers.forEach((r: string) => {
        const source = extractSource(r);
        sourceMap.set(source, (sourceMap.get(source) ?? 0) + 1);
      });

      const total = referrers.length || 1;
      const sourcesList: TrafficSource[] = Array.from(sourceMap.entries())
        .map(([name, count]) => ({ name, count, percentage: (count / total) * 100 }))
        .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count)
        .slice(0, 5);

      setSources(sourcesList);

      // Pays
      const countryMap = new Map<string, number>();
      (clicks ?? []).forEach((c: any) => {
        if (c.country) {
          countryMap.set(c.country, (countryMap.get(c.country) ?? 0) + 1);
        }
      });

      const countriesList: CountryData[] = Array.from(countryMap.entries())
        .map(([country, count]) => ({ country, count }))
        .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count)
        .slice(0, 5);

      setCountries(countriesList);
    })();
  }, [open, link]);

  const extractSource = (url: string): string => {
    try {
      const domain = new URL(url).hostname.replace(/^www\./, "");
      if (domain.includes("instagram")) return "Instagram";
      if (domain.includes("tiktok")) return "TikTok";
      if (domain.includes("facebook") || domain.includes("fb")) return "Facebook";
      if (domain.includes("twitter") || domain.includes("x.com")) return "X (Twitter)";
      if (domain.includes("youtube")) return "YouTube";
      if (domain.includes("linkedin")) return "LinkedIn";
      if (domain.includes("google")) return "Google";
      return domain;
    } catch {
      return "Direct";
    }
  };

  if (!open || !link) return null;

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
      exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-ink/50 p-4"
      onClick={onClose}
    >
      <motion.div
        initial={{ scale: 0.9, opacity: 0 }}
        animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1 }}
        exit={{ scale: 0.9, opacity: 0 }}
        className="bg-surface rounded-3xl border border-line shadow-xl max-w-2xl w-full max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-surface border-b border-line px-6 py-4 flex items-center justify-between">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-display font-bold">{link.title}</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-soft text-muted">
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-6 space-y-6">
          <AnalyticsChart profile={profile} linkId={link.id} />

          {sources.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
                <Globe size={16} className="text-fractal-ocre" />
                <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold">Sources de trafic</h3>
              </div>
              <ul className="space-y-3">
                {sources.map((s) => (
                  <li key={s.name}>
                    <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-1">
                      <span className="font-medium">{s.name}</span>
                      <span className="text-muted">{s.count} ({s.percentage.toFixed(1)}%)</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="h-2 rounded-full bg-line/50">
                      <motion.div
                        initial={{ width: 0 }}
                        animate={{ width: `${s.percentage}%` }}
                        transition={{ duration: 0.5 }}
                        className="h-2 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-fractal-ocre to-fractal-terra"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}

          {countries.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
                <TrendingUp size={16} className="text-fractal-ocre" />
                <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold">Pays visiteurs</h3>
              </div>
              <ul className="space-y-2">
                {countries.map((c) => (
                  <li key={c.country} className="flex justify-between text-sm">
                    <span>{c.country}</span>
                    <span className="text-muted">{c.count} clics</span>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </motion.div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}
===== FILE: src/components/dashboard/LinkItem.tsx =====

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Reorder, useDragControls, AnimatePresence, motion } from "framer-motion";
import { BarChart3, GripVertical, Settings2, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { clsx } from "clsx";
import type { DbLink } from "@/lib/dashboard-types";
import { detectIcon } from "@/lib/detect-icon";
import { ConfirmDialog } from "@/components/ui/ConfirmDialog";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { Field, Switch, inputClass } from "@/components/ui/Field";
import { getBlockMeta, normalizeUrl } from "./blockMeta";
import { useToast } from "./Toast";

export const validUrl = (v: string) => {
  try {
    const u = new URL(normalizeUrl(v));
    return u.protocol.startsWith("http") || u.protocol === "mailto:" || u.protocol === "tel:";
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
};

type Props = {
  link: DbLink;
  onUpdate: (id: string, updates: Partial<DbLink>) => void;
  onDelete: (id: string) => void;
  onDragEnd: () => void;
  onShowAnalytics?: (link: DbLink) => void;
};

export function LinkItem({ link, onUpdate, onDelete, onDragEnd, onShowAnalytics }: Props) {
  const controls = useDragControls();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [confirming, setConfirming] = useState(false);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState(link.title);
  const [url, setUrl] = useState(link.url);
  const { Icon, bg } = getBlockMeta(link.url);

  const save = () => {
    if (!title.trim() || !url.trim()) return toast("Le titre et l'adresse sont requis", false);
    if (!validUrl(url)) return toast("Adresse invalide (ex : https://wa.me/…)", false);
    const clean = normalizeUrl(url);
    // L'icône est détectée une seule fois, à l'enregistrement (et non à chaque touche).
    onUpdate(link.id, { title: title.trim(), url: clean, icon: detectIcon(clean) });
    setOpen(false);
  };

  return (
    <Reorder.Item
      value={link}
      dragListener={false}
      dragControls={controls}
      onDragEnd={onDragEnd}
      whileDrag={{ scale: 1.02, boxShadow: "0 10px 30px rgba(193,68,14,.18)" }}
      className="list-none rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface shadow-soft"
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-3 sm:gap-3 sm:px-4">
        <button
          type="button"
          onPointerDown={(e) => controls.start(e)}
          aria-label="Déplacer ce lien"
          className="touch-none cursor-grab text-muted/60 transition-colors hover:text-muted active:cursor-grabbing"
        >
          <GripVertical size={18} aria-hidden />
        </button>

        <span
          className={clsx(
            "flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl text-white shadow-soft",
            bg,
            !link.enabled && "opacity-40"
          )}
        >
          <Icon size={18} aria-hidden />
        </span>

        <div className={clsx("min-w-0 flex-1", !link.enabled && "opacity-60")}>
          <p className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-ink">{link.title}</p>
          <p className="truncate text-xs text-muted">
            {link.enabled ? "" : "Masqué · "}
            {link.url.replace(/^https?:\/\//, "")}
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onShowAnalytics?.(link)}
          disabled={!onShowAnalytics}
          title="Voir les statistiques de ce lien"
          className="hidden shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-soft px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-muted transition-colors hover:text-fractal-terra sm:inline-flex"
        >
          <BarChart3 size={13} aria-hidden /> {link.clicks} clic{link.clicks > 1 ? "s" : ""}
        </button>

        <Switch
          checked={link.enabled}
          onChange={(v) => onUpdate(link.id, { enabled: v })}
          label={link.enabled ? "Masquer ce lien de ma page" : "Publier ce lien sur ma page"}
        />

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)}
          aria-label="Modifier ce lien"
          aria-expanded={open}
          className={clsx(
            "rounded-full p-2 transition-colors",
            open ? "bg-fractal-ocre/10 text-fractal-terra" : "text-muted hover:bg-soft hover:text-ink"
          )}
        >
          <Settings2 size={16} aria-hidden />
        </button>
      </div>

      <AnimatePresence initial={false}>
        {open && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ height: "auto", opacity: 1 }}
            exit={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
            className="overflow-hidden"
          >
            <div className="grid gap-3 border-t border-line bg-soft/40 px-4 pb-4 pt-4">
              <Field label="Titre">
                <input value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} className={inputClass} />
              </Field>
              <Field label="Adresse du lien">
                <input
                  value={url}
                  onChange={(e) => setUrl(e.target.value)}
                  inputMode="url"
                  className={inputClass}
                />
              </Field>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 pt-1">
                <Button variant="danger" size="sm" onClick={() => setConfirming(true)}>
                  <Trash2 size={14} aria-hidden /> Supprimer
                </Button>
                <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-2">
                  {onShowAnalytics && (
                    <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => onShowAnalytics(link)} className="sm:hidden">
                      <BarChart3 size={14} aria-hidden /> Statistiques
                    </Button>
                  )}
                  <Button size="sm" onClick={save}>
                    Enregistrer
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>

      <ConfirmDialog
        open={confirming}
        title="Supprimer ce lien ?"
        message={`« ${link.title} » sera retiré de ta page. Tu pourras annuler juste après.`}
        confirmLabel="Supprimer"
        danger
        onConfirm={() => {
          setConfirming(false);
          onDelete(link.id);
        }}
        onCancel={() => setConfirming(false)}
      />
    </Reorder.Item>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/components/dashboard/LinkTemplates.tsx =====

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Briefcase, BookOpen, Calendar, Gift, Globe, Mail, MapPin, Phone, ShoppingBag } from "lucide-react";

export type LinkTemplate = {

  label: string;
  title: string;
  url: string;
  icon: string;
  Icon: LucideIcon | IconType;
};

const TEMPLATES: LinkTemplate[] = [
  { id: "whatsapp", label: "WhatsApp", title: "Me contacter sur WhatsApp", url: "https://wa.me/", icon: "message", Icon: FaWhatsapp },

  { id: "email", label: "E-mail", title: "M'envoyer un e-mail", url: "mailto:", icon: "mail", Icon: Mail },
  { id: "location", label: "Adresse", title: "Ma localisation", url: "https://maps.google.com/", icon: "pin", Icon: MapPin },

  { id: "linkedin", label: "LinkedIn", title: "Mon profil LinkedIn", url: "https://linkedin.com/in/", icon: "link", Icon: FaLinkedin },
];

const VISIBLE = 8;

type Props = {
  /** Dashboard : ouvre le formulaire pré-rempli (le lien reste à compléter). */
  onPick?: (t: LinkTemplate) => void;
  /** Onboarding : ajoute directement le modèle (comportement d'origine). */
  onAdd?: (title: string, url: string, icon: string) => Promise<boolean> | void;
};

/** Modèles d'ajout rapide. */
export function LinkTemplates({ onPick, onAdd }: Props) {
  const [all, setAll] = useState(false);
  const shown = all ? TEMPLATES : TEMPLATES.slice(0, VISIBLE);

  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="quick-add">
      <div className="mb-3 flex items-end justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h2 id="quick-add" className="font-display text-sm font-bold text-ink">
            Ajout rapide
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted">Choisis un modèle, puis colle ton lien.</p>
        </div>
        <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => setAll((v) => !v)}>
          {all ? "Réduire" : `Voir tout (${TEMPLATES.length})`}
        </Button>
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {shown.map((t) => (
          <button
            key={t.id}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => (onPick ? onPick(t) : onAdd?.(t.title, t.url, t.icon))}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-line bg-surface px-3.5 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-ink transition-colors hover:border-fractal-ocre hover:text-fractal-terra"
          >
            <t.Icon size={15} className="text-fractal-ocre" aria-hidden />
            {t.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/components/dashboard/LivePreview.tsx =====

"use client";

import { Eye } from "lucide-react";
import { ProfileRenderer } from "@/components/profile/ProfileRenderer";
import { buttonClass } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { useDashboard } from "./DashboardProvider";

/** Téléphone d'aperçu : reflète en direct les liens, le profil et le thème de la page. */
export function LivePreview({ framed = true }: { framed?: boolean }) {
  const { profile, plan, row } = useDashboard();
  if (!profile || !row) return null;

  const phone = (
    <div className="nd-phone nd-phone-sm mx-auto">
      <div className="nd-screen">
        <ProfileRenderer profile={profile} plan={plan} preview />
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  if (!framed) return phone;

  return (
    <div className="rounded-3xl border border-line bg-surface p-5 text-center shadow-soft">
      <p className="mb-4 font-display text-sm font-bold text-ink">Aperçu en direct</p>
      {phone}
      <a
        href={`/@${row.slug}`}
        target="_blank"
        rel="noopener noreferrer"
        className={buttonClass({ variant: "secondary", full: true, className: "mt-4" })}
      >
        <Eye size={16} aria-hidden /> Voir ma page publique
      </a>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/components/dashboard/OfflineBanner.tsx =====

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export function OfflineBanner() {
  const [online, setOnline] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    setOnline(navigator.onLine);
    const on = () => setOnline(true);
    const off = () => setOnline(false);
    window.addEventListener("online", on);
    window.addEventListener("offline", off);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("online", on);
      window.removeEventListener("offline", off);
    };
  }, []);

  if (online) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-x-0 top-0 z-[60] bg-fractal-terra py-2 text-center text-xs font-semibold text-white shadow-soft">
      Connexion perdue — tes modifications ne seront pas enregistrées tant que le réseau ne revient pas.
    </div>
  );
}

import type { LinkItem, Profile } from "@/lib/themes/types";

export function PhonePreview({ profile, links }: { profile: Profile; links: LinkItem[] }) {

          <div className="mt-4 w-full space-y-2">
            {active.length === 0 && (
              <p className="text-center text-[11px] text-muted py-6">Aucun bloc actif</p>
            )}
            {active.map((l, i) => {
              const { Icon, bg } = getBlockMeta(l.url);
              return i === 0 ? (
                <div key={l.id} className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl bg-ink text-bg px-3 py-3">
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold truncate">{l.title}</span>
                  <ArrowUpRight size={14} />
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div key={l.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-surface border border-line px-2.5 py-2.5">
                  <span className={`h-6 w-6 rounded-md ${bg} text-white flex items-center justify-center shrink-0`}>
                    <Icon size={12} />
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-ink truncate">{l.title}</span>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
===== FILE: src/components/dashboard/ProfileAvatar.tsx =====

import type { DbProfile } from "@/lib/dashboard-types";

/** Avatar rond avec repli sur l'initiale, aux couleurs de la marque. */
export function ProfileAvatar({
  profile,
  size = 40,
}: {
  profile: Pick<DbProfile, "display_name" | "slug" | "avatar_url">;
  size?: number;
}) {
  const name = profile.display_name || profile.slug;
  if (profile.avatar_url) {
    return (
      // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
      <img
        src={profile.avatar_url}
        alt=""
        width={size}
        height={size}
        className="shrink-0 rounded-full bg-soft object-cover"
        style={{ width: size, height: size }}
      />
    );
  }
  return (
    <span
      aria-hidden
      className="flex shrink-0 select-none items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-fractal-or to-fractal-terra font-display font-extrabold text-white"
      style={{ width: size, height: size, fontSize: Math.round(size / 2.4) }}
    >
      {name.slice(0, 1).toUpperCase()}
    </span>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Copy, Share2, Globe } from "lucide-react";
import type { Profile } from "@/lib/types";

export type { Profile };

export function Banner({ url, className = "" }: { url: string | null; className?: string }) {
  if (url) {
    return (
      <div
        className={`w-full bg-cover bg-center ${className}`}
        style={{ backgroundImage: `url(${url})` }}
        role="img"
        aria-label="Bannière du profil"
      />
    );
  }
  return <div className={`w-full bg-fractal-ocre/40 ${className}`} />;
}

export function Avatar({ profile, size = 88 }: { profile: Profile; size?: number }) {
  const name = profile.display_name || profile.slug;
  const initials = name.slice(0, 2).toUpperCase();

  if (profile.avatar_url) {
    return (
      // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
      <img
        src={profile.avatar_url}
        alt={name}
        width={size}
        height={size}
        className="rounded-full object-cover bg-surface"
        style={{ width: size, height: size }}
      />
    );
  }
  return (
    <div
      className="rounded-full bg-fractal-ocre text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold select-none"
      style={{ width: size, height: size, fontSize: Math.round(size / 2.6) }}
      aria-label={name}
    >
      {initials}
    </div>
  );
}

type ProfileCardProps = {

  onCopy: () => void;
  onShare: () => void;
};

export function ProfileCard({ profile, publicUrl, onCopy, onShare }: ProfileCardProps) {

===== FILE: src/components/dashboard/ProfileForm.tsx =====

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { Card, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/Card";
import { Field, inputClass } from "@/components/ui/Field";
import { useDashboard } from "./DashboardProvider";
import { ProfileAvatar } from "./ProfileAvatar";
import { UploadButton } from "./UploadButton";

/** Photo, bannière et informations du profil. À monter avec key={profileId} pour repartir des bonnes valeurs. */
export function ProfileForm() {
  const { row, saveProfile } = useDashboard();
  const [f, setF] = useState({
    slug: row?.slug ?? "",
    displayName: row?.display_name ?? "",
    headline: row?.headline ?? "",
    bio: row?.bio ?? "",
    avatarUrl: row?.avatar_url ?? "",
    coverUrl: row?.cover_url ?? "",
  });
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  if (!row) return null;

  const set = (k: keyof typeof f) => (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement>) =>
    setF((p) => ({ ...p, [k]: e.target.value }));

  const cleanSlug = f.slug.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_.-]/g, "") || row.slug;
  const dirty =
    cleanSlug !== row.slug ||
    f.displayName.trim() !== row.display_name ||
    f.headline.trim() !== (row.headline ?? "") ||
    f.bio.trim() !== (row.bio ?? "");

  const submit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true);
    await saveProfile({
      slug: cleanSlug,
      displayName: f.displayName.trim() || row.display_name,
      headline: f.headline.trim() || undefined,
      bio: f.bio.trim() || undefined,
    });
    setSaving(false);
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-6">
      <Card>
        <CardTitle hint="Format carré, 3 Mo maximum. Enregistrée tout de suite.">Photo de profil</CardTitle>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <ProfileAvatar profile={{ ...row, avatar_url: f.avatarUrl || null }} size={80} />
          <UploadButton
            kind="avatar"
            onDone={(url) => {
              setF((p) => ({ ...p, avatarUrl: url }));
              saveProfile({ avatarUrl: url });
            }}
          />
        </div>
      </Card>

      <Card>
        <CardTitle hint="Format rectangulaire, 3 Mo maximum. Enregistrée tout de suite.">Bannière</CardTitle>
        <div className="mb-3 h-24 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-line bg-fractal-ocre/15">
          {f.coverUrl && <div className="h-full bg-cover bg-center" style={{ backgroundImage: `url(${f.coverUrl})` }} />}
        </div>
        <UploadButton
          kind="banner"
          onDone={(url) => {
            setF((p) => ({ ...p, coverUrl: url }));
            saveProfile({ coverUrl: url });
          }}
        />
      </Card>

      <Card className="space-y-4">
        <CardTitle>Informations</CardTitle>
        <Field label="Pseudo" hint={`Ton adresse publique : /@${cleanSlug}`}>
          <input value={f.slug} onChange={set("slug")} className={inputClass} autoCapitalize="none" autoCorrect="off" />
        </Field>
        <Field label="Nom affiché">
          <input value={f.displayName} onChange={set("displayName")} className={inputClass} />
        </Field>
        <Field label="Titre (sous le nom)">
          <input value={f.headline} onChange={set("headline")} className={inputClass} placeholder="Ex : Coach business · Pointe-Noire" />
        </Field>
        <Field label="Bio">
          <textarea rows={3} value={f.bio} onChange={set("bio")} className={inputClass} />
        </Field>
        <Button type="submit" size="lg" full disabled={!dirty || saving}>
          {saving ? "Enregistrement…" : dirty ? "Enregistrer les modifications" : "Aucune modification"}
        </Button>
      </Card>
    </form>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/components/dashboard/ShortLinkManager.tsx =====

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";
import { Plus, Link2, Copy, QrCode, Trash2, X, Sparkles } from "lucide-react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabaseClient";
import { useToast } from "./Toast";
import { ConfirmDialog } from "@/components/ui/ConfirmDialog";

type ShortLink = {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  target_url: string;
  clicks: number;
  is_active: boolean;
  created_at: string;
};

const SLUG_REGEX = /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{2,29}$/;
const QUOTAS: Record<string, number> = { free: 3, pro: Infinity, business: Infinity };

type Props = { userId: string; plan: string };

export function ShortLinkManager({ userId, plan }: Props) {
  const { toast, node: toastNode } = useToast();
  const [links, setLinks] = useState<ShortLink[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);
  const [slug, setSlug] = useState("");
  const [target, setTarget] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [qrSlug, setQrSlug] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [deleteTarget, setDeleteTarget] = useState<ShortLink | null>(null);

  const quota = QUOTAS[plan] ?? QUOTAS.free;
  const atLimit = links.length >= quota;
  const remaining = quota === Infinity ? Infinity : Math.max(0, quota - links.length);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from("short_links")
        .select("*")
        .eq("user_id", userId)
        .order("created_at", { ascending: false });
      setLinks((data as ShortLink[]) ?? []);
      setLoading(false);
    })();
  }, [userId]);

  const fullUrl = (s: string) =>
    typeof window !== "undefined" ? `${window.location.origin}/go/${s}` : `/go/${s}`;

  const create = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!SLUG_REGEX.test(slug)) {
      return toast("Alias invalide : 3 à 30 caractères (a-z, 0-9, -)", false);
    }
    if (!target.trim()) return toast("URL cible requise", false);

    setBusy(true);
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from("short_links")
      .insert({ user_id: userId, slug: slug.trim().toLowerCase(), target_url: target.trim() })
      .select()
      .single();
    setBusy(false);

    if (error) {
      if (error.code === "23505") toast("Cet alias est déjà pris, essaie un autre", false);
      else toast(`Échec : ${error.message}`, false);
      return;
    }

    setLinks((prev) => [data as ShortLink, ...prev]);
    setSlug("");
    setTarget("");
    setShowForm(false);
    toast("Raccourci créé !");
  };

  const remove = async (id: string) => {
    const { error } = await supabase.from("short_links").delete().eq("id", id);
    if (error) return toast("Suppression échouée", false);
    setLinks((prev) => prev.filter((l) => l.id !== id));
    toast("Raccourci supprimé");
  };

  const copy = async (s: string) => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(fullUrl(s));
      toast("Lien copié !");
    } catch {
      toast("Copie impossible", false);
    }
  };

  const randomSlug = () => {
    const chars = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789";
    let out = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) out += chars[Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length)];
    setSlug(out);
  };

  const inputCls =
    "w-full rounded-xl border border-line bg-bg px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-ink focus:outline-none focus:border-fractal-ocre transition-colors";

  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-5 shadow-soft">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
        <div>
          <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <Sparkles size={14} className="text-fractal-ocre" /> Raccourcis Ndeletik
          </h3>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted mt-0.5">
            {atLimit ? (
              <span className="text-fractal-terra">
                Limite atteinte ({quota}) — passe au plan Pro pour des raccourcis illimités
              </span>
            ) : (
              <>
                {links.length} raccourci{links.length > 1 ? "s" : ""} ·{" "}
                {remaining === Infinity ? "illimité" : `${remaining} restant${remaining > 1 ? "s" : ""}`}
              </>
            )}
          </p>
        </div>
        {!atLimit && (
          <button
            onClick={() => setShowForm(!showForm)}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-fractal-ocre hover:bg-fractal-terra text-white text-xs font-semibold px-3 py-2 transition-colors"
          >
            {showForm ? <X size={14} /> : <Plus size={14} />}
            {showForm ? "Annuler" : "Nouveau"}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      <AnimatePresence initial={false}>
        {showForm && (
          <motion.form
            onSubmit={create}
            initial={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ height: "auto", opacity: 1 }}
            exit={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
            className="overflow-hidden mb-4"
          >
            <div className="grid gap-3 pb-4 border-b border-line">
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs text-muted mb-1.5 block">Alias (slug)</label>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <div className="flex-1 flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-line bg-bg px-3 py-2.5">
                    <span className="text-xs text-muted whitespace-nowrap">ndeletik.com/go/</span>
                    <input
                      value={slug}
                      onChange={(e) => setSlug(e.target.value.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9-]/g, ""))}
                      placeholder="commande"
                      className="flex-1 bg-transparent text-sm text-ink focus:outline-none min-w-0"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={randomSlug}
                    className="shrink-0 rounded-xl border border-line px-3 text-xs text-muted hover:border-fractal-ocre hover:text-fractal-terra transition-colors"
                    title="Générer un alias aléatoire"
                  >
                    🎲
                  </button>
                </div>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-muted mt-1">3 à 30 caractères : lettres minuscules, chiffres, tirets.</p>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs text-muted mb-1.5 block">URL cible</label>
                <input
                  value={target}
                  onChange={(e) => setTarget(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="https://wa.me/242..."
                  className={inputCls}
                />
              </div>
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={busy || !slug || !target}
                className="rounded-xl bg-fractal-ocre hover:bg-fractal-terra disabled:opacity-40 text-white text-sm font-semibold py-2.5 transition-colors"
              >
                {busy ? "Création…" : "Créer le raccourci"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </motion.form>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {[...Array(2)].map((_, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="h-14 rounded-xl bg-line/50 animate-pulse" />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : links.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-8 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-line">
          <Link2 size={28} className="mx-auto text-line mb-2" />
          <p className="text-xs text-muted">Aucun raccourci pour le moment.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <ul className="space-y-2">
          {links.map((l) => (
            <li key={l.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl bg-surface border border-line p-3">
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <a
                  href={fullUrl(l.slug)}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="text-sm font-semibold text-ink hover:text-fractal-terra transition-colors truncate block"
                >
                  /go/{l.slug}
                </a>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-muted truncate">{l.target_url}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="text-right shrink-0">
                <div className="text-sm font-bold text-fractal-ocre">{l.clicks}</div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-muted">clics</div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
                <button
                  onClick={() => copy(l.slug)}
                  aria-label="Copier"
                  className="p-1.5 rounded-md hover:bg-soft text-muted hover:text-fractal-ocre transition-colors"
                >
                  <Copy size={14} />
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setQrSlug(l.slug)}
                  aria-label="QR code"
                  className="p-1.5 rounded-md hover:bg-soft text-muted hover:text-fractal-ocre transition-colors"
                >
                  <QrCode size={14} />
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(l)}
                  aria-label="Supprimer"
                  className="p-1.5 rounded-md hover:bg-red-50 text-muted hover:text-red-600 transition-colors"
                >
                  <Trash2 size={14} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}

      {/* Confirmation de suppression */}
      <ConfirmDialog
        open={!!deleteTarget}
        title="Supprimer ce raccourci ?"
        message={`ndeletik.com/go/${deleteTarget?.slug} cessera de fonctionner immédiatement.`}
        confirmLabel="Supprimer"
        danger
        onConfirm={() => {
          if (deleteTarget) remove(deleteTarget.id);
          setDeleteTarget(null);
        }}
        onCancel={() => setDeleteTarget(null)}
      />

      {qrSlug && <QrModal slug={qrSlug} onClose={() => setQrSlug(null)} />}
      {toastNode}
    </div>
  );
}

function QrModal({ slug, onClose }: { slug: string; onClose: () => void }) {
  const [dataUrl, setDataUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      try {
        const { toDataURL } = await import("qrcode");
        const url =
          typeof window !== "undefined" ? `${window.location.origin}/go/${slug}` : `/go/${slug}`;
        const data = await toDataURL(url, {
          margin: 2,
          width: 400,
          color: { dark: "#18181B", light: "#FFF9F3" },
        });
        setDataUrl(data);
      } catch {
        setError(true);
      }
    })();
  }, [slug]);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center bg-ink/50 p-4" onClick={onClose}>
      <div
        className="bg-surface rounded-2xl p-6 border border-line shadow-xl max-w-sm w-full"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
          <h3 className="font-display font-bold">QR Code</h3>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1 rounded-md hover:bg-soft text-muted" aria-label="Fermer">
            <X size={16} />
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="aspect-square bg-surface rounded-xl border border-line p-4 flex items-center justify-center mb-3">
          {error ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-muted text-center">Impossible de générer le QR code</p>
          ) : dataUrl ? (
            // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
            <img src={dataUrl} alt={`QR code ndeletik.com/go/${slug}`} className="w-full h-full" />
          ) : (
            <div className="w-full h-full rounded-xl bg-soft animate-pulse" />
          )}
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted text-center mb-4">ndeletik.com/go/{slug}</p>
        <a
          href={dataUrl ?? "#"}
          download={`qr-${slug}.png`}
          className={`block w-full text-center rounded-xl bg-fractal-ocre hover:bg-fractal-terra text-white text-sm font-semibold py-2.5 transition-colors ${
            !dataUrl ? "pointer-events-none opacity-40" : ""
          }`}
        >
          Télécharger le QR
        </a>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
===== FILE: src/components/dashboard/Toast.tsx =====

"use client";

import { AnimatePresence, motion } from "framer-motion";
import { AlertCircle, CheckCircle2, Undo2 } from "lucide-react";
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useMemo, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";

export type ToastAction = { label: string; onClick: () => void };
type ToastState = { id: number; msg: string; ok: boolean; action?: ToastAction };

/**
 * Toasts empilés en bas de l'écran.
 * Signature rétro-compatible : toast(msg) / toast(msg, false) / toast(msg, true, action).
 */
type ToastFn = (msg: string, ok?: boolean, action?: ToastAction) => void;
const ToastCtx = createContext<{ toast: ToastFn } | null>(null);

/**
 * Fournit un seul empilement de toasts à tout le dashboard. Sans fournisseur, useToast()
 * garde son comportement d'origine (sa propre pile, à afficher via `node`).
 */
export function ToastProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { toast, node } = useToastState();
  const value = useMemo(() => ({ toast }), [toast]);
  return (
    <ToastCtx.Provider value={value}>
      {children}
      {node}
    </ToastCtx.Provider>
  );
}

export function useToast(): { toast: ToastFn; node: ReactNode } {
  const ctx = useContext(ToastCtx);
  const local = useToastState();
  return ctx ? { toast: ctx.toast, node: null } : local;
}

function useToastState() {
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState<ToastState[]>([]);
  const idRef = useRef(0);

  const dismiss = useCallback((id: number) => {
    setToasts((t) => t.filter((x) => x.id !== id));
  }, []);

  const toast = useCallback(
    (msg: string, ok = true, action?: ToastAction) => {
      const id = ++idRef.current;
      setToasts((t) => [...t.slice(-2), { id, msg, ok, action }]);
      window.setTimeout(() => dismiss(id), action ? 6000 : 3200);
    },
    [dismiss]
  );

  const node = (
    <div className="pointer-events-none fixed bottom-5 left-1/2 z-[80] flex w-full max-w-sm -translate-x-1/2 flex-col items-center gap-2 px-4">
      <AnimatePresence>
        {toasts.map((t) => (
          <motion.div
            key={t.id}
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 16, scale: 0.96 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, scale: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: 8, scale: 0.96 }}
            className={`pointer-events-auto flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-2xl border px-4 py-3 shadow-soft backdrop-blur ${
              t.ok
                ? "border-line bg-surface/95 text-ink"
                : "border-fractal-terra/30 bg-fractal-terra/10 text-fractal-terra"
            }`}
          >
            {t.ok ? (
              <CheckCircle2 size={16} className="shrink-0 text-fractal-ocre" />
            ) : (
              <AlertCircle size={16} className="shrink-0" />
            )}
            <span className="flex-1 text-sm">{t.msg}</span>
            {t.action && (
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  t.action!.onClick();
                  dismiss(t.id);
                }}
                className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-line bg-surface px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-fractal-terra hover:bg-soft"
              >
                <Undo2 size={12} /> {t.action.label}
              </button>
            )}
          </motion.div>
        ))}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  );

  return { toast, node };
}
===== FILE: src/components/dashboard/UploadButton.tsx =====

"use client";
import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Upload, Loader2, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { supabase } from "@/lib/supabaseClient";
import { CropModal } from "./CropModal";

type Props = {
  kind: "avatar" | "banner";
  onDone: (url: string) => void;
};

const MAX_SIZE = 3 * 1024 * 1024;

export function UploadButton({ kind, onDone }: Props) {
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const [state, setState] = useState<"idle" | "busy" | "ok" | "err">("idle");
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState("");
  const [imageSrc, setImageSrc] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const aspect = kind === "avatar" ? 1 : 3;

  const pick = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    e.target.value = "";
    if (!file) return;
    if (!file.type.startsWith("image/")) {
      setState("err");
      setMsg("Image uniquement");
      return;
    }
    if (file.size > MAX_SIZE) {
      setState("err");
      setMsg("3 Mo maximum");
      return;
    }
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => setImageSrc(reader.result as string);
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  };

  const handleCropConfirm = async (blob: Blob) => {
    setState("busy");
    setImageSrc(null);
    const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
    if (!session) return;
    const path = `${session.user.id}/${kind}-${Date.now()}.jpg`;
    const { error } = await supabase.storage.from("media").upload(path, blob, {
      contentType: "image/jpeg",
      cacheControl: "3600",
      upsert: false,
    });
    if (error) {
      setState("err");
      setMsg("Upload échoué");
      return;
    }
    const { data } = supabase.storage.from("media").getPublicUrl(path);
    onDone(data.publicUrl);
    setState("ok");
    setTimeout(() => setState("idle"), 2500);
  };

  return (
    <>
      <input ref={inputRef} type="file" accept="image/*" className="hidden" onChange={pick} />
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => inputRef.current?.click()}
        disabled={state === "busy"}
        className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-line hover:border-fractal-ocre text-ink hover:text-fractal-terra text-xs px-3 py-2 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 bg-surface"
      >
        {state === "busy" ? (
          <Loader2 size={14} className="animate-spin text-fractal-ocre" />
        ) : state === "ok" ? (
          <CheckCircle2 size={14} className="text-fractal-ocre" />
        ) : state === "err" ? (
          <AlertCircle size={14} className="text-red-500" />
        ) : (
          <Upload size={14} />
        )}
        {state === "busy" ? "Envoi…" : state === "ok" ? "En ligne !" : state === "err" ? msg : "Uploader"}
      </button>

      {imageSrc && (
        <CropModal imageSrc={imageSrc} aspect={aspect} onConfirm={handleCropConfirm} onClose={() => setImageSrc(null)} />
      )}
    </>
  );
}

export type BlockMeta = { Icon: IconType; bg: string };

export function normalizeUrl(raw: string) {
  const v = raw.trim();
  if (/^(https?:\/\/|mailto:|tel:)/i.test(v)) return v;
  if (v.includes("@") && !v.includes("/")) return `mailto:${v}`;
  return `https://${v}`;
}

export function getBlockMeta(url: string): BlockMeta {
  const u = url.toLowerCase();

  // ===== Messageries =====

  if (u.includes("instagram")) return { Icon: FaInstagram, bg: "bg-[linear-gradient(45deg,#FEDA75_0%,#FA7E1E_25%,#D62976_50%,#962FBF_75%,#4F5BD5_100%)]" };
