===== FILE: src/components/sections/page.tsx =====

import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { SectionsEditor } from "@/components/dashboard/BlocksEditor";
import { toProfile } from "@/lib/profile-mapper";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export const metadata: Metadata = { title: "Sections de ma page" };

export default async function SectionsPage() {
  const supabase = await createClient();
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (!user) redirect("/auth");

  const { data: row } = await supabase.from("profiles").select("*").eq("user_id", user.id).maybeSingle();
  if (!row) {
    return <main className="mx-auto max-w-xl px-6 py-24 text-center text-muted">Crée d&apos;abord ta page.</main>;
  }

  const { profile } = toProfile({ profile: row, plan: "free", links: [] });

  return (
    <SectionsEditor
      profileId={row.id}
      initialBlocks={profile.blocks ?? []}
      initialSocials={profile.socials}
    />
  );
}
===== FILE: src/components/ui/Badge.tsx =====

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { clsx } from "clsx";

const tones = {
  neutral: "bg-soft text-muted",
  brand: "bg-fractal-ocre/10 text-fractal-terra",
  success: "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700",
} as const;

export function Badge({ tone = "neutral", children }: { tone?: keyof typeof tones; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <span className={clsx("inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold", tones[tone])}>
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/components/ui/Button.tsx =====

import { forwardRef, type ButtonHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { clsx } from "clsx";
import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge";

export type ButtonVariant = "primary" | "secondary" | "ghost" | "danger";
export type ButtonSize = "sm" | "md" | "lg";

const base =
  "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full font-semibold transition-colors " +
  "focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-fractal-ocre " +
  "disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50";

const variants: Record<ButtonVariant, string> = {
  primary: "bg-fractal-ocre text-white shadow-soft hover:bg-fractal-terra",
  secondary: "border border-line bg-surface text-ink hover:border-fractal-ocre hover:text-fractal-terra",
  ghost: "text-muted hover:bg-soft hover:text-ink",
  danger: "text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50",
};

const sizes: Record<ButtonSize, string> = {
  sm: "px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs",
  md: "px-5 py-2.5 text-sm",
  lg: "px-6 py-3.5 text-sm",
};

/** Classes d'un bouton : à utiliser aussi sur <Link> / <a> pour garder le même rendu. */
export function buttonClass({
  variant = "primary",
  size = "md",
  full = false,
  className,
}: { variant?: ButtonVariant; size?: ButtonSize; full?: boolean; className?: string } = {}) {
  // twMerge : une classe passée en `className` (ex. « hidden sm:inline-flex ») l'emporte sur celles du bouton.
  return twMerge(clsx(base, variants[variant], sizes[size], full && "w-full", className));
}

type Props = ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> & {
  variant?: ButtonVariant;
  size?: ButtonSize;
  full?: boolean;
};

export const Button = forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, Props>(function Button(
  { variant, size, full, className, type = "button", ...rest },
  ref
) {
  return <button ref={ref} type={type} className={buttonClass({ variant, size, full, className })} {...rest} />;
});

===== FILE: src/components/ui/Card.tsx =====

import type { HTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { clsx } from "clsx";

/** Surface standard du dashboard : fond blanc, bordure chaude, ombre douce. */
export function Card({ className, padded = true, ...rest }: HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement> & { padded?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div
      className={clsx("rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface shadow-soft", padded && "p-5", className)}
      {...rest}
    />
  );
}

export function CardTitle({ children, hint }: { children: React.ReactNode; hint?: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="mb-3">
      <h2 className="font-display text-sm font-bold text-ink">{children}</h2>
      {hint ? <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted">{hint}</p> : null}
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/components/ui/ConfirmDialog.tsx =====

"use client";

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { createPortal } from "react-dom";

type Props = {
  open: boolean;
  title: string;
  message: string;
  confirmLabel?: string;
  cancelLabel?: string;
  danger?: boolean;
  onConfirm: () => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
};

/** Confirmation charte YEKOLA, tactile-friendly, fermable via Échap ou clic sur le fond. */
export function ConfirmDialog({
  open,
  title,
  message,
  confirmLabel = "Confirmer",
  cancelLabel = "Annuler",
  danger = false,
  onConfirm,
  onCancel,
}: Props) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const h = (e: KeyboardEvent) => e.key === "Escape" && onCancel();
    window.addEventListener("keydown", h);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", h);
  }, [open, onCancel]);

  if (!open) return null;

  return createPortal(
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[70] flex items-center justify-center bg-ink/50 p-4" onClick={onCancel}>
      <div
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-label={title}
        className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface p-6 shadow-xl"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <h3 className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-ink">{title}</h3>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted">{message}</p>
        <div className="mt-5 flex gap-3">
          <button
            onClick={onCancel}
            className="flex-1 rounded-full border border-line px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-muted hover:bg-soft"
          >
            {cancelLabel}
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={onConfirm}
            className={`flex-1 rounded-full px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white ${
              danger ? "bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700" : "bg-fractal-ocre hover:bg-fractal-terra"
            }`}
          >
            {confirmLabel}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>,
    document.body
  );
}
===== FILE: src/components/ui/EmptyState.tsx =====

import type { ReactNode } from "react";

export function EmptyState({
  icon,
  title,
  description,
  action,
}: {
  icon: ReactNode;
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  action?: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-line bg-surface/60 px-6 py-12 text-center">
      <div className="mx-auto mb-3 flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-fractal-ocre/10 text-fractal-ocre">
        {icon}
      </div>
      <p className="font-display text-base font-bold text-ink">{title}</p>
      {description ? <p className="mx-auto mt-1 max-w-sm text-sm text-muted">{description}</p> : null}
      {action ? <div className="mt-4 flex justify-center">{action}</div> : null}
    </div>
  );
}

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { clsx } from "clsx";

/** Style unique des champs de saisie du dashboard. */

  "w-full rounded-xl border border-line bg-bg px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-ink placeholder:text-muted/70 " +

/** Interrupteur accessible (role="switch"). */
export function Switch({
  checked,
  onChange,
  label,
  className,
}: {
  checked: boolean;
  onChange: (next: boolean) => void;
  label: string;
  className?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      role="switch"
      aria-checked={checked}
      aria-label={label}
      title={label}
      onClick={() => onChange(!checked)}
      className={clsx(
        "relative inline-flex h-6 w-10 shrink-0 items-center rounded-full transition-colors",
        "focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-fractal-ocre",
        checked ? "bg-fractal-ocre" : "bg-line",
        className
      )}
    >
      <span
        className={clsx(
          "inline-block h-4 w-4 rounded-full bg-white shadow transition-transform",
          checked ? "translate-x-5" : "translate-x-1"
        )}
      />
    </button>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/components/ui/Modal.tsx =====

"use client";

import { useEffect, useId, useRef, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { createPortal } from "react-dom";
import { X } from "lucide-react";
import { clsx } from "clsx";

type Props = {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  size?: "sm" | "md" | "lg";
  children: ReactNode;
};

const widths = { sm: "max-w-sm", md: "max-w-md", lg: "max-w-lg" };
const FOCUSABLE = "a[href], button:not([disabled]), input:not([disabled]), select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex='-1'])";

/**
 * Fenêtre unique du dashboard : feuille en bas sur mobile, centrée sur ordinateur.
 * Échap et clic sur le fond ferment ; le focus est piégé puis rendu à l'élément d'origine.
 */
export function Modal({ open, onClose, title, description, size = "md", children }: Props) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const titleId = useId();
  const descId = useId();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const node = ref.current;
    const opener = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
    (node?.querySelector<HTMLElement>("[data-autofocus]") ?? node)?.focus();
    const prevOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";

    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") return onClose();
      if (e.key !== "Tab" || !node) return;
      const items = Array.from(node.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE));
      if (!items.length) return;
      const first = items[0];
      const last = items[items.length - 1];
      if (e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
        e.preventDefault();
        last.focus();
      } else if (!e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
        e.preventDefault();
        first.focus();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
      document.body.style.overflow = prevOverflow;
      opener?.focus?.();
    };
  }, [open, onClose]);

  if (!open) return null;

  return createPortal(
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[70] flex items-end justify-center bg-ink/50 sm:items-center sm:p-4" onClick={onClose}>
      <div
        ref={ref}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby={titleId}
        aria-describedby={description ? descId : undefined}
        tabIndex={-1}
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        className={clsx(
          "relative max-h-[92vh] w-full overflow-y-auto rounded-t-3xl border border-line bg-surface p-5 shadow-xl outline-none sm:rounded-3xl sm:p-6",
          widths[size]
        )}
      >
        <div className="mb-4 flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <h2 id={titleId} className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-ink">
              {title}
            </h2>
            {description ? (
              <p id={descId} className="mt-0.5 text-sm text-muted">
                {description}
              </p>
            ) : null}
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Fermer"
            className="-mr-1 -mt-1 flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-muted hover:bg-soft"
          >
            <X size={18} aria-hidden />
          </button>
        </div>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>,
    document.body
  );
}

===== FILE: src/lib/countryCoords.ts =====

import { ALL_COUNTRIES } from "./countries";

const norm = (s: string) =>
  s
    .toLowerCase()
    .normalize("NFD")
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "");

// Compatibilité avec les anciennes saisies libres (aucune migration SQL nécessaire)
const ALIASES: Record<string, string> = {
  congo: "République du Congo",
  congobrazzaville: "République du Congo",
  rdc: "RD Congo",
  drc: "RD Congo",
  congokinshasa: "RD Congo",
  congodemocratique: "RD Congo",
  cotedivoire: "Côte d'Ivoire",
  ivorycoast: "Côte d'Ivoire",
  centrafricaine: "Centrafrique",
  guineeequatoriale: "Guinée équatoriale",
  afriquedusud: "Afrique du Sud",
  southafrica: "Afrique du Sud",
  swaziland: "Eswatini",
  ilesmaurice: "Maurice",
  reunion: "Réunion",
  lareunion: "Réunion",
  usa: "États-Unis",
  us: "États-Unis",
  etatsunis: "États-Unis",
  uk: "Royaume-Uni",
  angleterre: "Royaume-Uni",
};

export function getCoords(raw: string | null): [number, number] | null {
  if (!raw) return null;

  // 1. Correspondance exacte (valeurs issues du dropdown)
  const exact = ALL_COUNTRIES.find((c) => c.name === raw);
  if (exact) return [exact.lat, exact.lon];

  // 2. Alias legacy (anciennes saisies libres)
  const n = norm(raw);
  const alias = ALIASES[n];
  if (alias) {
    const c = ALL_COUNTRIES.find((x) => x.name === alias);
    if (c) return [c.lat, c.lon];
  }

  // 3. Correspondance normalisée (accents / espaces ignorés)
  const fuzzy = ALL_COUNTRIES.find((c) => norm(c.name) === n);
  return fuzzy ? [fuzzy.lat, fuzzy.lon] : null;
}
===== FILE: src/lib/cropImage.ts =====

export const createImage = (url: string): Promise<HTMLImageElement> =>
  new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const image = new Image();
    image.addEventListener("load", () => resolve(image));
    image.addEventListener("error", (error) => reject(error));
    image.setAttribute("crossOrigin", "anonymous");
    image.src = url;
  });

export async function getCroppedImg(
  imageSrc: string,
  pixelCrop: { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number }
): Promise<Blob> {
  const image = await createImage(imageSrc);
  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  if (!ctx) throw new Error("Impossible de créer le contexte 2D");

  canvas.width = pixelCrop.width;
  canvas.height = pixelCrop.height;

  ctx.drawImage(
    image,
    pixelCrop.x,
    pixelCrop.y,
    pixelCrop.width,
    pixelCrop.height,
    0,
    0,
    pixelCrop.width,
    pixelCrop.height
  );

  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    canvas.toBlob(
      (blob) => {
        if (!blob) reject(new Error("Canvas vide"));
        resolve(blob!);
      },
      "image/jpeg",
      0.95 // Qualité JPEG
    );
  });
}
===== FILE: src/lib/current-profile.ts =====

import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import type { SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
import { PROFILE_COOKIE, type DbProfile } from "./dashboard-types";

/**
 * Profil actuellement sélectionné dans le dashboard (cookie), avec repli sur le premier profil.
 * Remplace `.maybeSingle()` qui échouait dès qu'un utilisateur avait plusieurs profils.
 */
export async function getCurrentProfileRow(supabase: SupabaseClient, userId: string): Promise<DbProfile | null> {
  const { data } = await supabase.from("profiles").select("*").eq("user_id", userId);
  const rows = ((data ?? []) as DbProfile[]).slice().sort((a, b) => (a.created_at ?? "").localeCompare(b.created_at ?? ""));
  if (rows.length === 0) return null;
  const wanted = (await cookies()).get(PROFILE_COOKIE)?.value;
  return rows.find((r) => r.id === wanted) ?? rows[0];
}

===== FILE: src/lib/detect-icon.ts =====

/** Détection ordonnée : premier match gagnant. */
const PLATFORM_DOMAINS: [string, string][] = [
  ["instagram.com", "instagram"],
  ["tiktok.com", "tiktok"],
  ["youtube.com", "youtube"],
  ["youtu.be", "youtube"],
  ["facebook.com", "facebook"],
  ["fb.com", "facebook"],
  ["wa.me", "whatsapp"],
  ["whatsapp.com", "whatsapp"],
  ["twitter.com", "x"],
  ["x.com", "x"],
  ["linkedin.com", "linkedin"],
  ["t.me", "telegram"],
  ["telegram.me", "telegram"],
  ["pinterest.", "pinterest"],
  ["spotify.com", "spotify"],
  ["soundcloud.com", "soundcloud"],
  ["snapchat.com", "snapchat"],
  ["twitch.tv", "twitch"],
  ["discord.gg", "discord"],
  ["discord.com", "discord"],
  ["github.com", "github"],
  ["medium.com", "medium"],
  ["substack.com", "substack"],
  ["patreon.com", "patreon"],
  ["etsy.com", "etsy"],
  ["shopify.com", "shopify"],
  ["amazon.", "amazon"],
  ["paypal.me", "paypal"],
  ["paypal.com", "paypal"],
  ["calendly.com", "calendly"],
  ["maps.google.", "maps"],
  ["goo.gl/maps", "maps"],
  ["google.com/maps", "maps"],
];

/** Plateforme détectée dans l'URL (null si aucune). */
export function detectPlatform(url?: string | null): string | null {
  if (!url) return null;
  const u = url.toLowerCase();
  if (u.startsWith("mailto:")) return "email";
  if (u.startsWith("tel:")) return "phone";
  for (const [needle, key] of PLATFORM_DOMAINS) {
    if (u.includes(needle)) return key;
  }
  return null;
}

/** Nom stocké dans links.icon (contrainte SQL : ^[a-z]{2,20}$). */
export function detectIcon(url: string): string {
  const platform = detectPlatform(url);
  if (platform === "maps") return "pin";
  if (platform === "phone") return "phone";
  if (platform) return platform;

  const u = url.toLowerCase();
  if (u.includes("calendly") || u.includes("booking") || u.includes("rendez-vous")) return "calendar";
  if (u.includes("shop") || u.includes("boutique") || u.includes("store")) return "bag";
  if (u.includes("music") || u.includes("podcast") || u.includes("audio")) return "play";
  if (u.includes("blog") || u.includes("article") || u.includes("guide")) return "book";
  if (u.includes("portfolio") || u.includes("resume") || u.includes("cv")) return "briefcase";
  if (u.includes("donation") || u.includes("don") || u.includes("tip")) return "heart";
  if (u.includes("form") || u.includes("typeform") || u.includes("survey")) return "file";
  if (u.startsWith("http")) return "globe";
  return "link";
}
===== FILE: src/lib/entitlements.ts =====

import { planAtLeast, type Plan } from "./plans";
import { FONT_OPTIONS } from "./themes/fonts";
import { getTheme } from "./themes/registry";
import { CUSTOMIZABLE_KEYS, type CustomTokens } from "./themes/types";

/** Doit rester identique à la table SQL plan_entitlements. */
export const ENTITLEMENTS: Record<
  Plan,
  { maxLinks: number | null; customColors: boolean; removeBadge: boolean; customDomain: boolean }
> = {
  free: { maxLinks: 5, customColors: false, removeBadge: false, customDomain: false },
  pro: { maxLinks: null, customColors: true, removeBadge: true, customDomain: false },
  business: { maxLinks: null, customColors: true, removeBadge: true, customDomain: true },
};

export type Feature = "customColors" | "removeBadge" | "customDomain";

export const canUse = (plan: Plan, feature: Feature) => ENTITLEMENTS[plan][feature];
export const maxLinks = (plan: Plan) => ENTITLEMENTS[plan].maxLinks;

/** Le plan donne-t-il accès à ce thème ? (inconnu = non) */
export function canUseTheme(plan: Plan, themeId: string) {
  const t = getTheme(themeId);
  return !!t && planAtLeast(plan, t.plan);
}

export const requiredPlanForTheme = (themeId: string): Plan | null => getTheme(themeId)?.plan ?? null;

const COLOR = /^(#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}|rgba?(\s*[0-9.%\s,]+))$/;
const RADIUS = /^[0-9]{1,3}px$/;

/** Ne garde que des valeurs sûres (même règle que is_safe_token en SQL). */
export function sanitizeCustomTokens(input: unknown): CustomTokens {
  const out: CustomTokens = {};
  if (!input || typeof input !== "object") return out;
  for (const k of CUSTOMIZABLE_KEYS) {
    const v = (input as Record<string, unknown>)[k];
    if (typeof v !== "string") continue;
    const ok =
      k === "radius"
        ? RADIUS.test(v)
        : k === "fontDisplay" || k === "fontBody"
          ? (FONT_OPTIONS as readonly string[]).includes(v)
          : COLOR.test(v);
    if (ok) out[k] = v;
  }
  return out;
}
===== FILE: src/lib/image-transparency.ts =====

/**
 * Mesure si une image possède un fond transparent (PNG/WebP).
 * Renvoie null en cas de doute (CORS, format illisible) : jamais bloquant.
 */
export async function hasTransparency(url: string): Promise<boolean | null> {
  try {
    const img = new Image();
    img.crossOrigin = "anonymous";
    await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      img.onload = resolve;
      img.onerror = reject;
      img.src = url;
    });
    const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
    const w = (canvas.width = Math.min(128, img.naturalWidth || 128));
    const h = (canvas.height = Math.min(128, img.naturalHeight || 128));
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d", { willReadFrequently: true });
    if (!ctx) return null;
    ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, w, h);
    const { data } = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, w, h);
    let transparent = 0;
    for (let i = 3; i < data.length; i += 4) {
      if (data[i] < 250) transparent++;
    }
    return transparent / (w * h) > 0.05;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
===== FILE: src/lib/multi-profiles.ts =====

import type { Plan } from "./plans";

/** Limites de profils par plan (doit rester identique au SQL). */
export const PROFILE_LIMITS: Record<Plan, number> = {
  free: 1,
  pro: 3,
  business: 10,
};

export function canCreateProfile(plan: Plan, currentCount: number): boolean {
  return currentCount < PROFILE_LIMITS[plan];
}

export function profileLimitMessage(plan: Plan): string {
  const limit = PROFILE_LIMITS[plan];
  if (plan === "free") return `Plan Gratuit : 1 profil maximum. Passe au Pro pour en créer jusqu'à ${PROFILE_LIMITS.pro}.`;
  if (plan === "pro") return `Plan Pro : ${limit} profils maximum. Passe au Business pour en créer jusqu'à ${PROFILE_LIMITS.business}.`;
  return `Plan Business : ${limit} profils maximum.`;
}

/**
 * Télécharge l'avatar et le convertit en data URI pour que le rendu ne dépende
 * pas d'un téléchargement distant. PNG/JPEG uniquement (formats gérés par le moteur
 * d'image) ; renvoie null au moindre doute (le visuel retombe sur l'initiale).
 */
export async function avatarDataUri(url?: string): Promise<string | null> {
  if (!url) return null;
  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(2500) });
    if (!res.ok) return null;
    const type = (res.headers.get("content-type") ?? "").split(";")[0].trim();
    if (type !== "image/png" && type !== "image/jpeg") return null;
    const buf = Buffer.from(await res.arrayBuffer());
    if (buf.length === 0 || buf.length > 400_000) return null;
    return `data:${type};base64,${buf.toString("base64")}`;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

===== FILE: src/lib/plans.ts =====

export type Plan = "free" | "pro" | "business";

export const PLAN_RANK: Record<Plan, number> = { free: 0, pro: 1, business: 2 };

export function planAtLeast(current: Plan, required: Plan): boolean {
  return PLAN_RANK[current] >= PLAN_RANK[required];
}
===== FILE: src/lib/profile-mapper.ts =====

import { sanitizeCustomTokens } from "./entitlements";
import type { Plan } from "./plans";
import { safeImageUrl, safeUrl } from "./safe-url";
import type { Block, LinkItem, Profile, Social, SocialType } from "./themes/types";

const SOCIALS: SocialType[] = [
  "instagram", "tiktok", "youtube", "facebook", "whatsapp",
  "x", "linkedin", "telegram", "pinterest", "email",
];

const str = (v: unknown, max = 500) => (typeof v === "string" ? v.slice(0, max) : "");
const arr = (v: unknown): unknown[] => (Array.isArray(v) ? v : []);
const iconKey = (v: unknown) => (typeof v === "string" && /^[a-z]{2,20}$/.test(v) ? v : undefined);
const emoji = (v: unknown) => (typeof v === "string" ? v.slice(0, 16) : undefined);

function toBlocks(raw: unknown): Block[] {
  const out: Block[] = [];
  for (const b of arr(raw) as Record<string, unknown>[]) {
    if (!b || typeof b !== "object") continue;
    if (b.type === "about") {
      out.push({ type: "about", title: str(b.title, 80), text: str(b.text, 1200) });
    } else if (b.type === "newsletter") {
      out.push({ type: "newsletter", title: str(b.title, 80), subtitle: str(b.subtitle, 160) || undefined });
    } else if (b.type === "stats") {
      out.push({
        type: "stats",
        items: (arr(b.items) as Record<string, unknown>[]).slice(0, 4).map((i) => ({
          value: str(i?.value, 12),
          label: str(i?.label, 24),
        })),
      });
    } else if (b.type === "faq") {
      out.push({
        type: "faq",
        items: (arr(b.items) as Record<string, unknown>[]).slice(0, 10).map((i) => ({
          q: str(i?.q, 140),
          a: str(i?.a, 600),
        })),
      });
    } else if (b.type === "offer") {
      out.push({
        type: "offer",
        tag: str(b.tag, 24) || undefined,
        title: str(b.title, 80),
        desc: str(b.desc, 160) || undefined,
        icon: iconKey(b.icon),
        emoji: emoji(b.emoji),
        image: safeImageUrl(typeof b.image === "string" ? b.image : null),
        dateLabel: str(b.dateLabel, 12) || undefined,
        dateValue: str(b.dateValue, 12) || undefined,
        cta: str(b.cta, 24) || undefined,
        url: safeUrl(typeof b.url === "string" ? b.url : null),
      });
    } else if (b.type === "contact") {
      out.push({
        type: "contact",
        icon: iconKey(b.icon),
        emoji: emoji(b.emoji),
        title: str(b.title, 80),
        text: str(b.text, 160) || undefined,
        url: safeUrl(typeof b.url === "string" ? b.url : null),
      });
    } else if (b.type === "hint") {
      out.push({ type: "hint", emoji: emoji(b.emoji), label: str(b.label, 40) });
    } else if (b.type === "separator") {
      out.push({ type: "separator", label: str(b.label, 40) || undefined });
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/** Résultat de la fonction SQL get_public_profile → types du rendu. */
export function toProfile(payload: any): { profile: Profile; plan: Plan } {
  const p = payload.profile;
  const socials: Social[] = (arr(p.socials) as Record<string, unknown>[])
    .filter((s) => SOCIALS.includes(s?.type as SocialType) && safeUrl(str(s?.url, 2048)))
    .map((s) => ({ type: s.type as SocialType, url: str(s.url, 2048) }));
  const links: LinkItem[] = (arr(payload.links) as Record<string, any>[]).map((l) => ({
    id: l.id,
    title: str(l.title, 80),
    subtitle: str(l.subtitle, 160) || undefined,
    url: str(l.url, 2048),
    icon: l.icon ?? undefined,
    image: safeImageUrl(l.image_url),
    cta: str(l.cta, 24) || undefined,
    featured: !!l.featured,
    enabled: l.enabled !== false,
  }));
  return {
    plan: (["free", "pro", "business"].includes(payload.plan) ? payload.plan : "free") as Plan,
    profile: {
      slug: p.slug,
      displayName: str(p.display_name, 80),
      headline: str(p.headline, 120) || undefined,
      bio: str(p.bio, 400) || undefined,
      avatarUrl: safeImageUrl(p.avatar_url),
      coverUrl: safeImageUrl(p.cover_url),
      verified: !!p.verified,
      showBadge: p.show_badge !== false,
      themeId: p.theme_id,
      customTokens: sanitizeCustomTokens(p.custom_tokens),
      socials,
      links,
      blocks: toBlocks(p.blocks),
    },
  };
}

export const RESERVED_USERNAMES = [
  "admin", "dashboard", "auth", "api", "settings", "support", "help",
  "pricing", "blog", "contact", "demo", "www", "mail", "ftp", "root",
  "system", "ndeletik", "yekola", "staff", "team", "security", "app",
  "new", "edit", "profile", "profiles", "links", "stats", "analytics",
  "confidentialite", "conditions", "legal",
];

export function isReserved(username: string): boolean {

}
===== FILE: src/lib/safe-url.ts =====

export function safeUrl(u?: string | null): string | undefined {
  if (!u) return undefined;
  try {
    const p = new URL(u).protocol;
    return ["http:", "https:", "mailto:", "tel:"].includes(p) ? u : undefined;
  } catch { return undefined; }
}

export function safeImageUrl(u?: string | null): string | undefined {
  if (!u || !/^https:\/\/[^\s"'()]+$/.test(u)) return undefined;
  return u;
}

===== FILE: src/lib/supabase/server.ts =====

import { createServerClient } from "@supabase/ssr";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";

export const createClient = async () => {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();

  return createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        getAll() {
          return cookieStore.getAll();
        },
        setAll(cookiesToSet) {
          try {
            cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) =>
              cookieStore.set(name, value, options)
            );
          } catch {
            // Ignore
          }
        },
      },
    }
  );
};

===== FILE: src/lib/themes/fonts.ts =====

/** Liste FERMÉE des polices surchargeables (plan Pro+). Miroir exact de SQL is_safe_token. */
export const FONT_OPTIONS = [
  "Montserrat",
  "Poppins",
  "Playfair Display",
  "Inter",
  "Cormorant Garamond",
  "Roboto",
  "DM Sans",
  "Outfit",
  "Pacifico",
  "Open Sans",
] as const;

export type FontOption = (typeof FONT_OPTIONS)[number];

===== FILE: src/lib/themes/registry.ts =====

import { planAtLeast, type Plan } from "../plans";
import { BUSINESS_THEMES } from "./business";
import { FREE_THEMES } from "./free";
import { PRO_THEMES } from "./pro";
import type { CustomTokens, Theme } from "./types";

export const FALLBACK_THEME: Theme = {
  id: "fallback", name: "Classique", plan: "free", layout: "pills", buttonStyle: "solid", header: "center", avatarShape: "circle", dark: false,
  fonts: ["Montserrat:wght@600;700;800", "Poppins:wght@400;500;600"],
  tokens: { bg: "#FFF9F3", surface: "#FFFFFF", text: "#18181B", muted: "#71717A", accent: "#FF6A1A", accent2: "#C1440E", onAccent: "#FFFFFF", border: "#ECE4DA", radius: "999px", shadow: "0 6px 20px -8px rgba(193,68,14,.25)", headerBg: "#F9A825", fontDisplay: "Montserrat", fontBody: "Poppins" },
};

export const ALL_THEMES: Theme[] = [...FREE_THEMES, ...PRO_THEMES, ...BUSINESS_THEMES];
const BY_ID = new Map(ALL_THEMES.map((t) => [t.id, t]));
export const getTheme = (id: string) => BY_ID.get(id);

export function resolveTheme(themeId: string, plan: Plan): { theme: Theme; locked: boolean } {
  const wanted = BY_ID.get(themeId);
  if (wanted && planAtLeast(plan, wanted.plan)) return { theme: wanted, locked: false };
  return { theme: FREE_THEMES[0] ?? FALLBACK_THEME, locked: !!wanted };
}

export const resolveCustomTokens = (custom: CustomTokens | undefined, plan: Plan) =>
  planAtLeast(plan, "pro") ? custom : undefined;

===== FILE: src/lib/themes/tokens.ts =====

import type { CSSProperties } from "react";
import { FONT_QUERIES } from "./fonts";
import { CUSTOMIZABLE_KEYS, type CustomTokens, type Theme } from "./types";

/** Convertit un thème (+ surcharges Pro) en variables CSS --p-*. */
export function themeCssVars(theme: Theme, custom?: CustomTokens): CSSProperties {
  const t = { ...theme.tokens };
  if (custom) {
    for (const k of CUSTOMIZABLE_KEYS) {
      const v = custom[k];
      if (typeof v === "string" && v.trim()) t[k] = v;
    }
  }
  return {
    "--p-bg": t.bg,
    "--p-surface": t.surface,
    "--p-text": t.text,
    "--p-muted": t.muted,
    "--p-accent": t.accent,
    "--p-accent-2": t.accent2,
    "--p-on-accent": t.onAccent,
    "--p-border": t.border,
    "--p-radius": t.radius,
    "--p-shadow": t.shadow,
    "--p-header-bg": t.headerBg,
    "--p-font-display": `"${t.fontDisplay}"`,
    "--p-font-body": `"${t.fontBody}"`,
    "--p-tracking": theme.tracking ?? "0.02em",
  } as CSSProperties;
}

/**
 * URL Google Fonts du thème + polices surchargées (Pro).
 * Une famille déjà chargée par le thème n'est pas demandée deux fois.
 */
export function googleFontsHref(theme: Theme, custom?: CustomTokens): string | null {
  const list: string[] = [];
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const push = (q: string) => {
    const name = q.split(":")[0];
    if (!seen.has(name)) {
      seen.add(name);
      list.push(q);
    }
  };
  theme.fonts.forEach(push);
  if (custom?.fontDisplay) {
    const q = FONT_QUERIES[custom.fontDisplay];
    if (q) push(q);
  }
  if (custom?.fontBody) {
    const q = FONT_QUERIES[custom.fontBody];
    if (q) push(q);
  }
  if (!list.length) return null;
  return `https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?${list.map((f) => `family=${f}`).join("&")}&display=swap`;
}

import type { Plan } from "../plans";
export type { Plan };

/** Les 4 familles de mise en page des liens. */
export type LinkLayout = "pills" | "cards" | "grid" | "banners";
/** Style des boutons / cartes. */
export type ButtonStyle = "solid" | "outline" | "glass" | "soft" | "neu" | "gradient";
/** Variantes d'en-tête. */
export type HeaderVariant = "center" | "cover" | "arc";
export type AvatarShape = "circle" | "blob" | "rounded";

/** Valeurs CSS pures : tout le look d'un thème tient ici. */
export interface ThemeTokens {
  bg: string;
  surface: string;
  text: string;
  muted: string;
  accent: string;
  accent2: string;
  onAccent: string;
  border: string;
  radius: string;
  shadow: string;
  headerBg: string;
  fontDisplay: string;
  fontBody: string;
}

export interface Theme {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  plan: Plan;
  layout: LinkLayout;
  buttonStyle: ButtonStyle;
  header: HeaderVariant;
  avatarShape: AvatarShape;
  dark: boolean;
  uppercaseLinks?: boolean;
  uppercaseName?: boolean;
  tracking?: string;
  /** Polices Google, format API css2 : "Playfair+Display:wght@400;700". */
  fonts: string[];
  tokens: ThemeTokens;
}

/** Ce qu'un utilisateur Pro peut surcharger (liste blanche). */
export const CUSTOMIZABLE_KEYS = [
  "bg",
  "surface",
  "text",
  "accent",
  "accent2",
  "onAccent",
  "radius",
  "fontDisplay",
  "fontBody",
] as const;
export type CustomTokens = Partial<Pick<ThemeTokens, (typeof CUSTOMIZABLE_KEYS)[number]>>;

export type SocialType =
  | "instagram" | "tiktok" | "youtube" | "facebook" | "whatsapp"

  url: string;

}

  | { type: "hint"; emoji?: string; label: string }
  | { type: "separator"; label?: string };

}

===== FILE: src/middleware.ts =====

import { createServerClient } from "@supabase/ssr";
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from "next/server";

export async function middleware(request: NextRequest) {
  let response = NextResponse.next({ request: { headers: request.headers } });

  const supabase = createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        getAll() {
          return request.cookies.getAll();
        },
        setAll(cookiesToSet) {
          cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value }) => request.cookies.set(name, value));
          response = NextResponse.next({ request });
          cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) => response.cookies.set(name, value, options));
        },
      },
    }
  );

  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

  // Routes protégées : /dashboard, /dashboard/*, /onboarding
  const protectedPaths = ["/dashboard", "/onboarding"];
  const isProtected = protectedPaths.some((p) => request.nextUrl.pathname.startsWith(p));

  if (isProtected && !user) {
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL("/auth", request.url));
  }

  // Redirection onboarding : si l'utilisateur est connecté mais n'a pas complété l'onboarding
  if (user && request.nextUrl.pathname === "/dashboard") {
    const { data: profile } = await supabase
      .from("profiles")
      .select("onboarding_completed")
      .eq("user_id", user.id)
      .single();

    if (profile && !profile.onboarding_completed) {
      return NextResponse.redirect(new URL("/onboarding", request.url));
    }
  }

  // Si l'utilisateur a complété l'onboarding et essaie d'accéder à /onboarding, rediriger vers /dashboard
  if (user && request.nextUrl.pathname === "/onboarding") {
    const { data: profile } = await supabase
      .from("profiles")
      .select("onboarding_completed")
      .eq("user_id", user.id)
      .single();

    if (profile && profile.onboarding_completed) {
      return NextResponse.redirect(new URL("/dashboard", request.url));
    }
  }

  return response;
}

export const config = {
  matcher: ["/dashboard/:path*", "/onboarding"],
};
===== FILE: supabase/schema.sql =====
